📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 威斯特摩兰卫生官员 Marcia Graham 博士在一次市政会议上敦促居民采取预防乙型肝炎的措施,包括 疫苗接种 和安全性行为。 [news-0836a1daabc51b04]。
  • 一项多中心、前瞻性、单臂、开放标签试验,评估索磷布韦/维帕他韦 (SOF/VEL) 与富马酸替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 预 治疗 在慢性 HBV/HCV 合并感染患者中的安全性和有效性。该研究得到证实并发表在《临床和转化肝病学杂志》上。
  • 一项基于人群的 队列研究 研究报告称,根据 13.7 年的随访,慢性乙型肝炎 患者的代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝病 (MASLD) 与心血管疾病和肝外癌症风险增加相关。 [news-3a24b56e80d1ff5d]。
  • 一名 29 岁孕妇 (Night Sheritan) 在常规产前筛查中确诊为 诊断 乙型肝炎。诊断 出人意料,因为她没有任何症状,这凸显了乙型肝炎的“无声感染”性质。 [news-5345e67d66c73f50]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Hepatitis B virus news developments.
  • Medical Officer of Health for Westmoreland, Dr. Marcia Graham, urged residents to take precautionary measures against hepatitis B, including vaccination and safe sex practices, during a municipal meeting.
  • A multicenter, prospective, single-arm, open-label trial evaluating the safety and efficacy of sofosbuvir/velpatasvir (SOF/VEL) with pre-treatment of tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic HBV/HCV coinfected patients.
  • A population-based cohort study reported that metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease (MASLD) in people with chronic hepatitis B is associated with increased risks of cardiovascular disease and extrahepatic cancers, based on 13.7 years of follow-up.
  • Confirmed diagnosis of hepatitis B in a 29-year-old pregnant woman (Night Sheritan) during routine antenatal screening.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of medical virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/jmv.71070  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 98, 8, e71070
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

在病毒学受到抑制的 HIV 感染者和既往 HBV 感染者中改用不含替诺福韦的双重疗法后出现病毒学失败的风险。

Risk of Virological Failure After Switch to a Tenofovir-Sparing Dual Therapy in Virologically Suppressed People With HIV and Past HBV Infection.
作者： Borghetti A, Giacomelli A, P F Salvo, Cervo A, Colombaioni S, Lagi F, Baldin G, Mussini C, Oreni L, Antinori S
摘要中文翻译我们比较了基于替诺福韦 (TFV) 的三联疗法 (TT) 与保留 TFV 的双重疗法 (DT) 在病毒学抑制的 HBsAg-/HBcAb+ HIV 感染者 (PLWH) 中的有效性。我们模拟了一项目标试验,包括来自五所大学医院的具有 HBsAg-/HBcAb+ 血清状态的成人 PLWH,通过加权 Kaplan-Meier 估计器估计,以未抑制的血清 HIV-RNA 开始 TT,并达到 HIV-RNA 50 cp/mL 或单个 HIV-RNA ≥ 200 cp/mL。总体而言,415 名 PLWH 符合研究分析的条件:71 名(17.1%)在随访期间开始了 DT(基于 3TC:78.9%)。他们主要是男性(329 人,79.3%),中位年龄为 48 岁。在加权分析中,5 年时 VF 的估计概率为:DT 为 29.3%,TT 为 29.9%(差异:0.5%;95% CI:-28.7,+21.6)。在最长随访时间(4 年)内 HBsAb 检测不到的 PLWH 子集中观察到较高的 VF 风险,独立于 治疗 组:DT 和 TT 分别为 15.5% 和 36.8%(差异:-21.3%;95% CI:-30.3,-13.8)。与继续 TT 相比,改用保留 TFV 的 DT 与 PLWH 和既往 HBV 感染者发生 VF 的风险增加无关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较基于替诺福韦 (TFV) 的三联疗法 (TT) 与保留 TFV 的双重疗法 (DT) 对既往感染过 HBV 且病毒学受到抑制的 HIV 感染者 (PLWH) 的有效性,特别是那些 HBsAg-/HBcAb+ 的患者。
设计与对象
这是对一项目标试验的模拟,包括来自五家大学医院的 HIV 感染者,他们的 HBsAg-/HBcAb+ 且 HIV-RNA 病毒学受到抑制,415 名 PLWH 符合分析条件,其中 71 名 (17.1%) 在随访期间开始接受 DT。
核心方法
该研究使用加权 Kaplan-Meier 估计量来估计 5 年时病毒学失败 (VF) 的概率,其定义为连续两次 HIV-RNA > 50 cp/mL 或单个 HIV-RNA ≥ 200 cp/mL。
主要结果
在加权分析中,5 年时 DT 的 VF 估计概率为 29.3%,TT 为 29.9%(差异:0.5%;95% CI:-28.7,+21.6)。在最长随访时间(4 年)内未检出 HBsAb 的子集中,出现 VF 的风险较高:DT 和 TT 的风险分别为 15.5% 和 36.8%(差异:-21.3%;95% CI:-30.3,-13.8)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,与继续 TT 相比,转为保留 TFV 的 DT 与既往感染 HBV 的 PLWH 的 VF 风险增加无关,这为该人群中的 治疗 选择提供了新的证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为临床实践提供了信息,表明保留 TFV 的双重疗法可能是病毒学抑制的既往 HBV 感染的 PLWH 的可行选择,有可能减少长期毒性而不增加 VF 风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究是对目标试验的模拟,可能会引入无法测量的混杂因素,但大样本量和稳健的统计分析为结果提供了适度的可信度。

Risk of Virological Failure After Switch to a Tenofovir-Sparing Dual Therapy in Virologically Suppressed People With HIV and Past HBV Infection.

Authors: Borghetti A, Giacomelli A, P F Salvo, Cervo A, Colombaioni S, Lagi F, Baldin G, Mussini C, Oreni L, Antinori S
Original abstract (English)
We compared the effectiveness of tenofovir (TFV)-based triple regimens (TT) to TFV-sparing dual therapies (DT) in virologically suppressed, HBsAg-/HBcAb+ people living with HIV (PLWH). We emulated a target trial including adults PLWH from five University Hospitals, with HBsAg-/HBcAb+ serostatus, starting a TT with unsuppressed serum HIV-RNA and reaching HIV-RNA 50 cp/mL or a single HIV-RNA ≥ 200 cp/mL) were estimated through a weighted Kaplan-Meier estimator. Overall, 415 PLWH were eligible for study analysis: 71 (17.1%) started a DT (3TC-based: 78.9%) during follow-up. They were mainly men (329, 79.3%), with a median age of 48 years. In the weighted analysis, the estimated probabilities of VF were: 29.3% for DT and 29.9% for TT at 5 years (difference: 0.5%; 95% CI: -28.7, +21.6). A higher VF risk was seen in the subset of PLWH with undetectable HBsAb at the longest follow-up available (4 years), independently of treatment group: 15.5% and 36.8% with DT and TT, respectively (difference: -21.3%; 95% CI: -30.3, -13.8). Switch to a TFV-sparing DT, compared with continuing a TT, was not associated with an increased risk of VF in PLWH and prior HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the effectiveness of tenofovir (TFV)-based triple regimens (TT) to TFV-sparing dual therapies (DT) in virologically suppressed people living with HIV (PLWH) with past HBV infection, specifically those who were HBsAg-/HBcAb+.
Design and population
This was an emulation of a target trial including adults with HIV from five University Hospitals, who were HBsAg-/HBcAb+ and had virologically suppressed HIV-RNA, with 415 PLWH eligible for analysis, of whom 71 (17.1%) started a DT during follow-up.
Core methods
The study used a weighted Kaplan-Meier estimator to estimate the probabilities of virological failure (VF), defined as two consecutive HIV-RNA > 50 cp/mL or a single HIV-RNA ≥ 200 cp/mL, at 5 years.
Main results
In the weighted analysis, the estimated probabilities of VF were 29.3% for DT and 29.9% for TT at 5 years (difference: 0.5%; 95% CI: -28.7, +21.6). A higher VF risk was seen in the subset with undetectable HBsAb at the longest follow-up (4 years): 15.5% and 36.8% with DT and TT, respectively (difference: -21.3%; 95% CI: -30.3, -13.8).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that switching to a TFV-sparing DT, compared with continuing a TT, was not associated with an increased risk of VF in PLWH with prior HBV infection, providing new evidence for treatment options in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform clinical practice by suggesting that TFV-sparing dual therapy may be a viable option for virologically suppressed PLWH with past HBV infection, potentially reducing long-term toxicity without increasing VF risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was an emulation of a target trial, which may introduce unmeasured confounding, but the large sample size and robust statistical analysis provide moderate confidence in the results.

Journal: Infectious Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-03  |  DOI: 10.1080/23744235.2026.2701171  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 1-11

乙型肝炎病毒 基因型 E 感染的临床和病毒学特征:病例系列

Clinical and virological characteristics of hepatitis B virus genotype E infection: a case series
作者： Giorgio Tiecco, Maria Antonia De Francesco, Laert Zeneli, Federica Gottardi, Serena Zaltron, Angiola Spinetti, Francesco Castelli, Eugenia Quiros-Roldan, Eugenia Quirós-Roldán
摘要中文翻译简介:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 基因型 E 主要发现于撒哈拉以南非洲,表现出较低的遗传多样性、独特的分子标记和较高的重组潜力,但仍未得到充分研究。目的:本研究描述了意大利北部三级护理中心确诊的 E 型 HBV 感染患者的临床和病毒学特征、突变谱、抗病毒药物 治疗 反应和系统发育数据。方法:一项回顾性单中心研究于 2015 年至 2023 年在意大利布雷西亚 ASST Spedali Civili di Brescia 进行。研究纳入了记录有 E 型 HBV 且因临床原因接受基因型耐药测试 (GRT) 的患者。S/POL 区域的直接测序用于基因分型和 突变 分析,通过 Geno2pheno [hbv] 2.0 和斯坦福大学 HBV 耐药工具进行解释。结果:确定了 8 名患者,其中大多数为男性(62.5%),非洲裔(87.5%),中位年龄为 38.5 岁。尽管存在 抗病毒药物 治疗,但仍有 6 名 (75%) 患者因持续病毒血症而进行了 GRT,其中 5 名 (83.3%) 患者为 HIV/HBV 合并感染且可检测到 HIV 病毒载量。1 例(12.5%)发现拉米夫定耐药 突变(L180M、M204V),2 例(25%)发现替诺福韦耐药相关 突变(M267L/I),3 例(37.5%)发现免疫逃逸 突变,包括 T126I、D144E 和 G145R。尽管进行了 GRT,但由于依从性较差,四名 (50%) 患者的 治疗 并未得到修改。治疗调整导致三例(37.5%)病例病毒得到抑制。结论:在我们的环境中,E 型乙型肝炎病毒感染主要发生在来自流行地区的流动人口中。尽管核苷(酸)类似物在多种基因型中均有效,但不理想的依从性可能会阻碍病毒抑制并促进耐药性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 基因型 E 主要发现于撒哈拉以南非洲,表现出较低的遗传多样性、独特的分子标记和较高的重组潜力,但仍未得到充分研究。本研究旨在描述确诊的 E 型 HBV 感染患者的临床和病毒学特征、突变谱、抗病毒药物 治疗 反应和系统发育数据。
设计与对象
一项回顾性单中心研究于 2015 年至 2023 年在意大利布雷西亚 ASST Spedali Civili di Brescia 进行。研究纳入了记录有 E 型 HBV 且因临床原因接受基因型耐药测试 (GRT) 的患者。
核心方法
S/POL 区域的直接测序用于基因分型和 突变 分析,通过 Geno2pheno [hbv] 2.0 和斯坦福大学 HBV 耐药工具进行解释。
主要结果
确定了 8 名患者,其中大多数为男性(62.5%),非洲裔(87.5%),中位年龄为 38.5 岁。1 例(12.5%)发现拉米夫定耐药 突变(L180M、M204V),2 例(25%)发现替诺福韦耐药相关 突变(M267L/I),3 例(37.5%)发现免疫逃逸 突变,包括 T126I、D144E 和 G145R。治疗调整导致三例(37.5%)病例病毒得到抑制。
解释与创新
在这种情况下,E 型乙型肝炎病毒感染主要发生在来自流行地区的移民中。尽管核苷(酸)类似物在多种基因型中均有效,但不理想的依从性可能会阻碍病毒抑制并促进耐药性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,来自 HBV 基因型 E 流行地区的流动人口需要有针对性的 监测 和依从性支持,以防止 治疗 耐药并改善病毒抑制结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和单中心设计的限制,降低了普适性。然而,直接测序和抗性分析为观察到的突变谱和 治疗 反应提供了适度的证据。

Clinical and virological characteristics of hepatitis B virus genotype E infection: a case series

Authors: Giorgio Tiecco, Maria Antonia De Francesco, Laert Zeneli, Federica Gottardi, Serena Zaltron, Angiola Spinetti, Francesco Castelli, Eugenia Quiros-Roldan, Eugenia Quirós-Roldán
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) genotype E, mainly found in sub-Saharan Africa, exhibits low genetic diversity, unique molecular markers and high recombination potential but remains under-studied. AIM: This study describes the clinical and virological characteristics, mutational profiles, antiviral treatment responses and phylogenetic data of patients with confirmed HBV genotype E infection in a tertiary-care centre in northern Italy. METHODS: A retrospective monocentric study was conducted at ASST Spedali Civili di Brescia, Italy, from 2015 to 2023. Patients with documented HBV genotype E who underwent genotypic resistance testing (GRT) for clinical reasons were included. Direct sequencing of the S/POL region was used for genotyping and mutation analysis, interpreted via Geno2pheno [hbv] 2.0 and Stanford HBV resistance tools. RESULTS: Eight patients were identified, mostly male (62.5%) and of African origin (87.5%), with a median age of 38.5 years. GRT was performed in six (75%) patients because of persistent viremia despite antiviral treatment, five (83.3%) of whom were HIV/HBV coinfected with detectable HIV viral load. Lamivudine resistance mutations (L180M, M204V) were found in one (12.5%) case, tenofovir resistance-associated mutations (M267L/I) in two (25%) and immune escape mutations in three (37.5%), including T126I, D144E and G145R. Despite GRT, treatment was not modified in four (50%) patients due to poor adherence concerns. Therapy adjustments led to viral suppression in three (37.5%) cases. CONCLUSION: In our setting, HBV genotype E infections largely occurred among migrants from endemic regions. Although nucleos(t)ide analogues are effective across genotypes, suboptimal adherence may hinder viral suppression and promote resistance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus genotype E, mainly found in sub-Saharan Africa, exhibits low genetic diversity, unique molecular markers and high recombination potential but remains under-studied. This study aimed to describe the clinical and virological characteristics, mutational profiles, antiviral treatment responses and phylogenetic data of patients with confirmed HBV genotype E infection.
Design and population
A retrospective monocentric study was conducted at ASST Spedali Civili di Brescia, Italy, from 2015 to 2023. Patients with documented HBV genotype E who underwent genotypic resistance testing (GRT) for clinical reasons were included.
Core methods
Direct sequencing of the S/POL region was used for genotyping and mutation analysis, interpreted via Geno2pheno [hbv] 2.0 and Stanford HBV resistance tools.
Main results
Eight patients were identified, mostly male (62.5%) and of African origin (87.5%), with a median age of 38.5 years. Lamivudine resistance mutations (L180M, M204V) were found in one (12.5%) case, tenofovir resistance-associated mutations (M267L/I) in two (25%) and immune escape mutations in three (37.5%), including T126I, D144E and G145R. Therapy adjustments led to viral suppression in three (37.5%) cases.
Interpretation and novelty
In this setting, HBV genotype E infections largely occurred among migrants from endemic regions. Although nucleos(t)ide analogues are effective across genotypes, suboptimal adherence may hinder viral suppression and promote resistance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted surveillance and adherence support in migrant populations from HBV genotype E-endemic regions to prevent treatment resistance and improve viral suppression outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and monocentric design, reducing generalisability. However, the direct sequencing and resistance analysis provide moderate evidence for the observed mutational profiles and treatment responses.

在赞比亚处理 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的一线医护人员中验证肝炎护理点超声检查

Validation of a Point‐Of‐Care Ultrasound for Hepatitis Among Front‐Line Healthcare Workers Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Zambia
作者： Costanza Bertoni, Annie Kanunga, Edford Sinkala, Enock Syabbalo, Helen Chitundu, Alick Chikwezi, Carolyn Chibundi, Guy K. Muula, Samuel Bosomprah, Nicolò Capra
摘要中文翻译摘要:为解决在资源有限地区实施乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染者护理的差距,我们评估了一种新型肝脏即时超声(POCUS)协议(PUSH)在赞比亚前线非放射科医护人员中的准确性。2024年3月至6月,在赞比亚卢萨卡大学教学医院,我们培训了四名和六名有HBV管理经验但无超声(US)经验的护士和医师,学习PUSH协议,该协议包括通过三个窗口(上腹部、肋下和右肋间)观察肝脏,以识别肝硬化征象和可疑肝细胞癌(HCC)的肝脏病变。随后,连续的成年参与者仅感染HBV或HBV/HIV合并感染,由操作者在不知情临床数据的情况下进行PUSH检查。我们使用瞬时弹性成像(TE)作为参考标准试验(RST)评估PUSH对显著肝纤维化和肝硬化的准确性,并使用经验丰富的放射科医生进行的全面腹部超声作为RST评估可疑HCC的肝脏病变。对PUSH的受试者工作特征曲线(ROC)进行非参数分析,并根据操作者类型(医生vs护士)、性别和HIV状态进行调整。在分析的197名HBV感染者中,69.5%正在接受HBV抗病毒治疗,27.9%有HBV/HIV合并感染。根据RST结果,17.7%的HBV感染者有显著肝纤维化,11.6%有肝硬化,3.5%有肝脏病变。PUSH对显著肝纤维化的准确性较低,敏感性为8.3%(17.5-41.4),特异性为100.0%(96.1-100),曲线下面积(AUROC)为0.54(0.43-0.65);对肝硬化的准确性中等,敏感性为60.9%(38.5-80.3),特异性为97.7%(94.2-99.4),AUROC为0.79(0.69-0.89);对肝脏病变的准确性较高,敏感性为85.7%(42.1-99.6),特异性为98.4%(95.5-99.7),AUROC为0.92(0.78-1.00)。经过快速培训后,赞比亚前线治疗HBV的护士和医师能够使用PUSH以中等准确性诊断肝硬化,以高准确性诊断肝脏病变,尽管性能估计的置信区间较宽。在实验室系统和TE缺乏的情况下,包括PUSH在内的肝脏POCUS可能对HBV管理有用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于诊断能力的差距,慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染在资源有限的环境中仍然管理不善,这促使需要可用的护理点工具。该研究旨在评估新型肝脏即时超声 (POCUS) 方案 (PUSH) 是否能够准确识别由一线医护人员管理的慢性乙型肝炎成人患者中的显着纤维化、肝硬化和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 病变。
设计与对象
2024 年 3 月至 6 月,在赞比亚卢萨卡大学教学医院,四名护士和六名医生接受了 PUSH 方案的培训,这些护士具有 HBV 管理经验,但之前没有接受过超声培训。连续患有慢性 HBV 的成年人,包括 HBV/HIV 双重感染的患者,在不了解临床数据的操作员的情况下接受了 PUSH。
核心方法
PUSH 涉及在三个窗口(上腹、肋下和右经肋)观察肝脏,以识别肝硬化提示特征和 HCC 病变。以瞬时弹性成像 (TE) 作为参考标准评估显着纤维化和肝硬化的准确性,同时由经验丰富的放射技师将肝脏病变与综合腹部超声进行比较。使用根据操作员类型、性别和 HIV 状况进行调整的非参数 ROC 分析。
主要结果
根据参考标准,197 名参与者中,17.7% 患有明显纤维化,11.6% 患有肝硬化,3.5% 患有肝脏病变。PUSH 对显着纤维化的准确性较低(敏感性 8.3%,特异性 100.0%,AUROC 0.54),对肝硬化的准确性中等(敏感性 60.9%,特异性 97.7%,AUROC 0.79),对肝脏病变的准确性较高(敏感性 85.7%,特异性 98.4%,AUROC 0.92)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,经过快速培训后,一线护士和医生可以使用 PUSH 以中等准确度诊断肝硬化,并以较高准确度诊断肝脏病变,尽管置信区间较宽。他们强调了肝脏 POCUS(包括 PUSH)在实验室系统和 TE 不可用的 HBV 管理中的潜在效用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,PUSH 可以通过使一线提供者能够筛查肝硬化和 HCC 病变来支持资源匮乏环境中的分散式 HBV 护理,从而有可能改善早期检测以及在无法获得高级成像时与 治疗 的联系。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量小和置信区间宽限制了精度,并且训练时间加快,这可能无法反映现实世界中技能的保留情况。由于这些限制和单中心设计,证据是中等的。

Validation of a Point‐Of‐Care Ultrasound for Hepatitis Among Front‐Line Healthcare Workers Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Zambia

Authors: Costanza Bertoni, Annie Kanunga, Edford Sinkala, Enock Syabbalo, Helen Chitundu, Alick Chikwezi, Carolyn Chibundi, Guy K. Muula, Samuel Bosomprah, Nicolò Capra
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT To close gaps in implementing care of people with hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in resource‐limited settings, we evaluated the accuracy of a novel liver point‐of‐care ultrasound (POCUS) protocol for hepatitis (PUSH) among front‐line non‐radiology healthcare workers in Zambia. At University Teaching Hospital in Lusaka, Zambia, from March to June 2024, we trained four nurses and six physicians with experience in HBV management, but not in ultrasound (US), in the PUSH protocol, which includes visualizing the liver in three windows (epigastric, subcostal and right transcostal) to identify cirrhosis‐suggestive features and liver lesions suspicious for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Then, consecutive adult participants with chronic hepatitis B (PWHB) alone or HBV/HIV coinfection underwent PUSH with operators blinded to clinical data. We evaluated the accuracy of PUSH for significant fibrosis and cirrhosis using transient elastography (TE) as the reference standard test (RST) and for liver lesions that were possible HCC using a comprehensive abdominal ultrasound by an experienced radiographer as the RST. Nonparametric analysis of the receiver operating curve (ROC) for PUSH was adjusted for operator type (doctor vs. nurse), sex and HIV status. Among 197 PWHBs analysed, 69.5% were taking HBV antivirals and 27.9% had HBV/HIV. According to RSTs, 17.7% of PWHBs had significant fibrosis, 11.6% had cirrhosis and 3.5% had liver lesions. PUSH had low accuracy for significant fibrosis, with sensitivity of 8.3% (17.5–41.4), specificity of 100.0% (96.1–100) and an area under the curve (AUROC) of 0.54 (0.43–0.65), moderate accuracy for cirrhosis, with sensitivity of 60.9% (38.5–80.3), specificity of 97.7% (94.2–99.4) and AUROC of 0.79 (0.69–0.89), and higher accuracy for liver lesions, with 85.7% (42.1–99.6) sensitivity, 98.4% (95.5–99.7) specificity, AUROC of 0.92 (0.78–1.00). After an expedited training, front‐line nurses and physicians treating HBV in Zambia were able to use PUSH to diagnose cirrhosis with moderate accuracy and liver lesions with high accuracy, although confidence intervals around performance estimates were wide. Liver POCUS including PUSH may be useful in HBV management when laboratory systems and TE are lacking.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains under-managed in resource-limited settings due to gaps in diagnostic capacity, prompting the need for accessible point-of-care tools. The study aimed to evaluate whether a novel liver point-of-care ultrasound (POCUS) protocol (PUSH) could accurately identify significant fibrosis, cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) lesions in adults with chronic HBV managed by front-line healthcare workers.
Design and population
At University Teaching Hospital in Lusaka, Zambia, from March to June 2024, four nurses and six physicians with HBV management experience but no prior ultrasound training were trained in the PUSH protocol. Consecutive adults with chronic HBV, including those with HBV/HIV coinfection, underwent PUSH with operators blinded to clinical data.
Core methods
PUSH involved visualizing the liver in three windows (epigastric, subcostal, and right transcostal) to identify cirrhosis-suggestive features and HCC lesions. Accuracy for significant fibrosis and cirrhosis was assessed against transient elastography (TE) as the reference standard, while liver lesions were compared to comprehensive abdominal ultrasound by an experienced radiographer. Nonparametric ROC analysis adjusted for operator type, sex, and HIV status was used.
Main results
Among 197 participants, 17.7% had significant fibrosis, 11.6% had cirrhosis, and 3.5% had liver lesions by reference standards. PUSH showed low accuracy for significant fibrosis (sensitivity 8.3%, specificity 100.0%, AUROC 0.54), moderate accuracy for cirrhosis (sensitivity 60.9%, specificity 97.7%, AUROC 0.79), and high accuracy for liver lesions (sensitivity 85.7%, specificity 98.4%, AUROC 0.92).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that after expedited training, front-line nurses and physicians could use PUSH to diagnose cirrhosis with moderate accuracy and liver lesions with high accuracy, despite wide confidence intervals. They highlighted the potential utility of liver POCUS, including PUSH, in HBV management where laboratory systems and TE are unavailable.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest PUSH could support decentralized HBV care in low-resource settings by enabling front-line providers to screen for cirrhosis and HCC lesions, potentially improving early detection and linkage to treatment when advanced imaging is inaccessible.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s small sample size and wide confidence intervals limit precision, and the training duration was expedited, which may not reflect real-world retention of skills. The evidence is moderate due to these constraints and the single-centre design.

乙型肝炎病毒 基因型和人口统计学在信德省卡拉奇总人口中的分布

Distribution of hepatitis B virus genotypes and demography in the general population of Karachi, Sindh
作者： Saba Farooq, Hana’a Iqbal, Sirmast Faiz, Farwa Ahsan, Atia-tul-Wahab, M. Iqbal Choudhary
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎是一个重大的全球负担,有253万感染者。至少有十种乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)基因型和35种亚基因型,它们具有不同的种族-地理分布,并在生物学特性上表现出明显差异,与临床结局、疾病严重程度以及对抗病毒药物治疗的反应密切相关。本研究旨在确定信德省卡拉奇人群中主要流行的HBV基因型。在进行人口评估的同时,对HBsAg、HBsAb、HBeAb和HBcAb等标志物进行了血清学评估,以确定研究队列研究的临床感染谱。首先通过特异性引物P1和S1-2对PreS1/S2基因进行第一轮PCR进行初步分子鉴定。使用针对HBV pre-S基因和S基因保守序列的引物对通过巢式PCR确定HBV基因型(A至F),并通过系统发育分析进行了验证。研究结果显示,研究参与者的平均年龄为33.94岁,中位年龄为31.5岁。HBV阳性女性和男性的比例分别为51.5%和48.5%。这项初步研究的结果表明,卡拉奇地区可能流行的主导基因型是D型,其次是B型和C型。同时也观察到B、C和D型的混合感染。几乎所有D型阳性样本均为HBsAg阳性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Hepatitis B is a major global burden, with 253 million infected people, and there are at least ten HBV genotypes and 35 subgenotypes with distinct ethno-geographical distributions and clinical outcomes. The study aimed to identify the predominant circulating genotypes of HBV in the population of Karachi, Sindh.
设计与对象
人口统计评估与标记物(特别是 HBsAg、HBsAb、HBeAb 和 HBcAb)的血清学评估一起进行,以确定 队列研究 研究的临床感染概况。
核心方法
通过 PreS1/S2 基因的特异性引物 P1 和 S1-2 进行第一轮 PCR 进行初步分子鉴定,并使用 HBV pre-S 基因和 S 基因保守序列的引物对通过巢式 PCR 确定 HBV 基因型(A 至 F),随后通过系统发育分析进行验证。
主要结果
研究结果显示,研究参与者的平均年龄为33.94岁,中位年龄为31.5岁,女性和男性乙肝病毒阳性比例分别为51.5%和48.5%。结果表明,卡拉奇流行的可能优势基因型是D,其次是B和C,还观察到B、C和D的混合感染。
解释与创新
作者解释说,D 基因型在卡拉奇占主导地位的鉴定提供了对当地 HBV 流行病学 及其与临床结果的关联的深入了解,因为几乎所有 D 阳性样本均为 HBsAg 阳性。
科研与公卫意义
考虑到主要基因型 D 及其临床意义,这些发现可以为卡拉奇的针对性 监测 和 疫苗接种 策略提供信息,并可能指导未来针对基因型特异性 治疗 方法的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项试点,样本量有限,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性,但分子和系统发育方法的使用为基因型分布结果提供了适度的可信度。

Distribution of hepatitis B virus genotypes and demography in the general population of Karachi, Sindh

Authors: Saba Farooq, Hana’a Iqbal, Sirmast Faiz, Farwa Ahsan, Atia-tul-Wahab, M. Iqbal Choudhary
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B is a major global burden, with 253 million infected people. There are at least ten Hepatitis B virus (HBV) genotypes and 35 subgenotypes, with distinct ethno-geographical distributions and manifest differences in their biological characteristics, display close association with clinical outcomes, severity of disease, and response to antiviral therapy. The aim of the study was to identify the predominant circulating genotypes of HBV in the population of Karachi, Sindh. A demographic assessment was conducted alongside a serological evaluation of markers specifically HBsAg, HBsAb, HBeAb, and HBcAb to determine the clinical infection profile of the study cohort. An initial molecular identification was performed by first round of PCR via specific primers P1 and S1-2 for PreS1/S2 gene. The HBV genotypes (A to F) was determined by nested PCR using primer pairs for the conserved sequence of HBV pre-S gene and S gene, and subsequently validated by phylogenetic analysis. The study findings indicated that the mean age of the study participants was 33.94 years, with a median age of 31.5 years. The percentage of HBV positive female and male were 51.5% and 48.5%, respectively. The results of this pilot study indicated that the probable predominant genotype circulating in Karachi is D, followed by B and C. Mixed infection of B, C, and D was also observed. Almost all the D positive samples were positive for HBsAg.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B is a major global burden, with 253 million infected people, and there are at least ten HBV genotypes and 35 subgenotypes with distinct ethno-geographical distributions and clinical outcomes. The study aimed to identify the predominant circulating genotypes of HBV in the population of Karachi, Sindh.
Design and population
A demographic assessment was conducted alongside a serological evaluation of markers specifically HBsAg, HBsAb, HBeAb, and HBcAb to determine the clinical infection profile of the study cohort.
Core methods
An initial molecular identification was performed by first round of PCR via specific primers P1 and S1-2 for PreS1/S2 gene, and HBV genotypes (A to F) were determined by nested PCR using primer pairs for the conserved sequence of HBV pre-S gene and S gene, subsequently validated by phylogenetic analysis.
Main results
The study findings indicated that the mean age of the study participants was 33.94 years, with a median age of 31.5 years, and the percentage of HBV positive female and male were 51.5% and 48.5%, respectively. The results indicated that the probable predominant genotype circulating in Karachi is D, followed by B and C, with mixed infection of B, C, and D also observed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the identification of genotype D as predominant in Karachi provides insights into the local HBV epidemiology and its association with clinical outcomes, as almost all D positive samples were positive for HBsAg.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and vaccination strategies in Karachi, considering the predominant genotype D and its clinical implications, and may guide future studies on genotype-specific treatment approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a pilot with a limited sample size, which may affect the generalizability of the findings, but the use of molecular and phylogenetic methods provides moderate confidence in the genotype distribution results.

通过物理信息深度学习方法探索 乙型肝炎病毒 感染动力学

Exploration of Hepatitis B Virus Infection Dynamics through Physics-Informed Deep Learning Approach
作者： Bikram Das, Rupchand Sutradhar, D. C. Dalal
摘要中文翻译准确预测病毒性疾病 暴发 对于指导公共卫生应对措施和防止广泛的生命损失至关重要。最近,物理信息神经网络(PINN)已成为捕获病毒感染动态并预测其未来进展的有前途的框架。标准 PINN 可通过正向建模方法有效模拟疾病动态;然而,他们经常面临从逆问题中的稀疏或噪声数据估计关键生物参数的挑战。为了克服这些限制,最近的一个扩展,称为疾病信息神经网络(DINN),作为参数估计的强大框架而受到关注。在这项工作中,我们将这种 DINN 技术应用于最近提出的 传播 (HBV) 感染动力学模型,以预测肝脏内感染的 传播。该模型由四个部分组成:未感染和感染的肝细胞、含有松弛环状 DNA (rcDNA) 的衣壳和游离病毒。在估计参数之前,进行结构可识别性分析以确定该模型的可识别参数。然后,我们研究 (i) 参数范围的变化、(ii) 实验噪声、(iii) 样本大小、(iv) 网络架构和 (v) 学习率对 DINN 性能的影响。此外,使用九只感染 HBV 的黑猩猩的实验数据,我们观察到 DINN 可以估计未知参数、捕获感染动态并预测疾病进展,即使某些模型区室的实验数据不可用。此外,DINN 还可以识别不同黑猩猩之间存在显着差异的模型参数。进一步进行了全局敏感性分析,结果表明显着参数与 DINN 检测到的参数一致。最后,与非线性最小二乘拟合和最大似然估计的比较证明了 DINN 在参数估计方面的优势。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
准确预测病毒性疾病 暴发 对于指导公共卫生应对措施和防止广泛的生命损失至关重要。物理信息神经网络(PINN)已成为捕获病毒感染动态并预测其未来进展的有前途的框架。
设计与对象
本研究将疾病知情神经网络 (DINN) 应用于 传播 (HBV) 感染动力学模型,以预测肝脏内的 传播。该模型由四个部分组成:未感染和感染的肝细胞、含有松弛环状 DNA (rcDNA) 的衣壳和游离病毒。该分析使用了九只感染 HBV 的黑猩猩的实验数据。
核心方法
在估计参数之前,进行结构可识别性分析以确定可识别参数。该研究考察了参数变化、实验噪声、样本大小、网络架构和学习率对 DINN 性能的影响。进行全局敏感性分析,并将结果与​​非线性最小二乘拟合和最大似然估计进行比较。
主要结果
即使某些模型区室的实验数据不可用,DINN 也可以估计未知参数、捕获感染动态并预测疾病进展。该方法识别出黑猩猩之间差异显着的参数,并且 DINN 检测到的重要参数与全局敏感性分析中的参数一致。
解释与创新
作者将 DINN 解释为病毒感染动力学参数估计的稳健框架,克服了标准 PINN 在逆问题中的局限性。新颖之处在于将 DINN 应用于 HBV 感染动态,并通过稀疏或嘈杂的数据证明其有效性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过改进参数估计和疾病进展预测来为 HBV 监测 和 治疗 策略提供信息。该方法可能适用于其他具有隔室模型的病毒感染,从而增强公共卫生准备。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度中等,因为它通过黑猩猩数据证明了 DINN 在受控环境中的有效性,但缺乏在人群中的验证。局限性包括潜在的普遍性问题和对特定 HBV 模型结构的依赖。

Exploration of Hepatitis B Virus Infection Dynamics through Physics-Informed Deep Learning Approach

Authors: Bikram Das, Rupchand Sutradhar, D. C. Dalal
Original abstract (English)
Accurate forecasting of viral disease outbreaks is crucial for guiding public health responses and preventing widespread loss of life. Recently, Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising framework for capturing viral infection dynamics and predicting their future progression. Standard PINNs are effective in simulating disease dynamics through forward modeling approach; however, they often face challenges in estimating key biological parameters from sparse or noisy data in inverse problems. To overcome these limitations, a recent extension, known as Disease-Informed Neural Networks (DINNs), has gained attention as a robust framework for parameter estimation. In this work, we apply this DINN technique to a recently proposed hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection dynamics model to predict the transmission of infection within the liver. The model consists of four compartments: uninfected and infected hepatocytes, relaxed circular DNA (rcDNA)-containing capsids, and free viruses. Before estimating the parameters, a structural identifiability analysis is performed to determine the identifiable parameters of this model. Then, we study the impacts of (i) variations in parameter ranges, (ii) experimental noise, (iii) sample size, (iv) network architecture, and (v) learning rate on the performance of DINNs. Furthermore, using experimental data from nine HBV-infected chimpanzees, we observe that DINNs can estimate unknown parameters, capture infection dynamics, and predict disease progression even when experimental data for some model compartments are not available. Additionally, DINNs identify the model parameters that vary significantly across chimpanzees. A global sensitivity analysis is further performed, and the results indicate that significant parameters are consistent with those detected by DINNs. Finally, comparison with nonlinear least-squares fitting and maximum likelihood estimation demonstrates the advantages of DINNs in parameter estimation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Accurate forecasting of viral disease outbreaks is crucial for guiding public health responses and preventing widespread loss of life. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising framework for capturing viral infection dynamics and predicting their future progression.
Design and population
This study applies Disease-Informed Neural Networks (DINNs) to a hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection dynamics model to predict transmission within the liver. The model consists of four compartments: uninfected and infected hepatocytes, relaxed circular DNA (rcDNA)-containing capsids, and free viruses. The analysis uses experimental data from nine HBV-infected chimpanzees.
Core methods
Before estimating parameters, a structural identifiability analysis is performed to determine identifiable parameters. The study examines the impacts of parameter variations, experimental noise, sample size, network architecture, and learning rate on DINN performance. A global sensitivity analysis is conducted, and results are compared with nonlinear least-squares fitting and maximum likelihood estimation.
Main results
DINNs can estimate unknown parameters, capture infection dynamics, and predict disease progression even when experimental data for some model compartments are unavailable. The method identifies parameters that vary significantly across chimpanzees, and significant parameters detected by DINNs are consistent with those from global sensitivity analysis.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret DINNs as a robust framework for parameter estimation in viral infection dynamics, overcoming limitations of standard PINNs in inverse problems. The novelty lies in applying DINNs to HBV infection dynamics and demonstrating their effectiveness with sparse or noisy data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform HBV surveillance and treatment strategies by improving parameter estimation and prediction of disease progression. The approach may be applicable to other viral infections with compartmental models, enhancing public health preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate, as it demonstrates DINN effectiveness in a controlled setting with chimpanzee data but lacks validation in human populations. Limitations include potential generalizability issues and reliance on a specific HBV model structure.

非侵入性纤维化评分的动态变化作为 慢性乙型肝炎 恶化的早期预测因子

Dynamic changes in non-invasive fibrosis scores as early predictors of deterioration in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Muhammet Salih Tarhan
摘要中文翻译背景和研究目的:APRI、FIB-4、GPR 和 ALBI 等无创评分通常用于评估 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的肝纤维化和功能储备。然而,解释主要集中在单次测量上,短期变化(增量分数)的预后价值仍然不确定。本研究评估了这些评分的一年变化是否与慢性乙型肝炎患者常规随访期间基于实验室的综合恶化有关。患者和方法:这项回顾性 队列研究 包括成年 CHB 患者,两次实验室评估相隔约 12 个月。在基线(T0)和随访(T1)时计算APRI、FIB-4、GPR和ALBI;增量值定义为 ΔScore = T1 - T0。基于实验室的复合恶化被定义为基于白蛋白、总胆红素、INR或血小板计数满足≥1个标准。根据年龄、性别、HBV-DNA 水平、HBeAg 状态和 抗病毒药物 治疗进行调整的逻辑回归模型评估了 delta 评分与恶化之间的关联。ROC 分析评估了判别性能。结果:总共纳入 297 名患者(56.9% 为男性;中位年龄 45 岁)。实验室复合材料劣化发生率为 23.6%。基线无创评分与病情恶化无关,而 ΔAPRI、ΔFIB-4 和 ΔGPR 是显着的预测因子。在多变量分析中,每增加 0.1 个单位对应更高的风险:ΔAPRI (aOR 1.55;p = 0.004)、ΔFIB-4 (aOR 1.29;p < 0.001) 和 ΔGPR (aOR 1.86;p = 0.001)。标准化后,ΔFIB-4 表现出最强的关联(每增加 1-SD,aOR 7.32)。ΔFIB-4 也表现出最好的辨别力(AUC 0.730)。结论:非侵入性评分(尤其是 ΔFIB-4)的一年动态变化为在常规随访期间识别早期恶化提供了额外的临床相关预后信息。将 delta 评分监测纳入临床实践可能有助于在早期阶段检测基于实验室的恶化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 慢性乙型肝炎 患者非侵入性纤维化评分的一年变化是否与实验室综合恶化相关。
设计与对象
对 队列研究 成年患者进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,这些患者进行了两次实验室评估,两次实验室评估相隔约 12 个月。
核心方法
Logistic 回归模型用于评估 delta 评分与病情恶化之间的关联,并调整年龄、性别、HBV-DNA 水平、HBeAg 状态和 抗病毒药物 治疗。采用 ROC 分析来评估判别性能。
主要结果
23.6% 的患者发生实验室综合恶化。ΔAPRI、ΔFIB-4 和 ΔGPR 是恶化的显着预测因子,其中 ΔFIB-4 显示出最强的关联性(每增加 1-SD,aOR 7.32)和最佳的区分度(AUC 0.730)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,非侵入性评分(尤其是 ΔFIB-4)的一年动态变化为在常规随访期间识别早期恶化提供了额外的临床相关预后信息。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于将 delta 评分监测整合到临床实践中,以便在早期阶段检测 慢性乙型肝炎 患者基于实验室的恶化情况。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制,但证据充足,因为它基于明确定义的 队列研究 并针对关键临床变量进行了调整。

Dynamic changes in non-invasive fibrosis scores as early predictors of deterioration in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Muhammet Salih Tarhan
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND STUDY AIMS: Non-invasive scores such as APRI, FIB-4, GPR and ALBI are commonly used to evaluate liver fibrosis and functional reserve in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). However, interpretation has predominantly focused on single-time measurements, and the prognostic value of short-term changes (delta scores) remains uncertain. This study evaluated whether one-year changes in these scores are associated with laboratory-based composite deterioration during routine follow-up of CHB patients. PATIENTS AND METHODS: This retrospective cohort included adult CHB patients with two laboratory assessments approximately 12 months apart. APRI, FIB-4, GPR and ALBI were calculated at baseline (T0) and follow-up (T1); delta values were defined as ΔScore = T1 - T0. Laboratory-based composite deterioration was defined as meeting ≥1 criterion based on albumin, total bilirubin, INR, or platelet count. Logistic regression models, adjusted for age, gender, HBV-DNA level, HBeAg status, and antiviral therapy assessed associations between delta scores and deterioration. ROC analysis evaluated discriminative performance. RESULTS: A total of 297 patients were included (56.9% male; median age 45 years). Laboratory-based composite deterioration occurred in 23.6%. Baseline non-invasive scores were not associated with deterioration, while ΔAPRI, ΔFIB-4 and ΔGPR were significant predictors. In multivariable analyses, each 0.1-unit increase corresponded to higher risk: ΔAPRI (aOR 1.55; p = 0.004), ΔFIB-4 (aOR 1.29; p < 0.001) and ΔGPR (aOR 1.86; p = 0.001). When standardized, ΔFIB-4 demonstrated the strongest association (aOR 7.32 per 1-SD increase). ΔFIB-4 also showed the best discrimination (AUC 0.730). CONCLUSION: One-year dynamic changes in non-invasive scores, particularly ΔFIB-4, provide additional and clinically relevant prognostic information for identifying early deterioration during routine follow-up. Incorporating delta score monitoring into clinical practice may help to detect laboratory-based deterioration at an earlier stage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate whether one-year changes in non-invasive fibrosis scores are associated with laboratory-based composite deterioration in patients with chronic hepatitis B.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on adult patients with chronic hepatitis B who had two laboratory assessments approximately 12 months apart.
Core methods
Logistic regression models were used to assess associations between delta scores and deterioration, adjusting for age, gender, HBV-DNA level, HBeAg status, and antiviral therapy. ROC analysis was employed to evaluate discriminative performance.
Main results
Laboratory-based composite deterioration occurred in 23.6% of patients. ΔAPRI, ΔFIB-4, and ΔGPR were significant predictors of deterioration, with ΔFIB-4 showing the strongest association (aOR 7.32 per 1-SD increase) and best discrimination (AUC 0.730).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that one-year dynamic changes in non-invasive scores, particularly ΔFIB-4, provide additional and clinically relevant prognostic information for identifying early deterioration during routine follow-up.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the integration of delta score monitoring into clinical practice to detect laboratory-based deterioration at an earlier stage in patients with chronic hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong as it is based on a well-defined cohort and adjusted for key clinical variables.

HBeAg 阴性 乙型肝炎病毒 感染中核苷(酸)戒断与核苷(酸)戒断与聚乙二醇化干扰素佐剂的比较(NUC-B 试验)。

Nucleos(t)ide withdrawal vs Nucleos(t)ide withdrawal with adjuvant pegylated-interferon in HBeAg-negative hepatitis B virus infection (NUC-B Trial).
作者： Mark Thursz, Maud Lemoine, Ashley Brown, Ivana Carey, Jack Message, Patrick T F Kennedy, Daniel Forton, Martin Wiselka, Mark Aldersley, Martin Prince
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
对于慢性 HBV 感染 (CHB) 患者来说,有限治疗导致乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 持续丧失是一个重要的治疗目标。在 HBeAg 阴性感染患者中,停用核苷(酸)类似物 (NA) 可能会在 3 年后使 5-20% 的患者实现 HBsAg 消失。
设计与对象
NUC-B是一项随机、多中心试验,研究对象为接受NA治疗的非肝硬化HBeAg阴性CHB患者。2017年至2021年间共入组156例患者,其中82例分配至对照组,74例分配至PEG-IFNa组;中位年龄45岁,女性占24%,HBV基因型分布为:A型16%、B型6%、C型4%、D型24%、E型22%、其他1%、未知26%。
核心方法
患者被分配为仅 NA 停药组(对照)或 NA 停药组,然后在 NA 停止 (PEG-IFNa) 4 周后开始每周 16 周的 PEG-IFNa 180ug 疗程。主要终点是 3 年时 HBsAg 消失。
主要结果
At 3 years 3% of patients in the control arm and 14% of patients in the PEG-IFNa arm lost HBsAg (odds ratio 5.39; 95% confidence interval, (1.11, 26.19); p=0.037). In the control arm 34.9% of patients returned to NA therapy compared to 28.4% in the PEG_IFNa- arm. Exaggerated flares occurred in 27.9% of patients in the control arm compared with 13.4% in the PEG-IFNa arm.
解释与创新
取消 NA 治疗后使用辅助 PEG-IFNa 治疗会增加 HBsAg 消失率,同时减少过度发作的次数。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在 NA 停药中添加 PEG-IFNa 可能会通过增加 HBsAg 丢失和减少肝脏耀斑来改善 HBeAg 阴性 CHB 患者的 治疗 结局。
局限与证据强度
招募 240 名患者的目标并未实现,限制了统计能力。由于试验力度不足,证据强度中等,但显着的比值比和一致的次要结果支持了这一结论。

Nucleos(t)ide withdrawal vs Nucleos(t)ide withdrawal with adjuvant pegylated-interferon in HBeAg-negative hepatitis B virus infection (NUC-B Trial).

Authors: Mark Thursz, Maud Lemoine, Ashley Brown, Ivana Carey, Jack Message, Patrick T F Kennedy, Daniel Forton, Martin Wiselka, Mark Aldersley, Martin Prince
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Finite therapy resulting in sustained hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss for patients with chronic HBV infection (CHB) is an important therapeutic goal. In patients with HBeAg-negative infection, nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) withdrawal may achieve HBsAg loss in 5-20% of patients after 3 years. Pegylated interferon (PEG-IFNa) is a recognised treatment for CHB. METHODS: NUC-B was a randomised, multi-centre trial in NA-treated non-cirrhotic HBeAg negative patients with CHB. Patients were allocated to either NA withdrawal alone (control) or NA withdrawal followed by a 16 week course of PEG-IFNa 180ug weekly commencing 4 weeks after NA cessation (PEG-IFNa). The primary endpoint was HBsAg loss at 3 years. RESULTS: The target recruitment of 240 patients was not achieved. 156 patients, 82 to control arm , 74 to PEG-IFNa arm , were recruited between 2017 and 2021; median age 45 years, 24% female, HBV Genotypes -A 16%, B 6%, C 4%, D 24%, E 22%, other 1%, unknown 26%. At 3 years 3% of patients in the contol arm and 14% of patients in the PEG-IFNa arm lost HBsAg (odds ratio 5.39; 95% confidence interval, (1.11, 26.19); p=0.037). In the control arm 34.9% of patients returned to NA therapy compared to 28.4% in the PEG_IFNa- arm. Exaggerated flares occurred in 27.9% of patients in the control arm compared with 13.4% in the PEG-IFNa arm. CONCLUSIONS: The use of adjuvant PEG-IFNa therapy after withdrawal of NA therapy increases the rate of HBsAg loss whilst simultaneously reducing the number of exaggerated flares.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Finite therapy resulting in sustained hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss for patients with chronic HBV infection (CHB) is an important therapeutic goal. In patients with HBeAg-negative infection, nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) withdrawal may achieve HBsAg loss in 5-20% of patients after 3 years.
Design and population
NUC-B was a randomised, multi-centre trial in NA-treated non-cirrhotic HBeAg negative patients with CHB. 156 patients, 82 to control arm and 74 to PEG-IFNa arm, were recruited between 2017 and 2021; median age 45 years, 24% female, HBV Genotypes -A 16%, B 6%, C 4%, D 24%, E 22%, other 1%, unknown 26%.
Core methods
Patients were allocated to either NA withdrawal alone (control) or NA withdrawal followed by a 16 week course of PEG-IFNa 180ug weekly commencing 4 weeks after NA cessation (PEG-IFNa). The primary endpoint was HBsAg loss at 3 years.
Main results
At 3 years 3% of patients in the control arm and 14% of patients in the PEG-IFNa arm lost HBsAg (odds ratio 5.39; 95% confidence interval, (1.11, 26.19); p=0.037). In the control arm 34.9% of patients returned to NA therapy compared to 28.4% in the PEG_IFNa- arm. Exaggerated flares occurred in 27.9% of patients in the control arm compared with 13.4% in the PEG-IFNa arm.
Interpretation and novelty
The use of adjuvant PEG-IFNa therapy after withdrawal of NA therapy increases the rate of HBsAg loss whilst simultaneously reducing the number of exaggerated flares.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that adding PEG-IFNa to NA withdrawal may improve treatment outcomes for HBeAg-negative CHB patients by increasing HBsAg loss and reducing liver flares.
Limitations and evidence strength
The target recruitment of 240 patients was not achieved, limiting the statistical power. The evidence strength is moderate due to the underpowered trial but the significant odds ratio and consistent secondary outcomes support the conclusion.

替诺福韦艾拉酚胺和恩替卡韦之间的乙型肝炎表面抗原聚糖异构体动力学差异:乙型肝炎 e 抗原阳性患者的肝细胞癌风险分层。

Differential Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Glycan Isomer Kinetics Between Tenofovir Alafenamide and Entecavir: Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Stratification in Hepatitis B e Antigen-Positive Patients.
作者： Yuji Kita, Ayato Murata, Hiroki Nago, Masahiro Yamaguchi, Yoko Kato, Rihwa Om, Yuichro Terai, Yuji Ikeda, Shunsuke Sato, Yuji Shimada
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
核苷类似物 (NA) 疗法可抑制 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA,但残留肝细胞癌 (HCC) 风险仍然存在。O-糖基化乙型肝炎表面抗原聚糖异构体 (HBsAgGi) 独特地反映了病毒颗粒负荷。
设计与对象
我们评估了替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 与恩替卡韦 (ETV) 对 48 周 HBsAgGi 动力学的影响及其在 90 名未接受 NA 治疗的患者中对 HCC 风险进行分层的效用(ETV:73;TAF:17)。
核心方法
由于该测定以基因型 C 特异性 O-糖基化为目标,因此研究结果不能推广到基因型 A、B 或 D。有利的反应被定义为降低或维持低 HBsAgGi 水平。
主要结果
TAF independently predicted a favourable response compared with ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p = 0.030). In HBeAg-positive patients, a poor response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.96-20.35, p = 0.001); however, the exact magnitude warrants cautious interpretation due to wide CIs.
解释与创新
总之,on-治疗 HBsAgGi 反应是 HBeAg 阳性患者残留 HCC 风险分层的特异性替代标志物,TAF 在诱导这些有利动力学方面比 ETV 更有效。
科研与公卫意义
将 HBsAgGi 反应添加到 aMAP 评分中,仅改善了 HBeAg 阳性 队列研究 中的长期 HCC 预测。HBeAg 状态和 HBsAgGi 反应之间存在与 HCC 发展相关的显着交互作用 (p = 0.038)。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告基因型 C 之外的具体设计限制、偏倚、混杂因素或普遍性。由于样本量有限且置信区间较宽,证据强度中等。

Differential Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Glycan Isomer Kinetics Between Tenofovir Alafenamide and Entecavir: Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Stratification in Hepatitis B e Antigen-Positive Patients.

Authors: Yuji Kita, Ayato Murata, Hiroki Nago, Masahiro Yamaguchi, Yoko Kato, Rihwa Om, Yuichro Terai, Yuji Ikeda, Shunsuke Sato, Yuji Shimada
Original abstract (English)
Nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy suppresses hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA, but residual hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk persists. O-glycosylated hepatitis B surface antigen glycan isomer (HBsAgGi) uniquely reflects virion burden. We evaluated the impact of tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) versus entecavir (ETV) on 48-week HBsAgGi kinetics and its utility in stratifying HCC risk in 90 NA-naïve patients (ETV: 73; TAF: 17). A favourable response was defined as a reduction or maintenance of low HBsAgGi levels. TAF independently predicted a favourable response compared with ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p = 0.030). A significant interaction occurred between HBeAg status and HBsAgGi response in relation to HCC development (p = 0.038). In HBeAg-positive patients, a poor response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.96-20.35, p = 0.001); however, the exact magnitude warrants cautious interpretation due to wide CIs. This association was absent in HBeAg-negative cases. Adding HBsAgGi response to the aMAP score improved long-term HCC prediction exclusively in the HBeAg-positive cohort. As the assay targets genotype C-specific O-glycosylation, the findings cannot be generalised to genotypes A, B, or D. In conclusion, on-treatment HBsAgGi response is a specific surrogate marker for stratifying residual HCC risk in HBeAg-positive patients, and TAF is more effective than ETV in inducing these favourable kinetics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background

荷兰乙型肝炎、丙型肝炎和 HIV 血清流行率 的趋势:二十年全国 PIENTER 血清调查的见解。

Trends in Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and HIV Seroprevalence in the Netherlands: Insights From Two Decades of Nationwide PIENTER Serosurveys.
作者： van Straten CGJI, Birgit H B van Benthem, Fiona R M van der Klis, Gaby Smits, Eric R A Vos, Tom Woudenberg, Marc van der Valk, Eline L M Op de Coul, Christine G J I van Straten
摘要中文翻译尽管采取了有效的 治疗 和预防措施,乙型肝炎 (HBV)、丙型肝炎 (HCV) 和艾滋病毒仍然是公共卫生挑战。感染通常是无症状的,导致未确诊病例、传播 以及可预防的发病率和 传播。在荷兰,疾病负担集中在特定人群,有许多感染未被发现。我们使用来自三项全国性横断面血清调查(PIENTER:1995-1996、2006-2007 和 2016-2017)的 14,444 名 15-79 岁参与者的数据估计了 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 血清流行率,确定了决定因素并评估了 20 年来的趋势。参与者完成了有关人口统计、行为和社会经济因素的问卷调查,并在 2017 年对所有轮次的 HBV 和 HCV 以及 HIV 样本进行了血清样本检测。血清流行率 估计值根据荷兰人口进行加权,使用加权泊松回归分析趋势,并使用多变量泊松回归评估决定因素。加权 HBV 血清流行率 在整个调查中保持稳定(3%-5% 过去或现在的感染;< 0.4% 活动感染)。乙型肝炎病毒 患病率 在第一代流动人口中最高,年龄、流动背景和性传播感染史是关键决定因素。HCV 血清流行率 仍然较低(< 0.4%),并且在非西方移民中最高。HIV 患病率 为 0.1%,在非西方移民和男男性行为者中感染率最高。只有 9% 的活动性 HBV 病例知道自己的状况。总体而言,荷兰的 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 血清流行率 仍然处于较低水平,但集中在意识有限的特定群体中。这些发现支持有针对性的预防、筛查和与护理挂钩的战略,以实现世卫组织 2030 年消除目标,并强调持续进行基于人群的(血清)监测以指导公共卫生政策的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管有有效的 传播 和预防措施,乙型肝炎 (HBV)、丙型肝炎 (HCV) 和 HIV 仍然是公共卫生挑战,感染通常无症状,导致未确诊病例和进一步的 传播。在荷兰,疾病负担集中在特定人群中,有许多未发现的感染,因此需要对 血清流行率 随着时间的推移的趋势进行评估。
设计与对象
该研究利用了三项全国性横断面血清调查(PIENTER:1995-1996、2006-2007 和 2016-2017)的数据,包括 14,444 名 15-79 岁的参与者,以估计 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 血清流行率、确定决定因素并评估 20 年来的趋势。
核心方法
参与者完成了有关人口统计、行为和社会经济因素的问卷调查,并在 2017 年对所有轮次的 HBV 和 HCV 以及 HIV 样本进行了血清样本检测。血清流行率 估计值根据荷兰人口进行加权,使用加权泊松回归分析趋势,并使用多变量泊松回归评估决定因素。
主要结果
加权 HBV 血清流行率 在整个调查中保持稳定(3%-5% 过去或现在感染;< 0.4% 活动性感染),在第一代移民中最高,只有 9% 的活动性 HBV 病例知道自己的状况。HCV 血清流行率 仍然较低(< 0.4%),在非西方移民中最高,而 HIV 患病率 为 0.1%,在非西方移民和男男性行为者中最高。
解释与创新
总体而言,荷兰的HBV、HCV和HIV 血清流行率仍然较低,但集中在认知有限的特定群体中,这表明虽然一般人群患病率稳定,但在这些亚群中存在持续的传播风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持有针对性的预防、筛查和与护理挂钩的战略,以实现世卫组织 2030 年消除目标,并强调持续进行基于人群的(血清)监测以指导公共卫生政策的重要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告具体的设计限制、偏差或混杂因素,但使用二十年来三项大规模全国性调查的加权数据为评估人口趋势提供了中等水平的证据。

Trends in Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and HIV Seroprevalence in the Netherlands: Insights From Two Decades of Nationwide PIENTER Serosurveys.

Authors: van Straten CGJI, Birgit H B van Benthem, Fiona R M van der Klis, Gaby Smits, Eric R A Vos, Tom Woudenberg, Marc van der Valk, Eline L M Op de Coul, Christine G J I van Straten
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B (HBV), hepatitis C (HCV), and HIV remain public health challenges despite effective treatments and preventive options. Infections are often asymptomatic, leading to undiagnosed cases, onward transmission, and preventable morbidity and mortality. In the Netherlands, disease burden is concentrated in specific populations, with many undetected infections. We estimated HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence, identified determinants, and assessed trends over 20 years using data from 14,444 participants aged 15-79 years from three nationwide cross-sectional serosurveys (PIENTER: 1995-1996, 2006-2007, and 2016-2017). Participants completed questionnaires on demographics, behaviour, and socio-economic factors, and serum samples were tested for HBV and HCV in all rounds and HIV in 2017. Seroprevalence estimates were weighted to the Dutch population, trends were analysed using weighted Poisson regression, and determinants were evaluated using multivariable Poisson regression. Weighted HBV seroprevalence remained stable across surveys (3%-5% past or present infection; < 0.4% active infection). HBV prevalence was highest among first-generation migrants, with older age, migration background, and STI history as key determinants. HCV seroprevalence remained low (< 0.4%) and was highest among non-Western migrants. HIV prevalence was 0.1% and highest among non-Western migrants and men who have sex with men. Only 9% of active HBV cases were aware of their status. Overall, HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence remain low in the Netherlands but are concentrated in specific groups with limited awareness. These findings support targeted prevention, screening, and linkage-to-care strategies to achieve the 2030 WHO elimination goals and highlight the importance of continued population-based (sero-)monitoring to guide public health policy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B (HBV), hepatitis C (HCV), and HIV remain public health challenges despite effective treatments and preventive options, with infections often asymptomatic leading to undiagnosed cases and onward transmission. In the Netherlands, the disease burden is concentrated in specific populations with many undetected infections, necessitating an assessment of seroprevalence trends over time.
Design and population
The study utilized data from three nationwide cross-sectional serosurveys (PIENTER: 1995-1996, 2006-2007, and 2016-2017) including 14,444 participants aged 15-79 years to estimate HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence, identify determinants, and assess trends over 20 years.
Core methods
Participants completed questionnaires on demographics, behaviour, and socio-economic factors, and serum samples were tested for HBV and HCV in all rounds and HIV in 2017. Seroprevalence estimates were weighted to the Dutch population, trends were analysed using weighted Poisson regression, and determinants were evaluated using multivariable Poisson regression.
Main results
Weighted HBV seroprevalence remained stable across surveys (3%-5% past or present infection; < 0.4% active infection), was highest among first-generation migrants, and only 9% of active HBV cases were aware of their status. HCV seroprevalence remained low (< 0.4%) and was highest among non-Western migrants, while HIV prevalence was 0.1% and highest among non-Western migrants and men who have sex with men.
Interpretation and novelty
Overall, HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence remain low in the Netherlands but are concentrated in specific groups with limited awareness, indicating that while general population prevalence is stable, persistent transmission risks exist within these subpopulations.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support targeted prevention, screening, and linkage-to-care strategies to achieve the 2030 WHO elimination goals and highlight the importance of continued population-based (sero-)monitoring to guide public health policy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, bias, or confounding factors, but the use of weighted data from three large-scale nationwide surveys over two decades provides a moderate level of evidence for assessing population trends.

乙型肝炎病毒 血清流行率 乌干达古卢地区转诊医院接受产前护理的异性夫妇

Hepatitis B virus Seroprevalence Among Heterosexual Couples Attending Antenatal Care at Gulu Regional Referral Hospital, Uganda
作者： Patrick Otim, Felix Bongomin, Emmanuel Ochola
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 是一个全球性的健康问题,也是慢性肝病(包括肝硬化和肝细胞癌)的主要原因。关于夫妇测试和结果的文献有限,特别是在乌干达北部。
设计与对象
2023年12月1日至2024年2月28日期间,对参加乌干达古卢GRRH ANC的符合条件的孕妇及其男性伴侣进行了一项以医院为基础的横断面研究。
核心方法
系统随机抽样后,访谈员进行了预先测试的问卷调查。收集血样进行乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)快速检测,该检测按照制造商的说明进行。使用 STATA 版本 15 SE 中的逻辑回归分析定性数据。
主要结果
A total of 242 couple pairs were enrolled. Overall, 8.3% (n=40) participants were seropositive for HBsAg; Male partners [M] (10.7%, n=26) and pregnant women [F] (5.8%, n=14). Factors associated with positive HBsAg were being male (adjusted odds ratio [aOR]: 2.45, 95% Confidence Interval [CI], 1.15 - 5.21, p=0.02), daily alcohol consumption (aOR: 4.73, 95% CI: 1.40 -16.04, p=0.01), and no prior vaccination against HBV (aOR: 12.01, 95% CI: 4.07 - 35.65, p<0.001).
解释与创新
HBsAg 血清阳性率很高,特别是在孕妇的男性伴侣中。该研究强调迫切需要加强年轻人婚前乙型肝炎筛查并提高其敏感性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,参加产前保健的夫妇需要进行乙型肝炎病毒筛查,这有助于早期发现和预防 传播。这支持了乌干达卫生部关于在产​​前护理期间进行乙型肝炎病毒筛查的建议。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在单一医院环境中进行的,这可能会限制普遍性。由于横截面设计和潜在的选择偏差,证据强度中等。

Hepatitis B virus Seroprevalence Among Heterosexual Couples Attending Antenatal Care at Gulu Regional Referral Hospital, Uganda

Authors: Patrick Otim, Felix Bongomin, Emmanuel Ochola
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is a global health concern and the leading cause of chronic liver disease, including liver cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. The Uganda Ministry of health recommends screening of HBV for all pregnant mothers during antenatal care (ANC). However, limited literature exists regarding couple testing and outcomes, particularly in Northern Uganda. Objective We determined HBV seroprevalence and associated factors among pregnant women and their partner attending ANC clinic of Gulu Regional Referral hospital (GRRH), Gulu, Uganda. Methods Between 1st December 2023 and 28th February 2024, a hospital-based, cross-sectional study was conducted among eligible pregnant women and their male partners attending ANC of GRRH, Gulu, Uganda. Following systematic random sampling, pretested questionnaire was administered by an interviewer. Blood samples were collected for HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) rapid test, which was performed according to manufacturer’s instructions. Qualitative data was analysed using logistic regression in STATA version 15 SE. P<0.05 was considered statistically significant. Results A total of 242 couple pairs were enrolled. Overall, 8.3% (n=40) participants were seropositive for HBsAg; Male partners [M] (10.7%, n=26) and pregnant women [F] (5.8%, n=14). Sero-concordance (M negative [-ve] and F [-ve]; 83.5%, n=202), (M positive [+ve] and F [+ve]; 4.1%, n=10) and sero-discordance (M +ve and F -ve; 8.7%, n=21), (M -ve and F +ve; 3.7%, n=9). Factors associated with positive HBsAg were, being male (adjusted odds ratio [aOR]: 2.45, 95% Confidence Interval [CI], 1.15 - 5.21, p=0.02), daily alcohol consumption (aOR: 4.73, 95% CI: 1.40 -16.04, p=0.01), and no prior vaccination against HBV (aOR: 12.01, 95% CI: 4.07 - 35.65, p<0.001). Conclusion There is a high HBsAg seropositivity rate, particularly among male partners of pregnant women. There is an urgent need to have couples who come for ANC to be screened for HBV and to strengthen and sensitise young people for premarital HBV screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is a global health concern and the leading cause of chronic liver disease, including liver cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Limited literature exists regarding couple testing and outcomes, particularly in Northern Uganda.
Design and population
Between 1st December 2023 and 28th February 2024, a hospital-based, cross-sectional study was conducted among eligible pregnant women and their male partners attending ANC of GRRH, Gulu, Uganda.
Core methods
Following systematic random sampling, a pretested questionnaire was administered by an interviewer. Blood samples were collected for HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) rapid test, which was performed according to manufacturer's instructions. Qualitative data was analysed using logistic regression in STATA version 15 SE.
Main results
A total of 242 couple pairs were enrolled. Overall, 8.3% (n=40) participants were seropositive for HBsAg; Male partners [M] (10.7%, n=26) and pregnant women [F] (5.8%, n=14). Factors associated with positive HBsAg were being male (adjusted odds ratio [aOR]: 2.45, 95% Confidence Interval [CI], 1.15 - 5.21, p=0.02), daily alcohol consumption (aOR: 4.73, 95% CI: 1.40 -16.04, p=0.01), and no prior vaccination against HBV (aOR: 12.01, 95% CI: 4.07 - 35.65, p<0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
There is a high HBsAg seropositivity rate, particularly among male partners of pregnant women. The study highlights the urgent need to strengthen and sensitise young people for premarital HBV screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for couples attending antenatal care to be screened for HBV, which can help in early detection and prevention of transmission. This supports the Uganda Ministry of Health's recommendation for HBV screening during antenatal care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a single hospital setting, which may limit generalisability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and potential selection bias.

美国取消通用乙型肝炎出生剂量 疫苗接种 的影响。

Impact of Removing the Universal Hepatitis B Birth-Dose Vaccination in the US.
作者： Margaret L Lind, Matt D T Hitchings, Roshni P Singh, Benjamin P Linas, Derek A T Cummings, Rachel L Epstein
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于 ACIP 于 2025 年决定改变政策,这项研究需要评估以针对美国新生儿和慢性 HBV 感染的针对性建议取代通用乙型肝炎出生剂量 疫苗接种 的潜在影响。
设计与对象
隔室模型模拟了美国 3,659,289 名婴儿在两个 疫苗 建议下的出生 队列研究:通用出生剂量 疫苗接种 和目标出生剂量 疫苗接种,参数值来自文献,并纳入了 5,000 次迭代的不确定性。
核心方法
该模型使用孕产妇 HBV 筛查率和未筛查母亲的婴儿出生剂量 疫苗接种 覆盖率作为关键变量,比较了普遍与目标出生剂量 疫苗接种 建议下的结果。
主要结果
在86%的产前筛查率下,普遍出生剂量疫苗接种导致中位数为1,292例新生儿感染(95%预测区间,670-2,228),而针对性建议在10%覆盖率下与628例额外新生儿感染相关(95%预测区间,340-1,034),在80%覆盖率下与69例额外感染相关(95%预测区间,-32至190),这些感染均发生在未筛查母亲的婴儿中。
解释与创新
作者解释说,目标出生剂量 疫苗 的建议可能会增加新生儿感染,除非孕产妇筛查大幅增加,或者未筛查母亲的婴儿的 疫苗接种 覆盖率超过目前的水平,这凸显了普遍筛查和 疫苗接种 作为补充保障措施的持续重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为政策决策提供了信息,表明取消通用出生剂量 疫苗接种 可能会导致 HBV 感染增加,除非伴随着孕产妇筛查或 疫苗接种 覆盖率的实质性改善,强调需要仔细考虑替代保障措施。
局限与证据强度
尽管大样本量和广泛的迭代提供了稳健的估计,但由于依赖于具有参数不确定性的分区模型,证据强度中等;局限性包括历史覆盖率趋势将持续的假设以及未建模的现实世界因素的潜力。

Impact of Removing the Universal Hepatitis B Birth-Dose Vaccination in the US.

Authors: Margaret L Lind, Matt D T Hitchings, Roshni P Singh, Benjamin P Linas, Derek A T Cummings, Rachel L Epstein
Original abstract (English)
IMPORTANCE: In December 2025, the Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices (ACIP) voted to replace the universal hepatitis B birth-dose recommendation with shared clinical decision-making for infants born to mothers who screen negative for hepatitis B surface antigen. Although the proposal would not alter recommendations for infants of unscreened mothers, historical data suggest that removing a universal birth-dose vaccine recommendation may reduce vaccination coverage in this group. OBJECTIVE: To estimate the impact of replacing universal hepatitis B virus (HBV) birth-dose vaccination with a targeted recommendation on neonatal and subsequent chronic HBV infections in the US. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: A compartmental model and simulated a US birth cohort (n = 3 659 289) under the 2 vaccine recommendations: universal birth-dose vaccination and targeted birth-dose vaccination vaccine recommendations, where the birth dose was recommended to infants of screened-positive or unscreened mothers and shared-decision making is recommended for infants of screened-negative mothers. Parameter values were literature derived and uncertainty was incorporated across 5000 iterations. These data were analyzed from September through November 2025. EXPOSURES: Birth-dose vaccine recommendations and birth-dose vaccination coverage among infants of unscreened mothers. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: Numbers of neonatal and subsequent chronic HBV infections. RESULTS: With the current maternal HBV screening rate of 86%, the universal birth-dose vaccine recommendation resulted in a median of 1292 neonatal infections (95% percentile interval [PI], 670-2228). In comparison, the targeted birth-dose vaccine recommendation was associated with 628 additional neonatal infections (95% PI, 340-1034) when birth-dose vaccination coverage among infants of unscreened mothers was 10% (mirroring historic coverage declines under a targeted recommendation) and 69 additional infections (95% PI, -32 to 190) when coverage was 80% (mirroring levels under a universal recommendation.) To offset the excess infections under the targeted birth-dose vaccine recommendation, more than 100 000 additional pregnant individuals would need to be screened if the birth-dose vaccination coverage among infants of unscreened mothers was 80%, and more than 400 000 if coverage was 10%. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: Findings from this study indicate that the targeted birth-dose vaccine recommendation will likely increase neonatal infections unless maternal screening rises substantially or vaccination coverage among infants of unscreened mothers exceeds current levels. As historic data show such improvements are unlikely, these findings underscore the continued importance of universal screening and vaccination as complementary safeguards.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to evaluate the potential impact of replacing universal hepatitis B birth-dose vaccination with a targeted recommendation on neonatal and chronic HBV infections in the US, given the ACIP's 2025 decision to change the policy.
Design and population
A compartmental model simulated a US birth cohort of 3,659,289 infants under two vaccine recommendations: universal birth-dose vaccination and targeted birth-dose vaccination, with parameter values derived from literature and uncertainty incorporated across 5,000 iterations.
Core methods
The model compared outcomes under universal versus targeted birth-dose vaccination recommendations, using maternal HBV screening rates and birth-dose vaccination coverage among infants of unscreened mothers as key variables.
Main results
With an 86% maternal screening rate, universal birth-dose vaccination resulted in a median of 1,292 neonatal infections (95% PI, 670-2,228), while the targeted recommendation was associated with 628 additional neonatal infections (95% PI, 340-1,034) at 10% coverage and 69 additional infections (95% PI, -32 to 190) at 80% coverage among infants of unscreened mothers.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the targeted birth-dose vaccine recommendation will likely increase neonatal infections unless maternal screening rises substantially or vaccination coverage among infants of unscreened mothers exceeds current levels, highlighting the continued importance of universal screening and vaccination as complementary safeguards.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform policy decisions by suggesting that removing universal birth-dose vaccination could lead to increased HBV infections unless accompanied by substantial improvements in maternal screening or vaccination coverage, emphasizing the need for careful consideration of alternative safeguards.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on a compartmental model with parameter uncertainty, though the large sample size and extensive iterations provide robust estimates; limitations include the assumption that historical coverage trends will continue and the potential for unmodeled real-world factors.

澳大利亚 慢性乙型肝炎 护理和 治疗 依从性的纵向模式:国家关联数据 队列研究 研究。

Longitudinal Patterns of Chronic Hepatitis B Care and Treatment Adherence in Australia: A National Linked Data Cohort Study.
作者： Jennifer H MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Stacey L Rowe, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C Cowie
摘要中文翻译目前澳大利亚对 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 治疗 的吸收和护理远低于目标水平,导致可预防的肝病和癌症的持续负担。了解个人层面的护理参与轨迹和社会人口因素的变化对于指导改善护理获取和保留的努力至关重要。这项研究分析了通过澳大利亚国家健康保险系统提供的乙型肝炎病毒载量 (VL) 检测和 Z​​XQTERMNQXZ 治疗 记录,这些记录与来自各个政府数据集的移民、死亡和社会人口统计数据相关。对人群的 治疗 和 VL 测试进行了评估,并使用慢性乙型肝炎患者人数的模型估计来计算吸收率。还评估了基于分发脚本的 VL 测试频率和 治疗 遵守情况。2023 年澳大利亚估计有 219,800 名慢性乙型肝炎患者,其中 118,334 人 (53.8%) 在 2014-2023 年期间接受了 VL 检测或 治疗。原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民对 VL 测试和 治疗 的估计采用率较低,并且因出生地区而异。在接受任何 VL 测试的 81,360 人中,只有 17,352 人 (21.3%) 定期接受测试,而 31,715 人 (39.0%) 有先前监测的证据,但自 2019 年以来没有进行 VL 测试。大多数开始 治疗 的人继续治疗并获得足够的供应以维持依从性。这项研究表明,澳大利亚在慢性乙型肝炎护理方面存在巨大差距,特别是在对未参加 治疗 的人群进行持续监测方面。优先人群的吸收差异很明显。应优先考虑重新参与那些失去随访的人。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前澳大利亚对 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 治疗 的吸收和护理远低于目标水平,导致可预防的肝病和癌症的持续负担。
设计与对象
这项研究分析了通过澳大利亚国家健康保险系统提供的乙型肝炎病毒载量 (VL) 检测和 Z​​XQTERMNQXZ 治疗 记录,这些记录与来自各个政府数据集的移民、死亡和社会人口统计数据相关。
核心方法
对人群的 治疗 和 VL 测试进行了评估,并使用慢性乙型肝炎患者人数的模型估计来计算吸收率。
主要结果
2023 年澳大利亚估计有 219,800 名慢性乙型肝炎患者,其中 118,334 人 (53.8%) 在 2014-2023 年期间接受了 VL 检测或 治疗。在接受任何 VL 检测的 81,360 人中,只有 17,352 人 (21.3%) 定期接受检测,而 31,715 人 (39.0%) 有先前监测的证据,但自 2019 年以来没有进行 VL 检测。
解释与创新
这项研究表明,澳大利亚在慢性乙型肝炎护理方面存在巨大差距,特别是在对未参加 治疗 的人群进行持续监测方面。
科研与公卫意义
原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民对 VL 测试和 治疗 的估计采用率较低,并且因出生地区而异。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究设计的局限性。

Longitudinal Patterns of Chronic Hepatitis B Care and Treatment Adherence in Australia: A National Linked Data Cohort Study.

Authors: Jennifer H MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Stacey L Rowe, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C Cowie
Original abstract (English)
Current uptake of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) treatment and care in Australia is well below target levels, leading to an ongoing burden of preventable liver disease and cancer. Understanding individual-level trajectories of care engagement and variation by sociodemographic factors is essential to guide efforts to improve access to and retention in care. This study analysed records of hepatitis B viral load (VL) testing and antiviral treatment provided through Australia's national health insurance system, which were linked to migration, death, and sociodemographic data from various government datasets. Treatment and VL testing by population group were assessed, and uptake was calculated using modelled estimates of the number living with CHB. VL testing frequency and treatment adherence based on scripts dispensed were also assessed. Of the estimated 219,800 people living with CHB in Australia in 2023, 118,334 (53.8%) received either VL testing or treatment during 2014-2023. Estimated uptake of VL testing and treatment uptake were lower among Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander people and also varied by region of birth. Of the 81,360 who received any VL testing, only 17,352 (21.3%) received it regularly, while 31,715 (39.0%) had evidence of prior monitoring but no VL testing since 2019. The majority of those who initiated treatment continued therapy and received supply sufficient for adherence. This study shows that substantial gaps exist in CHB care in Australia, particularly in continued monitoring among those not on treatment. Disparities in uptake are evident by priority population. Re-engaging those lost to follow-up should be prioritised.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Current uptake of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) treatment and care in Australia is well below target levels, leading to an ongoing burden of preventable liver disease and cancer.
Design and population
This study analysed records of hepatitis B viral load (VL) testing and antiviral treatment provided through Australia's national health insurance system, which were linked to migration, death, and sociodemographic data from various government datasets.
Core methods
Treatment and VL testing by population group were assessed, and uptake was calculated using modelled estimates of the number living with CHB.
Main results
Of the estimated 219,800 people living with CHB in Australia in 2023, 118,334 (53.8%) received either VL testing or treatment during 2014-2023. Of the 81,360 who received any VL testing, only 17,352 (21.3%) received it regularly, while 31,715 (39.0%) had evidence of prior monitoring but no VL testing since 2019.
Interpretation and novelty
This study shows that substantial gaps exist in CHB care in Australia, particularly in continued monitoring among those not on treatment.
Scientific and public-health significance
Estimated uptake of VL testing and treatment uptake were lower among Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander people and also varied by region of birth.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study design.

未经治疗的免疫耐受者与免疫耐受者之间的肝细胞癌风险接受治疗的免疫活跃 慢性乙型肝炎 患者。

Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Between Untreated Immune Tolerant Vs. Treated Immune Active Chronic Hepatitis B Patients.
作者： Robert J Wong, Zeyuan Yang, Ramsey Cheung
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者在未经治疗的免疫耐受 (U-IT) 与治疗的免疫活跃 (T-IA) 阶段发生肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的风险尚不清楚,并且大多数数据来自亚洲人群。
设计与对象
我们对 2010 年 1 月 1 日至 2021 年 12 月 31 日期间符合 U-IT 或 T-IA 标准的慢性乙型肝炎退伍军人进行了倾向评分加权分析(随访至 2024 年 12 月 31 日)。总共确定了 591 名 U-IT 和 555 名 T-IA CHB 患者。
核心方法
使用倾向评分加权分析比较 U-IT 组和 T-IA 组之间的肝硬化、HCC 或死亡的 发病率。
主要结果
Over a median follow up of 6.4 years (IQR 3.0-10.4), incidence of HCC was significantly lower in U-IT patients vs. T-IA patients (0.28 vs. 0.54 per 100 person-years; HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.32-0.83, p = 0.007). However, the risk of cirrhosis or all-cause mortality was similar between both T-IA and U-IT CHB patients.
解释与创新
在主要为非亚裔的慢性乙型肝炎成人 队列研究 中,我们观察到与 T-IA 患者相比,U-IT 患者的 HCC 风险较低。虽然这与亚洲国家的一些研究形成鲜明对比,但这些数据确实强调了在 IA 阶段优先考虑并确保及时治疗 CHB 患者的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了在免疫活跃期及时治疗慢性乙型肝炎患者的重要性,尽管免疫耐受患者的常规 治疗 仍存在争议,并且需要更多数据来更好地了解长期结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在主要非亚洲人 队列研究 中进行的,这可能会限制对亚洲人群的普遍适用性。尽管有倾向评分加权,但由于观察设计和潜在的残留混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Between Untreated Immune Tolerant Vs. Treated Immune Active Chronic Hepatitis B Patients.

Authors: Robert J Wong, Zeyuan Yang, Ramsey Cheung
Original abstract (English)
The risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) between chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients in the untreated immune tolerant (U-IT) vs. treated immune active (T-IA) phases is not clear, and most data are in Asian populations. We aim to evaluate long-term risks of cirrhosis, HCC, and overall mortality between U-IT vs. T-IA CHB patients among a national cohort of U.S. We conducted a propensity score weighted analysis of Veterans with CHB who met criteria for U-IT or T-IA from 1/1/2010 to 12/31/2021 (with follow-up through 12/31/2024). Incidence of cirrhosis, HCC, or death were compared between U-IT and T-IA groups. A total of 591 U-IT and 555 T-IA CHB patients were identified (mean age 56.4-57.5 years, 45%-50% non-Hispanic white, 37%-40% African American, 9%-10% Asian, 25%-29% concurrent diabetes, 35%-39% with FIB-4 > 3.25). Over a median follow up of 6.4 years (IQR 3.0-10.4), incidence of HCC was significantly lower in U-IT patients vs. T-IA patients (0.28 vs. 0.54 per 100 person-years; HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.32-0.83, p = 0.007). However, the risk of cirrhosis or all-cause mortality was similar between both T-IA and U-IT CHB patients. Among a predominantly non-Asian cohort of adults with CHB, we observed that HCC risk was lower in U-IT patients compared to T-IA patients. While this contrasts with some studies in Asian countries, these data do emphasize the importance of prioritizing and ensuring timely therapy for CHB patients in the IA phase. However, routine treatment in IT patients remains controversial and more data are needed to better understand long-term outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) between chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients in the untreated immune tolerant (U-IT) vs. treated immune active (T-IA) phases is not clear, and most data are in Asian populations.
Design and population
We conducted a propensity score weighted analysis of Veterans with CHB who met criteria for U-IT or T-IA from 1/1/2010 to 12/31/2021 (with follow-up through 12/31/2024). A total of 591 U-IT and 555 T-IA CHB patients were identified.
Core methods
Incidence of cirrhosis, HCC, or death were compared between U-IT and T-IA groups using a propensity score weighted analysis.
Main results
Over a median follow up of 6.4 years (IQR 3.0-10.4), incidence of HCC was significantly lower in U-IT patients vs. T-IA patients (0.28 vs. 0.54 per 100 person-years; HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.32-0.83, p = 0.007). However, the risk of cirrhosis or all-cause mortality was similar between both T-IA and U-IT CHB patients.
Interpretation and novelty
Among a predominantly non-Asian cohort of adults with CHB, we observed that HCC risk was lower in U-IT patients compared to T-IA patients. While this contrasts with some studies in Asian countries, these data do emphasize the importance of prioritizing and ensuring timely therapy for CHB patients in the IA phase.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of timely therapy for CHB patients in the immune active phase, though routine treatment in immune tolerant patients remains controversial and more data are needed to better understand long-term outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a predominantly non-Asian cohort, which may limit generalizability to Asian populations. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and potential residual confounding despite propensity score weighting.

西班牙 队列研究 患者中与表面抗原 (HBsAg) 清除相关的因素:对长期预后的影响。

Factors Associated With Surface Antigen (HBsAg) Clearance in a Spanish Cohort of Chronic Hepatitis B Patients: Influence on the Long-Term Prognosis.
作者： Paula Gavilán, Juan-Carlos Gavilán, Alberto Piserra-Lopez
摘要中文翻译目的是在西班牙南部未经选择的长期随访乙型肝炎开放 队列研究 中,确定有助于 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染患者乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 清除的因素。一项前瞻性开放 队列研究 研究于 1991 年至 2022 年间在马拉加(西班牙)两家医院内科肝脏病房招募了 453 名 CHB 感染患者,以评估随访期间乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)清除率的 发病率,确定与其相关的因素,并确定清除率对长期预后的影响。随访时间为0.5至36.08年。进行多变量 Cox 回归分析以确定与 HBsAg 清除相关的因素。使用联合预后终点评估清除率对长期预后的影响:需要肝移植、肝脏并发症或肝脏相关的 死亡率。采用Kaplan-Meir方法构建时间-事件曲线,随后通过对数秩检验对组间事件发生情况进行比较。在随后的随访期间,96/453名患者(21.2%)失去表面抗原(每年1.96/100名患者)。多变量 Cox 回归分析确定为与 HBsAg 清除率相关的独立因素:首次评估年龄超过 40 岁:HR 2.21 (1.38-3.52);基线 DNA HBV 峰值低于 3.3log10 (< 2000 IU/mL):HR 3.44 (2.01-5.89);随访期间乙型肝炎 e 抗原 (HBeAg) 丢失:HR 7.23 (3.87-13.50)。HBsAg 清除率的 发病率 为 1.96 例/100 患者年。这种情况在 40 岁以上、基线 DNA-HBV 低于 3.3 log10 (< 2000 IU/mL) 的患者以及随访期间 HBeAg 消失的慢性 HBeAg+ 患者中更为常见。HBsAg 清除率与更好的长期肝脏预后相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染患者乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 清除的影响因素,并确定其对长期预后的影响。
设计与对象
1991 年至 2022 年间,西班牙马拉加两家医院的肝脏病房对 453 名 CHB 感染患者进行了一项前瞻性开放 队列研究 研究。发病率 HBsAg 清除率为每 100 患者年 1.96 例。
核心方法
使用多变量 Cox 回归分析来确定与 HBsAg 清除率相关的因素。使用联合预后终点评估清除率对长期预后的影响:需要肝移植、肝脏并发症或肝脏相关的 死亡率。
主要结果
HBsAg 清除率与更好的长期肝脏预后相关。与清除相关的独立因素包括首次评估时年龄超过 40 岁、基线 HBV DNA 水平低于 3.3 log10 (< 2000 IU/mL) 以及随访期间乙型肝炎 e 抗原 (HBeAg) 丢失。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HBsAg 清除与 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的长期结果改善相关,强调了可能影响这种清除的特定人口和病毒学因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 监测 和 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的管理提供了信息,特别是在识别 HBsAg 清除风险较高的患者及其对长期预后的相关益处方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于样本量大和随访期长,证据有力。

Factors Associated With Surface Antigen (HBsAg) Clearance in a Spanish Cohort of Chronic Hepatitis B Patients: Influence on the Long-Term Prognosis.

Authors: Paula Gavilán, Juan-Carlos Gavilán, Alberto Piserra-Lopez
Original abstract (English)
The objective is to identify factors that contribute to hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) clearance in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection in an unselected, long-term follow-up hepatitis B open cohort in southern Spain. A prospective open cohort study of 453 patients with CHB infection was recruited in the Liver Unit of the Internal Department of two hospitals in Málaga (Spain) between 1991 and 2022 to assess the incidence of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) clearance during follow-up, identify factors related to it, and determine the influence that clearance had on the long-term prognosis. The duration of follow-up ranged from 0.5 to 36.08 years. A multivariate Cox regression analysis was conducted to identify factors associated with HBsAg clearance. The impact of clearance on long-term prognosis was evaluated using a combined prognostic end point: need for liver transplantation, liver complications, or liver-related mortality. The construction of time-to-event curves was achieved by employing the Kaplan-Meir method, with subsequent comparison of event occurrence between groups conducted via log-rank test. During the subsequent follow-up period, 96/453 patients (21.2%) lost surface antigen (1.96/100 patients per year). Multivariate Cox regression analysis identified as independent factors associated with HBsAg clearance: age at first assessment over 40 years: HR 2.21 (1.38-3.52); a baseline DNA HBV peak below 3.3log10 (< 2000 IU/mL): HR 3.44 (2.01-5.89); loss of hepatitis B e-antigen (HBeAg) during follow-up: HR 7.23 (3.87-13.50). The incidence of HBsAg clearance was 1.96 cases/100 patient-years. It was more frequent in patients older than 40 years, with baseline DNA-HBV below 3.3 log10 (< 2000 IU/mL), and in chronic HBeAg+ patients who lost HBeAg during follow-up. HBsAg clearance was associated with a better long-term hepatic prognosis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify factors contributing to hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) clearance in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection and to determine its influence on long-term prognosis.
Design and population
A prospective open cohort study of 453 patients with CHB infection was conducted in the Liver Unit of two hospitals in Málaga, Spain, between 1991 and 2022. The incidence of HBsAg clearance was 1.96 cases per 100 patient-years.
Core methods
A multivariate Cox regression analysis was used to identify factors associated with HBsAg clearance. The impact of clearance on long-term prognosis was evaluated using a combined prognostic endpoint: need for liver transplantation, liver complications, or liver-related mortality.
Main results
HBsAg clearance was associated with a better long-term hepatic prognosis. Independent factors associated with clearance included age at first assessment over 40 years, baseline HBV DNA levels below 3.3 log10 (< 2000 IU/mL), and loss of hepatitis B e-antigen (HBeAg) during follow-up.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HBsAg clearance is linked to improved long-term outcomes in chronic hepatitis B patients, highlighting specific demographic and virological factors that may influence this clearance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the surveillance and management of chronic hepatitis B patients, particularly in identifying those at higher risk for HBsAg clearance and its associated benefits for long-term prognosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and long follow-up period.
