免疫检查点抑制剂在 乙型肝炎病毒 阳性癌症患者中的安全性:多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究

Safety of Immune Checkpoint Inhibitors in Hepatitis B Virus-Positive Cancer Patients: A Multicenter Retrospective Cohort Study
作者： Meshail Baswaid, Alaa Shahbar, Afnan Noor, Danyah Ahmed Katlan, Abdulfattah Alhazmi, Mohammed Alnuhait, Aryaf Alsulami, Baker Saemaldaher, Hussam Magliah
摘要中文翻译背景:免疫检查点抑制剂(ICIs)广泛用于癌症治疗,但其在既往存在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染的患者中的安全性仍不确定。HBV 再激活是免疫抑制癌症治疗的公认并发症,但支持 ICI 治疗 期间常规 抗病毒药物 预防的证据有限。本研究评估了接受 ICI 的 HBV 阳性癌症患者的 HBV 再激活和 HBV 相关结果。方法:这项多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2017 年 1 月至 2022 年 12 月期间接受 ICI 治疗的成年患者。根据接受 抗病毒药物 预防治疗对患者进行分组。主要结局是 ICI 治疗期间 HBV 再激活。次要结局包括肝脏耀斑、严重程度和再激活时间、对 抗病毒药物 治疗的反应以及与 HBV 相关结局相关的因素。结果:共纳入160例患者;68 人接受了 抗病毒药物 预防,92 人没有接受。3 名患者 (1.9%) 发生 HBV 再激活,抗病毒药物 预防与 HBV 再激活之间的奇数比较宽且不精确 (2.76;95% CI 0.25–31.05)。16 例患者 (10.0%) 发生肝脏耀斑,预防组比未预防组更常见 (19.1% vs. 结论:在这项多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究中,HBV 再激活率较低,无法确定 抗病毒药物 预防是否降低了再激活风险。接受 抗病毒药物 治疗的患者中肝脏耀斑明显更常见预防,可能反映了该组中 HBV 相关并发症的基线风险较高,有必要进行一项未来的随机对照试验来指导风险适应的 抗病毒药物 预防和监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
免疫检查点抑制剂 (ICIs) 广泛用于癌症 治疗,但其在既往存在 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的患者中的安全性仍不确定。本研究评估了接受 ICI 的 HBV 阳性癌症患者的 HBV 再激活和 HBV 相关结果。
设计与对象
这项多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2017 年 1 月至 2022 年 12 月期间接受 ICI 治疗的成年患者。患者根据接受 抗病毒药物 预防治疗进行分组。
核心方法
主要结局是 ICI 治疗期间 HBV 再激活。次要结局包括肝脏耀斑、严重程度和再激活时间、对 抗病毒药物 治疗的反应以及与 HBV 相关结局相关的因素。
主要结果
总共纳入160名患者;68 人接受了 抗病毒药物 预防,92 人没有接受。3 名患者 (1.9%) 发生 HBV 再激活,抗病毒药物 预防与 HBV 再激活之间的奇数比较宽且不精确 (2.76;95% CI 0.25–31.05)。16 名患者 (10.0%) 发生肝耀斑,预防组比未预防组更常见(19.1% vs. 19.1%)。
解释与创新
在这项多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究中,HBV 再激活率较低,无法确定 抗病毒药物 预防是否降低了再激活风险。在接受 抗病毒药物 预防的患者中,肝脏耀斑更为常见,这可能反映了该组患者发生 HBV 相关并发症的基线风险较高。
科研与公卫意义
未来有必要进行随机对照试验来指导风险适应的 抗病毒药物 预防和监测。研究结果表明,对于所有接受 ICI 的 HBV 阳性癌症患者来说,常规 抗病毒药物 预防可能不是必需的。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和样本量较小而受到限制,这可能影响了结果的精确度。由于多中心设计,证据强度中等,但由于缺乏随机性和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制。

Safety of Immune Checkpoint Inhibitors in Hepatitis B Virus-Positive Cancer Patients: A Multicenter Retrospective Cohort Study

Authors: Meshail Baswaid, Alaa Shahbar, Afnan Noor, Danyah Ahmed Katlan, Abdulfattah Alhazmi, Mohammed Alnuhait, Aryaf Alsulami, Baker Saemaldaher, Hussam Magliah
Original abstract (English)
Background: Immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) are widely used in cancer treatment, but their safety in patients with pre-existing hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains uncertain. HBV reactivation is a recognized complication of immunosuppressive cancer therapy, yet evidence supporting routine antiviral prophylaxis during ICI treatment is limited. This study evaluated HBV reactivation and HBV-related outcomes among HBV-positive cancer patients receiving ICIs. Methods: This multicenter retrospective cohort study included adult patients treated with ICIs between January 2017 and December 2022. Patients were grouped according to receipt of antiviral prophylaxis. The primary outcome was HBV reactivation during ICI therapy. Secondary outcomes included hepatic flare, severity, and timing of reactivation, response to antiviral therapy, and factors associated with HBV-related outcomes. Results: A total of 160 patients were included; 68 received antiviral prophylaxis and 92 did not. HBV reactivation occurred in 3 patients (1.9%) and the odd ratio between antiviral prophylaxis and HBV reactivation was wide and imprecise (2.76; 95% CI 0.25–31.05). Hepatic flare occurred in 16 patients (10.0%) and was more frequent in the prophylaxis group than in the no-prophylaxis group (19.1% vs. Conclusion: In this multicenter retrospective cohort study, the HBV reactivation rate was low and could not determine whether antiviral prophylaxis reduced the risk of reactivation. Hepatic flares were significantly more common among patients who received antiviral prophylaxis, likely reflecting a higher baseline risk of HBV-related complications in this group. A future randomized controlled trial is warranted to guide risk-adapted antiviral prophylaxis and monitoring.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) are widely used in cancer treatment, but their safety in patients with pre-existing hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains uncertain. This study evaluated HBV reactivation and HBV-related outcomes among HBV-positive cancer patients receiving ICIs.
Design and population
This multicenter retrospective cohort study included adult patients treated with ICIs between January 2017 and December 2022. Patients were grouped according to receipt of antiviral prophylaxis.
Core methods
The primary outcome was HBV reactivation during ICI therapy. Secondary outcomes included hepatic flare, severity, and timing of reactivation, response to antiviral therapy, and factors associated with HBV-related outcomes.
Main results
A total of 160 patients were included; 68 received antiviral prophylaxis and 92 did not. HBV reactivation occurred in 3 patients (1.9%) and the odd ratio between antiviral prophylaxis and HBV reactivation was wide and imprecise (2.76; 95% CI 0.25–31.05). Hepatic flare occurred in 16 patients (10.0%) and was more frequent in the prophylaxis group than in the no-prophylaxis group (19.1% vs.
Interpretation and novelty
In this multicenter retrospective cohort study, the HBV reactivation rate was low and could not determine whether antiviral prophylaxis reduced the risk of reactivation. Hepatic flares were significantly more common among patients who received antiviral prophylaxis, likely reflecting a higher baseline risk of HBV-related complications in this group.
Scientific and public-health significance
A future randomized controlled trial is warranted to guide risk-adapted antiviral prophylaxis and monitoring. The findings suggest that routine antiviral prophylaxis may not be necessary for all HBV-positive cancer patients receiving ICIs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its retrospective design and small sample size, which may have affected the precision of the results. The evidence strength is moderate due to the multicenter design but limited by the lack of randomization and potential confounding factors.

非酒精性脂肪肝对 慢性乙型肝炎 患者聚乙二醇化干扰素 α 治疗反应的影响

Impact of nonalcoholic fatty liver disease on the response to pegylated interferon-α therapy in patients with chronic hepatitis B
作者： Huiqing Liang, Yizhi Xie, Yan Dai, Xiaorong Yang, Xiaoting Zheng, Jiaen Yang, Yaoyu Liu, Chuncheng Wu, Shaodong Chen, Manying Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查非酒精性脂肪性肝病(NAFLD)对聚乙二醇化干扰素-α(PegIFNα)对 慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者的 抗病毒药物 疗效的影响,并确定乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)清除的基线预测因子,解决 NAFLD 是否影响 PegIFNα 治疗 反应这一有争议的问题。
设计与对象
这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了 201 名接受 PegIFNα 单药治疗或与核苷(酸)类似物联合治疗的 CHB 患者,根据肝脂肪变性分为仅 CHB(n = 125)和 CHB + NAFLD(n = 76)组。
核心方法
比较基线临床、病毒学和组织学特征,并使用 Cox 比例风险回归模型进行单变量和多变量分析,评估累积 HBsAg 清除率、生化反应和病毒学反应率。
主要结果
CHB + NAFLD 组与单纯 CHB 组之间的 48 周累积 HBsAg 清除率(18.42% vs 23.2%,P = 0.41)和病毒学缓解率无显着差异,但 CHB + NAFLD 组的生化缓解率显着较低(13.79% vs 22.03%,P = 0.042)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,并发 NAFLD 不会显着损害接受基于 PegIFNα 治疗的慢性乙型肝炎患者的 HBsAg 清除率,但与生化反应率降低相关,基线 HBsAg 和 HBV DNA 水平可作为 HBsAg 清除率的独立预测因子。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 CHB 和 NAFLD 患者的治疗提供了重要的临床见解,表明 HBsAg 清除率主要取决于基线病毒水平而不是 NAFLD 的存在,尽管 NAFLD 可能会延迟生化标志物的正常化。
局限与证据强度
尽管该研究提供了有关临床相关人群的有价值的真实数据,但由于采用回顾性 队列研究 设计,证据强度中等,可能会引入混杂因素。

Impact of nonalcoholic fatty liver disease on the response to pegylated interferon-α therapy in patients with chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Huiqing Liang, Yizhi Xie, Yan Dai, Xiaorong Yang, Xiaoting Zheng, Jiaen Yang, Yaoyu Liu, Chuncheng Wu, Shaodong Chen, Manying Zhang
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) clearance is regarded as the optimal therapeutic endpoint in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). Pegylated interferon-α (PegIFNα) is currently one of the most effective strategies for achieving this goal. However, the impact of concurrent nonalcoholic fatty liver disease (NAFLD) on the PegIFNα treatment response in CHB patients remains controversial. The aim of this study was to investigate the impact of NAFLD on the antiviral efficacy of PegIFNα in patients with CHB and identify baseline predictors of HBsAg clearance. In this retrospective cohort study, 201 CHB patients receiving PegIFNα monotherapy or PegIFNα in combination with nucleos(t)ide analogs were enrolled and stratified into CHB-only ( n = 125) and CHB + NAFLD ( n = 76) groups based on the presence of hepatic steatosis. Baseline clinical, virological (including HBsAg levels), and histological characteristics were collected and compared. Cumulative HBsAg clearance, biochemical response, and virological response rates were assessed. Two Cox proportional hazards regression models were constructed to identify independent predictors of HBsAg clearance through univariable and multivariable analyses. No significant difference was observed in the 48-week cumulative HBsAg clearance rate between the CHB + NAFLD and CHB-only groups (18.42% vs 23.2%, P = 0.41), nor in the virological response rates. However, the biochemical response rate was significantly lower in the CHB + NAFLD group (13.79% vs 22.03%, P = 0.042). Multivariate Cox regression analysis identified baseline HBsAg levels (Model 2: HR = 0.36, 95% CI: 0.26–0.51, P P = 0.049) as independent predictors of HBsAg clearance, while NAFLD comorbidity was not independently associated with HBsAg clearance ( P = 0.58). In CHB patients receiving PegIFNα-based therapy, concurrent NAFLD did not significantly impair HBsAg clearance rates (18.42% vs 23.2% in week 48). However, it was associated with a reduced biochemical response rate. Baseline levels of HBsAg and HBV DNA served as independent predictors of HBsAg clearance. IMPORTANCE Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and nonalcoholic fatty liver disease (NAFLD) are two of the most common liver diseases worldwide, and their coexistence is increasingly frequent due to the rising prevalence of metabolic disorders. Achieving hepatitis B surface antigen clearance is a key therapeutic goal, and pegylated interferon-α (PegIFNα) remains one of the most effective treatments to achieve it. However, it remains unclear whether the presence of NAFLD influences the response to PegIFNα therapy. The study found that NAFLD does not impair the clearance or suppression of hepatitis B virus, but it is associated with delayed normalization of biochemical markers. Whether the hepatitis B surface antigen becomes negative depends mainly on baseline viral levels, rather than the presence of NAFLD. These findings provide important clinical insights for the management of patients with both CHB and NAFLD.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the impact of nonalcoholic fatty liver disease (NAFLD) on the antiviral efficacy of pegylated interferon-α (PegIFNα) in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and identify baseline predictors of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) clearance, addressing the controversial question of whether NAFLD affects PegIFNα treatment response.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study enrolled 201 CHB patients receiving PegIFNα monotherapy or in combination with nucleos(t)ide analogs, stratified into CHB-only (n = 125) and CHB + NAFLD (n = 76) groups based on hepatic steatosis presence.
Core methods
Baseline clinical, virological, and histological characteristics were compared, and cumulative HBsAg clearance, biochemical response, and virological response rates were assessed using Cox proportional hazards regression models for univariable and multivariable analyses.
Main results
No significant difference was observed in the 48-week cumulative HBsAg clearance rate between the CHB + NAFLD and CHB-only groups (18.42% vs 23.2%, P = 0.41), nor in virological response rates, but the biochemical response rate was significantly lower in the CHB + NAFLD group (13.79% vs 22.03%, P = 0.042).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that concurrent NAFLD does not significantly impair HBsAg clearance rates in CHB patients receiving PegIFNα-based therapy but is associated with reduced biochemical response rates, with baseline HBsAg and HBV DNA levels serving as independent predictors of HBsAg clearance.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide important clinical insights for the management of patients with both CHB and NAFLD, indicating that HBsAg clearance depends mainly on baseline viral levels rather than NAFLD presence, though NAFLD may delay biochemical marker normalization.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the retrospective cohort design, which may introduce confounding factors, though the study provides valuable real-world data on a clinically relevant population.

HBV RNA 动态和细胞因子驱动的过度炎症:乙型肝炎相关慢加急性肝衰竭的协同预后轴

HBV RNA dynamics and cytokine-driven hyperinflammation: a synergistic prognostic axis in hepatitis B-related acute-on-chronic liver failure
作者： Keli Qian, Ying Xue, Xiaofeng Shi
摘要中文翻译背景和目的 在乙型肝炎相关的慢加急性肝衰竭 (HBV-ACLF) 中,乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 复制和 宿主 免疫之间的相互作用仍不清楚。本研究旨在探讨血清 HBV RNA 与细胞因子驱动的过度炎症之间的动态拮抗作用,并评估其综合预后价值。方法 在这项前瞻性 队列研究 研究(2021 年 3 月至 2022 年 12 月)中,纳入了 133 名 HBV-ACLF 患者。测量 HBV RNA 和 Th1/Th2 细胞因子(IFN-γ、TNF-α、IL-4、IL-6、IL-10)的血清水平。Logistic 回归分析用于确定 12 周预后的独立预测因素,并构建和内部验证预后模型。结果 与幸存者相比,非幸存者 (40.6%,54/133) 的 HBV RNA 水平显着降低(2.97 ± 1.22 vs. 4.05 ± 1.30 log 10 拷贝/mL,p < 0.001),但 IFN-γ 水平高出 1.2 倍(p < 0.001)。在 HBV RNA 和 IFN-γ 之间观察到强烈的负相关(r = -0.326,p < 0.001),表明病毒复制受到抑制可能引发 Th1 主导的高炎症状态。多变量逻辑回归确定 HBV RNA(AUC = 0.730,最佳截止值 = 3.98 log 10 拷贝/mL)和 IFN-γ(AUC = 0.821,最佳截止值 = 496.23 pg/mL)作为 12 周 死亡率 的独立预测因子。包含这些因素的列线图表现出卓越的预测性能 (AUC = 0.917),优于 MELD 评分,并达到与 COSSH-ACLF II 评分相当的准确性。结论 HBV RNA 和 Th1 细胞因子形成 HBV-ACLF 的动态预后轴。HBV RNA 的下降表明病毒已得到控制,但矛盾的是,它预示着与器官衰竭相关的细胞因子驱动的高炎症状态。这种病毒与免疫的相互作用提供了一种新颖的分层工具来指导早期免疫调节治疗,挑战了传统上仅关注病毒载量的做法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在乙型肝炎相关的慢加急性肝衰竭 (HBV-ACLF) 中,乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 复制和 宿主 免疫之间的相互作用仍不清楚。本研究旨在探讨血清 HBV RNA 与细胞因子驱动的过度炎症之间的动态拮抗作用,并评估其综合预后价值。
设计与对象
在这项前瞻性 队列研究 研究(2021 年 3 月至 2022 年 12 月)中,纳入了 133 名 HBV-ACLF 患者。
核心方法
测量 HBV RNA 和 Th1/Th2 细胞因子(IFN-γ、TNF-α、IL-4、IL-6、IL-10)的血清水平。Logistic 回归分析用于确定 12 周预后的独立预测因素,并构建和内部验证预后模型。
主要结果
与幸存者相比,非幸存者 (40.6%,54/133) 的 HBV RNA 水平显着降低(2.97 ± 1.22 vs. 4.05 ± 1.30 log 10 拷贝/mL,p < 0.001),但 IFN-γ 水平高出 1.2 倍(p < 0.001)。HBV RNA 和 IFN-γ 之间观察到强烈的负相关(r = -0.326,p < 0.001)。多变量逻辑回归确定 HBV RNA(AUC = 0.730,最佳截止值 = 3.98 log 10 拷贝/mL)和 IFN-γ(AUC = 0.821,最佳截止值 = 496.23 pg/mL)作为 12 周 死亡率 的独立预测因子。包含这些因素的列线图表现出卓越的预测性能 (AUC = 0.917),优于 MELD 评分,并达到与 COSSH-ACLF II 评分相当的准确性。
解释与创新
HBV RNA 和 Th1 细胞因子形成 HBV-ACLF 的动态预后轴。HBV RNA 的下降表明病毒已得到控制,但矛盾的是,它预示着与器官衰竭相关的细胞因子驱动的高炎症状态。这种病毒与免疫的相互作用提供了一种新颖的分层工具来指导早期免疫调节治疗,挑战了传统上仅关注病毒载量的做法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 HBV-ACLF 提供了一个新的预后轴,可以指导早期免疫调节治疗,有可能改善患者分层和超越传统病毒载量监测的 治疗 决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究是作为单中心前瞻性 队列研究 进行的,样本量有限(n=133),这可能会影响普遍性。由于前瞻性设计,证据强度中等,但需要在更大的多中心 队列研究 中进行验证。

HBV RNA dynamics and cytokine-driven hyperinflammation: a synergistic prognostic axis in hepatitis B-related acute-on-chronic liver failure

Authors: Keli Qian, Ying Xue, Xiaofeng Shi
Original abstract (English)
Background and aims The interplay between hepatitis B virus (HBV) replication and host immunity remains poorly defined in hepatitis B-related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF). This study aimed to investigate the dynamic antagonism between serum HBV RNA and cytokine-driven hyperinflammation, and to evaluate their combined prognostic value. Methods In this prospective cohort study (March 2021–December 2022), 133 patients with HBV-ACLF were enrolled. Serum levels of HBV RNA and Th1/Th2 cytokines (IFN-γ, TNF-α, IL-4, IL-6, IL-10) were measured. Logistic regression analysis was used to identify independent predictors of 12-week prognosis, and to construct and internally validate prognostic models. Results Non-survivors (40.6%, 54/133) exhibited significantly lower HBV RNA levels (2.97 ± 1.22 vs. 4.05 ± 1.30 log 10 copies/mL, p < 0.001) but 1.2-fold higher IFN-γ levels ( p < 0.001) compared to survivors. A strong inverse correlation was observed between HBV RNA and IFN-γ (r = -0.326, p < 0.001), suggesting that suppressed viral replication may trigger a Th1-dominant hyperinflammatory state. Multivariate logistic regression identified HBV RNA (AUC = 0.730, optimal cutoff = 3.98 log 10 copies/mL) and IFN-γ (AUC = 0.821, optimal cutoff = 496.23 pg/mL) as independent predictors of 12-week mortality. A nomogram incorporating these factors demonstrated superior predictive performance (AUC = 0.917), outperforming the MELD score and achieving comparable accuracy to the COSSH-ACLF II score. Conclusions HBV RNA and Th1 cytokines form a dynamic prognostic axis in HBV-ACLF. Declining HBV RNA, indicative of viral control, paradoxically heralds a cytokine-driven hyperinflammatory state associated with organ failure. This virus-immunity interplay provides a novel stratification tool to guide early immunomodulatory therapy, challenging the traditional focus on viral load alone.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The interplay between hepatitis B virus (HBV) replication and host immunity remains poorly defined in hepatitis B-related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF). This study aimed to investigate the dynamic antagonism between serum HBV RNA and cytokine-driven hyperinflammation, and to evaluate their combined prognostic value.
Design and population
In this prospective cohort study (March 2021–December 2022), 133 patients with HBV-ACLF were enrolled.
Core methods
Serum levels of HBV RNA and Th1/Th2 cytokines (IFN-γ, TNF-α, IL-4, IL-6, IL-10) were measured. Logistic regression analysis was used to identify independent predictors of 12-week prognosis, and to construct and internally validate prognostic models.

Non-survivors (40.6%, 54/133) exhibited significantly lower HBV RNA levels (2.97 ± 1.22 vs. 4.05 ± 1.30 log 10 copies/mL, p < 0.001) but 1.2-fold higher IFN-γ levels (p < 0.001) compared to survivors. A strong inverse correlation was observed between HBV RNA and IFN-γ (r = -0.326, p < 0.001). Multivariate logistic regression identified HBV RNA (AUC = 0.730, optimal cutoff = 3.98 log 10 copies/mL) and IFN-γ (AUC = 0.821, optimal cutoff = 496.23 pg/mL) as independent predictors of 12-week mortality. A nomogram incorporating these factors demonstrated superior predictive performance (AUC = 0.917), outperforming the MELD score and achieving comparable accuracy to the COSSH-ACLF II score.

Declining HBV RNA, indicative of viral control, paradoxically heralds a cytokine-driven hyperinflammatory state associated with organ failure. This virus-immunity interplay provides a novel stratification tool to guide early immunomodulatory therapy, challenging the traditional focus on viral load alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a novel prognostic axis for HBV-ACLF that can guide early immunomodulatory therapy, potentially improving patient stratification and treatment decisions beyond traditional viral load monitoring.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted as a single-center prospective cohort with a limited sample size (n=133), which may affect generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the prospective design but requires validation in larger, multicenter cohorts.

越南南部艾滋病毒感染者隐匿性乙型肝炎感染的高负担:来自血清学和分子分析的见解

High Burden of Occult Hepatitis B Infection in HIV-Infected Patients in Southern Vietnam: Insights from Serological and Molecular Analysis
作者： Huynh Hoang Khanh Thu, Yu. V. Ostankova, А. N. Shchemelev, Е. Н. Серикова, V. S. Davydenko, Nadezhda A. Pechnikova, Ton Tran, Truong Thi Xuan Lien, Edward S. Ramsay, Арег А. Тотолян
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是艾滋病毒感染者 (PLWH) 的一个主要问题,特别是在越南等高流行地区。该研究评估了越南南部接受抗逆转录病毒治疗(ART)的感染者中乙肝病毒的血清学和分子特征。对 316 名接受 ART 的 HIV 感染患者进行了一项横断面研究。使用高灵敏度测定法评估血清学标志物(HBsAg、抗 HBs IgG 和抗 HBc IgG)和 HBV DNA,并对 Pre-S1/Pre-S2/S 区域进行测序以进行基因型和 突变 分析。使用适当的统计方法评估关联性。32.6% 的患者检测到 HBV DNA,而仅 16.1% 的患者检测到 HBsAg。最常见的血清学特征是易感性(36.4%),其次是隐匿性 HBV 感染(OBI)(17.4%),其中 6.6% 的 OBI 完全血清阴性,以及慢性 HBV(CHB)(15.8%)。男性和老年人患 CHB 的风险显着较高(调整后的比值比 [aOR] = 2.58 和 aOR = 1.87,分别)。总体而言,基因型 B 占主导地位 (78.4%),但基因型 C 在 OBI 中比在 CHB 患者中出现的频率显着更高(31.5% vs.)。此外,表面 S 基因逃逸 突变 的负担在 OBI 组中显着更高 (p = 0.023)。LASSO 模型在预测 OBI 状态方面显示出中等的辨别能力(接受者操作特征 [ROC] 曲线下面积 [AUC]) = 0.76) 拉米夫定相关耐药 (rtM204I/V) 是越南南部 RT/S 重叠区域中最常见的 RT 突变,HIV 感染者同时面临 CHB 和 OBI,其中血清阴性 OBI 的比例很高。血清学检测与分子诊断,以改善高危人群中的乙型肝炎病毒检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是艾滋病毒感染者 (PLWH) 的一个主要问题,特别是在越南等高流行地区。该研究评估了越南南部接受抗逆转录病毒治疗(ART)的感染者中乙肝病毒的血清学和分子特征。
设计与对象
对越南南部 316 名接受 ART 的 HIV 感染患者进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
使用高灵敏度测定法评估血清学标志物(HBsAg、抗 HBs IgG 和抗 HBc IgG)和 HBV DNA,并对 Pre-S1/Pre-S2/S 区域进行测序以进行基因型和 突变 分析。使用适当的统计方法评估关联性。
主要结果
32.6% 的患者检测到 HBV DNA,而仅 16.1% 的患者检测到 HBsAg。最常见的血清学特征是易感性(36.4%),其次是隐匿性 HBV 感染(OBI)(17.4%),其中 6.6% 的 OBI 完全血清阴性,以及慢性 HBV(CHB)(15.8%)。男性和老年人患 CHB 的风险显着较高(调整后的比值比 [aOR] = 2.58 和 aOR = 1.87,分别)。
解释与创新
血清阴性 OBI 的存在进一步增加了仅依靠标准血清学标志物时诊断不足的风险,并反映出需要将高灵敏度血清学检测与分子诊断相结合的综合策略,以改善高危人群中的 HBV 检测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了越南南部 HIV 感染者隐匿性 HBV 感染的高负担,强调需要改进诊断策略以预防 HBV 相关并发症和 传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了因果推断,但使用高度敏感的测定和分子分析为结果提供了适度的可信度。

High Burden of Occult Hepatitis B Infection in HIV-Infected Patients in Southern Vietnam: Insights from Serological and Molecular Analysis

Authors: Huynh Hoang Khanh Thu, Yu. V. Ostankova, А. N. Shchemelev, Е. Н. Серикова, V. S. Davydenko, Nadezhda A. Pechnikova, Ton Tran, Truong Thi Xuan Lien, Edward S. Ramsay, Арег А. Тотолян
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major concern among people living with HIV (PLWH), particularly in high-endemic settings such as Vietnam. The study evaluated the serological and molecular characteristics of HBV among PLWH receiving antiretroviral therapy (ART) in southern Vietnam. A cross-sectional study was conducted among 316 HIV-infected patients receiving ART. Serological markers (HBsAg, anti-HBs IgG, and anti-HBc IgG) and HBV DNA were assessed using highly sensitive assays, and the Pre-S1/Pre-S2/S regions were sequenced for genotype and mutation analysis. Associations were evaluated using appropriate statistical methods. HBV DNA was detected in 32.6% of the patients, whereas HBsAg was present in only 16.1%. The most common serological profile was susceptibility (36.4%), followed by occult HBV infection (OBI) (17.4%), including 6.6% with completely seronegative OBI, and chronic HBV (CHB) (15.8%). Males and older individuals showed a significantly higher risk of CHB (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 2.58 and aOR = 1.87, respectively). Genotype B predominated (78.4%) overall, but genotype C was significantly more frequent in OBI than in CHB patients (31.5% vs. Moreover, the burden of surface S gene escape mutations was significantly higher in the OBI group (p = 0.023). The LASSO model showed a moderate discriminatory ability for predicting OBI status (Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic [ROC] Curve [AUC] = 0.76). Lamivudine-associated resistance (rtM204I/V) was the most common RT mutation detected in the overlapping RT/S region. HIV-infected individuals in southern Vietnam face a burden of both CHB and OBI, including a high proportion of seronegative OBI. The presence of seronegative OBI further supports the risk of underdiagnosis when relying only on standard serological markers, and reflects the need for integrated strategies combining highly sensitive serological assays with molecular diagnostics to improve HBV detection among high-risk populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major concern among people living with HIV (PLWH), particularly in high-endemic settings such as Vietnam. The study evaluated the serological and molecular characteristics of HBV among PLWH receiving antiretroviral therapy (ART) in southern Vietnam.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 316 HIV-infected patients receiving ART in southern Vietnam.
Core methods
Serological markers (HBsAg, anti-HBs IgG, and anti-HBc IgG) and HBV DNA were assessed using highly sensitive assays, and the Pre-S1/Pre-S2/S regions were sequenced for genotype and mutation analysis. Associations were evaluated using appropriate statistical methods.
Main results
HBV DNA was detected in 32.6% of the patients, whereas HBsAg was present in only 16.1%. The most common serological profile was susceptibility (36.4%), followed by occult HBV infection (OBI) (17.4%), including 6.6% with completely seronegative OBI, and chronic HBV (CHB) (15.8%). Males and older individuals showed a significantly higher risk of CHB (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 2.58 and aOR = 1.87, respectively).

The presence of seronegative OBI further supports the risk of underdiagnosis when relying only on standard serological markers, and reflects the need for integrated strategies combining highly sensitive serological assays with molecular diagnostics to improve HBV detection among high-risk populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the high burden of occult HBV infection in HIV-infected patients in southern Vietnam, emphasizing the need for improved diagnostic strategies to prevent HBV-related complications and transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was cross-sectional, limiting causal inference, but the use of highly sensitive assays and molecular analysis provides moderate confidence in the results.

中国南宁市孕妇乙型肝炎病毒感染血清流行病学特征及影响因素(2024-2025年):横断面研究

Seroepidemiological Characteristics and Influencing Factors of Hepatitis B Virus Infection Among Pregnant Women in Nanning City, China (2024–2025): A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Chang Huang, Tao Lan, Bin Xu, Qiuyun Deng, Chi Tang, Huang Chang, Deng QiuYun
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是孕妇的主要公共卫生问题,其流行特征的地区差异阻碍了母婴传播的精准预防。本研究旨在了解南宁市孕妇乙型肝炎病毒感染的血清流行病学特征,并确定其相关影响因素,为优化阻断乙型肝炎母婴传播的针对性干预措施提供循证见解。采用分层整群随机抽样策略,招募2024年10月至2025年5月在南宁市定点医疗机构接受常规产前保健的孕妇,采用标准化面对面调查问卷,收集人口学、行为和疾病相关数据。采集静脉血样本,通过化学发光免疫分析法定量检测 5 种血清学 HBV 标志物。采用Kruskal-Wallis检验比较乙型肝炎表面抗体(HBsAb)的几何平均浓度(GMC),采用卡方检验分析5种HBV标志物血清阳性率的组间差异。构建多变量非条件logistic回归模型来探索与乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)、HBsAb和乙型肝炎核心抗体(HBcAb)血清阳性独立相关的因素。共有 1,935 名符合条件的孕妇入组。HBsAg、HBsAb、乙型肝炎e抗原(HBeAg)、戊型肝炎抗体(HBeAb)和HBcAb总体血清阳性率分别为6.98%、59.48%、1.50%、15.50%和33.02%。HBsAg、HBsAb、HBeAb 和 HBcAb 的血清阳性率呈现出年龄依赖性上升趋势,在 14-19 岁年龄组中观察到最低阳性率,在 40-49 岁年龄组中观察到最高阳性率。与城市居民相比,农村居民的 HBsAg 和 HBeAb 血清阳性率显着较高,但 HBsAb 血清阳性率较低。与过去 5 年内或 ≥ 5 年前接种过疫苗的参与者相比,既往未知或无 HBV 疫苗接种 病史的参与者表现出 HBsAg、HBeAg、HBeAb 和 HBcAb 血清阳性率明显较高,同时 HBsAb 阳性率降低(所有 P < 0.001)。此外,与 5 年内接种疫苗的个体相比,≥5 年前接种疫苗的个体表现出较高的 HBV 标志物血清阳性率和较低的 HBsAb 阳性率(所有 P < 0.001)。多因素Logistic回归显示,高龄产妇、农村居民、合并慢性基础疾病以及与HBV携带者/患者密切接触是HBsAg血清阳性的独立危险因素,而既往HBV 疫苗接种则发挥保护作用。高龄、较高的教育程度和 HBV 疫苗接种 病史独立预测 HBsAb 阳性几率较高;农村居住、慢性合并症和非医疗职业状况是 HBsAb 血清转化不足的危险因素。对于HBcAb血清阳性,高龄、慢性基础疾病和接触HBV感染者是独立危险因素,而高等教育和疫苗接种病史是保护因素。南宁市孕妇乙型肝炎病毒患病率超过全国平均水平,城乡差异突出。高龄产妇和慢性合并症的存在增加了 HBV 感染的脆弱性,而育龄妇女中的 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍然不理想,保护性抗体滴度随着时间的推移逐渐减弱。我们建议针对 HBsAb 水平不足的育龄妇女使用靶向加强型 HBV 传播,以遏制 HBV 感染事件并降低垂直 传播 的风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染对孕妇构成持续的公共卫生挑战,流行模式的地区差异使预防母婴 传播 (MTCT) 的工作变得更加复杂。该研究解决了有关该人群乙型肝炎病毒感染的血清流行病学特征和影响因素的知识差距,为有针对性的干预措施提供信息。
设计与对象
对2024年10月至2025年5月在南宁市定点医疗机构接受常规产前保健的孕妇进行横断面研究。采用分层整群随机抽样策略,共纳入1,935名符合条件的受试者。
核心方法
标准化面对面调查问卷收集人口、行为和疾病相关数据。使用化学发光免疫测定法分析静脉血样本中的五种 HBV 血清学标志物(HBsAg、HBsAb、HBeAg、HBeAb、HBcAb)。多变量无条件逻辑回归模型确定了与 HBsAg、HBsAb 和 HBcAb 血清阳性独立相关的因素。
主要结果
总体血清阳性率为 HBsAg 6.98%、HBsAb 59.48%、HBeAg 1.50%、HBeAb 15.50% 和 HBcAb 33.02%。HBsAg、HBsAb、HBeAb 和 HBcAb 的血清阳性率随着年龄的增长而增加,在 40-49 岁年龄组达到峰值。农村居民的HBsAg和HBeAb血清阳性率高于城市居民,但HBsAb血清阳性率低于城市居民。
解释与创新
该研究确定高龄产妇、农村居民、慢性合并症以及与 HBV 携带者的密切接触是 HBsAg 血清阳性的独立危险因素,而既往 HBV 疫苗接种 具有保护作用。它强调了育龄妇女中 疫苗接种 覆盖率不理想,并且抗体滴度随着时间的推移而下降。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,南宁市需要有针对性的 HBV 传播 策略,包括对 HBsAb 水平不足的女性进行加强剂量,以减少感染事件并减轻垂直 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
作为一项横断面研究,因果推断是有限的,尽管进行了多变量调整,残留的混杂因素可能仍然存在。由于依赖血清学标记而没有确凿的病毒学或纵向数据,因此证据强度中等。

Seroepidemiological Characteristics and Influencing Factors of Hepatitis B Virus Infection Among Pregnant Women in Nanning City, China (2024–2025): A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Chang Huang, Tao Lan, Bin Xu, Qiuyun Deng, Chi Tang, Huang Chang, Deng QiuYun
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health concern among pregnant women, with regional disparities in epidemic characteristics that hinder precise prevention of mother-to-child transmission (MTCT). This study aimed to characterize the seroepidemiological profiles of HBV infection and identify its associated influencing factors among pregnant women in Nanning City, providing evidence-based insights for optimizing targeted interventions to block HBV MTCT. A stratified cluster random sampling strategy was adopted to recruit pregnant women receiving routine antenatal care at designated medical institutions across Nanning between October 2024 and May 2025. Standardized face-to-face questionnaires were administered to collect demographic, behavioral, and disease-related data. Venous blood samples were collected for quantitative detection of five serological HBV markers via chemiluminescence immunoassay. The Kruskal–Wallis test was used to compare the geometric mean concentration (GMC) of hepatitis B surface antibody (HBsAb), while the chi-square test was applied to analyze intergroup differences in the seropositivity rates of the five HBV markers. Multivariate unconditional logistic regression models were constructed to explore factors independently associated with seropositivity of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), HBsAb, and hepatitis B core antibody (HBcAb). A total of 1,935 eligible pregnant women were enrolled. The overall seropositivity rates of HBsAg, HBsAb, hepatitis B e-antigen (HBeAg), hepatitis e-antibody (HBeAb), and HBcAb were 6.98%, 59.48%, 1.50%, 15.50%, and 33.02%, respectively. Seropositivity of HBsAg, HBsAb, HBeAb, and HBcAb exhibited an age-dependent ascending trend, with the lowest rates observed in the 14–19 age group and peak rates in the 40–49 age group. Rural residents presented significantly higher HBsAg and HBeAb seropositivity but lower HBsAb seropositivity compared with urban counterparts. Participants with unknown or no prior HBV vaccination history showed markedly higher seropositivity of HBsAg, HBeAg, HBeAb, and HBcAb, alongside reduced HBsAb positivity, relative to those vaccinated within the past 5 years or ≥5 years prior (all P < 0.001). Furthermore, individuals vaccinated ≥5 years ago demonstrated higher HBV marker seropositivity and lower HBsAb positivity than those vaccinated within 5 years (all P < 0.001). Multivariate logistic regression revealed that advanced maternal age, rural residency, comorbid chronic underlying diseases, and close contact with HBV carriers/patients were independent risk factors for HBsAg seropositivity, whereas prior HBV vaccination exerted a protective effect. Advanced age, higher educational attainment, and HBV vaccination history independently predicted higher odds of HBsAb positivity; rural residency, chronic comorbidities, and non-medical occupational status were risk factors for insufficient HBsAb seroconversion. For HBcAb seropositivity, advanced age, chronic underlying diseases, and exposure to HBV-infected individuals were independent risk factors, while higher education and vaccination history served as protective factors. HBV prevalence among pregnant women in Nanning exceeds the national average, accompanied by prominent urban–rural disparities. Advanced maternal age and the presence of chronic comorbidities increase HBV infection vulnerability, while vaccination coverage among childbearing-age women remains suboptimal with progressive attenuation of protective antibody titers over time. We recommend targeted booster HBV vaccination for childbearing-age women with insufficient HBsAb levels to curb incident HBV infections and mitigate the risk of vertical transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection poses a persistent public health challenge among pregnant women, with regional disparities in epidemic patterns complicating efforts to prevent mother-to-child transmission (MTCT). The study addressed a knowledge gap regarding the seroepidemiological characteristics and influencing factors of HBV infection in this population to inform targeted interventions.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among pregnant women receiving routine antenatal care at designated medical institutions in Nanning City between October 2024 and May 2025. A total of 1,935 eligible participants were enrolled using a stratified cluster random sampling strategy.
Core methods
Standardized face-to-face questionnaires collected demographic, behavioral, and disease-related data. Venous blood samples were analyzed for five HBV serological markers (HBsAg, HBsAb, HBeAg, HBeAb, HBcAb) using chemiluminescence immunoassay. Multivariate unconditional logistic regression models identified factors independently associated with seropositivity of HBsAg, HBsAb, and HBcAb.
Main results
The overall seropositivity rates were 6.98% for HBsAg, 59.48% for HBsAb, 1.50% for HBeAg, 15.50% for HBeAb, and 33.02% for HBcAb. Seropositivity of HBsAg, HBsAb, HBeAb, and HBcAb increased with age, peaking in the 40–49 age group. Rural residents had higher HBsAg and HBeAb seropositivity but lower HBsAb seropositivity than urban residents.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified advanced maternal age, rural residency, chronic comorbidities, and close contact with HBV carriers as independent risk factors for HBsAg seropositivity, while prior HBV vaccination was protective. It highlighted suboptimal vaccination coverage and waning antibody titers over time among childbearing-age women.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for targeted HBV vaccination strategies, including booster doses for women with insufficient HBsAb levels, to reduce incident infections and mitigate vertical transmission risk in Nanning City.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a cross-sectional study, causal inference is limited, and residual confounding may persist despite multivariate adjustment. The evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on serological markers without confirmatory virological or longitudinal data.

新型 HBx 相互作用的 宿主 细胞因子调节 乙型肝炎病毒 X 蛋白 (HBx) 的动态核和细胞质定位

Novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors regulate dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of Hepatitis B virus X protein (HBx)
作者： Yasuo Ariumi
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) X 蛋白 (HBx) 在病毒转录、复制和 HBV 相关肝癌发生中发挥关键作用。鉴定了几种新型 HBx 相互作用 宿主 细胞因子:DDX1、DDX3、MOV10 RNA 解旋酶、INI1/hSNF5 染色质重塑因子、Rad18 DNA 修复酶和 CPSF6 裂解因子。DDX1、DDX3 和 MOV10 与 HBx 共定位于细胞质灶或聚集体中。相反,INI1/hSNF5 和 CPSF6 与 HBx 一起重新定位于核斑点、染色质间颗粒簇 (IGC) 和/或 PML 核体中。Rad18 将 HBx 隔离在核仁中。重要的是,DDX3、MOV10 和 INI1/hSNF5 限制 HBV 复制,而 CPSF6 促进 HBV 复制。此外,DDX1 增强 HBx 介导的 NF-κB 转录,而 MOV10 和 Rad18 抑制它。总之,HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子决定 HBx 的动态核和细胞质定位,并调节 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在鉴定与 HBV X 蛋白 (HBx) 相互作用的 宿主 细胞因子,并了解它们在调节 HBx 定位和病毒复制中的作用。乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) X 蛋白 (HBx) 在病毒转录、复制和 HBV 相关肝癌发生中发挥关键作用。
设计与对象
该研究是在实验室环境中使用人类乙型肝炎病毒感染模型进行的。它涉及通过生化和细胞测定来鉴定与 HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子。
核心方法
研究人员使用生化和细胞测定来识别 HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子,包括细胞质灶或聚集体和核斑点、染色质间颗粒簇 (IGC) 和/或 PML 核体的共定位研究。他们还评估了这些因素对 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录的影响。
主要结果
DDX1、DDX3 和 MOV10 与 HBx 共定位于细胞质灶或聚集体中。INI1/hSNF5 和 CPSF6 与 HBx 一起重新定位于核斑点、IGC 和/或 PML 核体中。Rad18 将 HBx 隔离在核仁中。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子决定 HBx 的动态核和细胞质定位,并调节 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录。这项研究确定了几种与 HBx 相互作用并对病毒过程具有独特影响的新因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 HBV 复制和 发病机制 的分子机制的见解,这可以为靶向治疗的开发提供信息并提高对 HBV 相关肝癌发生的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,不提供详细的统计分析或实验对照。由于缺乏有关这些因素体内功能影响的全面数据,证据强度中等。

Novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors regulate dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of Hepatitis B virus X protein (HBx)

Authors: Yasuo Ariumi
Original abstract (English)
The hepatitis B virus (HBV) X protein (HBx) is a key player in viral transcription, replication, and HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis. Several novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors: DDX1, DDX3, MOV10 RNA helicases, INI1/hSNF5 chromatin-remodeling factor, Rad18 DNA repair enzyme, and CPSF6 cleavage factor were identified. DDX1, DDX3, and MOV10 colocalized with HBx in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates. In contrast, INI1/hSNF5 and CPSF6 relocalized with HBx in nuclear speckles, interchromatin granule clusters (IGCs), and/or PML-nuclear bodies. Rad18 sequestered HBx in the nucleoli. Importantly, DDX3, MOV10, and INI1/hSNF5 restricted HBV replication, whereas CPSF6 facilitated HBV replication. Furthermore, DDX1 enhanced HBx-mediated NF-κB transcription, whereas MOV10 and Rad18 inhibited it. Altogether, HBx-interacting host factors determine dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of HBx and regulate HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify host cellular factors that interact with HBV X protein (HBx) and understand their role in regulating HBx localization and viral replication. The hepatitis B virus (HBV) X protein (HBx) is a key player in viral transcription, replication, and HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a laboratory setting using human HBV infection models. It involved the identification of host factors interacting with HBx through biochemical and cellular assays.
Core methods
The researchers used biochemical and cellular assays to identify HBx-interacting host factors, including colocalization studies in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates and nuclear speckles, interchromatin granule clusters (IGCs), and/or PML-nuclear bodies. They also assessed the impact of these factors on HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription.
Main results
DDX1, DDX3, and MOV10 colocalized with HBx in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates. INI1/hSNF5 and CPSF6 relocalized with HBx in nuclear speckles, IGCs, and/or PML-nuclear bodies. Rad18 sequestered HBx in the nucleoli.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HBx-interacting host factors determine the dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of HBx and regulate HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription. This study identified several novel factors that interact with HBx and have distinct effects on viral processes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of HBV replication and pathogenesis, which could inform the development of targeted therapies and improve understanding of HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence and does not provide detailed statistical analyses or experimental controls. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of comprehensive data on the functional impact of these factors in vivo.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯治疗的晚期肝纤维化/代偿性肝硬化患者出现低水平病毒血症的危险因素

Risk factors for low-level viremia in tenofovir disoproxil fumarate-treated chronic hepatitis B patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis
作者： Run-Ling Zhang, Hai-Ying Zhang, Yu-Yuan Jia, Zhen-Yu Wang, Feng Liu, Hong-Song Chen, Hui-Ying Rao
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
诊断为慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)并发晚期肝纤维化/代偿性肝硬化的患者面临着重大的疾病进展风险,而针对该患者组低水平病毒血症(LLV)准确预后指标的临床研究仍然相对较少。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究纳入了 141 名患有晚期肝纤维化/代偿性肝硬化的 CHB 患者,他们接受富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 治疗,根据第 48 周时的 HBV DNA 水平将患者分为 LLV 组和完全病毒学缓解 (CVR) 组。
核心方法
进行单变量和多变量 Logistic 回归分析以确定 LLV 发生的危险因素,LLV 定义为 HBV DNA 水平为 20-2000 IU/mL,CVR 定义为 HBV DNA 水平低于 20 IU/mL。
主要结果
A total of 141 patients were included, with 17.0% (24/141) developing LLV, and multivariate logistic regression confirmed that HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL at baseline was associated with LLV (OR = 18.824, 95%CI: 1.608-220.404, P = 0.019).
解释与创新
作者解释说,对于接受 TDF 治疗的晚期肝纤维化/代偿性肝硬化 CHB 患者,HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL 是 LLV 的高危因素,为这一特定人群提供了一个新的预后指标。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为患有晚期纤维化的 CHB 患者的临床监测策略提供信息,从而可以对 LLV 及其相关并发症(如加速肝纤维化进展和肝细胞癌)风险较高的患者进行早期干预。
局限与证据强度
由于回顾性设计和单中心研究,证据强度中等,这可能会限制普遍性,尽管样本量和统计分析对已确定的风险因素提供了合理的信心。

Risk factors for low-level viremia in tenofovir disoproxil fumarate-treated chronic hepatitis B patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis

Authors: Run-Ling Zhang, Hai-Ying Zhang, Yu-Yuan Jia, Zhen-Yu Wang, Feng Liu, Hong-Song Chen, Hui-Ying Rao
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Patients diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) complicated by advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis face a significant risk of disease progression. Despite first-line nucleos(t)ide analog therapy, some patients continues to exhibit low-level viremia (LLV). Furthermore, LLV is closely associated with accelerated liver fibrosis progression and a higher likelihood of hepatocellular carcinoma. Clinical studies on accurate prognostic indicators for LLV in this patient group are still comparatively rare. AIM To identify the high-risk factors linked to the occurrence of LLV among CHB patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis. METHODS This retrospective study enrolled 141 CHB patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis who received tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF). Patients were allocated into two groups according to their virological response at week 48 after treatment initiation: LLV, defined as hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA levels of 20-2000 IU/mL, and complete virological response (CVR), defined as HBV DNA levels below 20 IU/mL. Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analysis were carried out to determine risk factors for LLV development. RESULTS A total of 141 patients were included in this study, among whom 17.0% (24/141) developed LLV. Significant differences were identified between the LLV and CVR groups regarding baseline HBV DNA, HBV RNA, hepatitis B e antigen positivity, HBV DNA ≥ 5.0 Log10 IU/mL, 6.0 Log10 IU/mL, 7.0 Log10 IU/mL, HBV RNA ≥ 5.0 Log10 copies/mL, and baseline alpha-fetoprotein. Multivariate logistic regression confirmed that that HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL (OR = 18.824, 95%CI: 1.608-220.404, P = 0.019) at baseline was associated with the occurrence of LLV. CONCLUSION In TDF-treated CHB with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated liver cirrhosis, HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL is a high-risk factor associated with LLV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Patients diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) complicated by advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis face a significant risk of disease progression, and clinical studies on accurate prognostic indicators for low-level viremia (LLV) in this patient group are still comparatively rare.
Design and population
This retrospective study enrolled 141 CHB patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis who received tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF), with patients allocated into LLV and complete virological response (CVR) groups based on HBV DNA levels at week 48.
Core methods
Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analysis were carried out to determine risk factors for LLV development, with LLV defined as HBV DNA levels of 20-2000 IU/mL and CVR as HBV DNA levels below 20 IU/mL.
Main results
A total of 141 patients were included, with 17.0% (24/141) developing LLV, and multivariate logistic regression confirmed that HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL at baseline was associated with LLV (OR = 18.824, 95%CI: 1.608-220.404, P = 0.019).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that HBV DNA ≥ 7.0 Log10 IU/mL is a high-risk factor for LLV in TDF-treated CHB patients with advanced hepatic fibrosis/compensated cirrhosis, providing a novel prognostic indicator for this specific population.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform clinical monitoring strategies for CHB patients with advanced fibrosis, allowing for earlier intervention in those at higher risk of LLV and its associated complications like accelerated liver fibrosis progression and hepatocellular carcinoma.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the retrospective design and single-center study, which may limit generalizability, though the sample size and statistical analyses provide reasonable confidence in the identified risk factor.

Peg-INF 加 TDF 与 TDF 单药治疗在 HBsAg 水平高、HBeAg 阴性状态和 ALT 水平正常的不确定期 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中的比较

Comparison of Peg-INF plus TDF versus TDF Monotherapy in Indeterminate-phase Chronic Hepatitis B Patients with High HBsAg levels, HBeAg-negative status, and Normal ALT Levels
作者： Min Liu, An Xiao, Bing Bu, Lili Zuo, Yuting Zhang, Ling Zhu, Liping Huang, Yilan Wang, Jinbo Luo, Wei Yue
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎基线乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)3000 IU/mL)、乙型肝炎e抗原阴性状态、不确定期(HBEIP)丙氨酸转氨酶水平正常的患者,过渡到有利组。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 Peg-INF 加 TDF 与 TDF 单药治疗相比,在不确定阶段治疗 HBsAg 水平高、HBeAg 阴性状态和 ALT 水平正常的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的疗效。
设计与对象
该研究针对处于不确定期的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者进行,其特征是 HBsAg 水平高、HBeAg 阴性状态和丙氨酸转氨酶水平正常。研究人群包括过渡到有利群体的个体。
核心方法
该研究在具有特定 HBV 特征的患者中比较了 Peg-INF 加 TDF 与 TDF 单一疗法。这些方法涉及根据患者的临床状况评估患者向有利组的转变。
主要结果
该研究直接观察到,与 TDF 单药治疗组相比,Peg-INF 加 TDF 组的患者表现出向有利组的转变。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明联合疗法可能更有效地将患者转变为有利组,这是在该患者群体中的一个新观察结果。

这些发现可能为不确定阶段的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者(特别是 HBsAg 水平高且 ALT 水平正常的患者)提供 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度

Comparison of Peg-INF plus TDF versus TDF Monotherapy in Indeterminate-phase Chronic Hepatitis B Patients with High HBsAg levels, HBeAg-negative status, and Normal ALT Levels

Authors: Min Liu, An Xiao, Bing Bu, Lili Zuo, Yuting Zhang, Ling Zhu, Liping Huang, Yilan Wang, Jinbo Luo, Wei Yue
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B patients with baseline hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) 3000 IU/mL), hepatitis B e antigen-negative status, and normal alanine transaminase levels in the indeterminate phase (HBEIP), transition to the favorable group.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the efficacy of Peg-INF plus TDF compared to TDF monotherapy in managing chronic hepatitis B patients with high HBsAg levels, HBeAg-negative status, and normal ALT levels during the indeterminate phase.
Design and population
The study was conducted on patients with chronic hepatitis B in the indeterminate phase, characterized by high HBsAg levels, HBeAg-negative status, and normal alanine transaminase levels. The study population included individuals transitioning to the favorable group.
Core methods
The study compared Peg-INF plus TDF with TDF monotherapy in patients with specific HBV characteristics. The methods involved assessing the transition of patients to the favorable group based on their clinical status.
Main results
The study directly observed that patients in the Peg-INF plus TDF group showed a transition to the favorable group compared to those in the TDF monotherapy group.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that the combination therapy may be more effective in transitioning patients to the favorable group, which is a novel observation in this patient population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform treatment strategies for chronic hepatitis B patients in the indeterminate phase, particularly those with high HBsAg levels and normal ALT levels.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its abstract-only nature, which restricts the depth of analysis. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed data.

纳武单抗诱导转移性胃腺癌患者乙型肝炎再激活 - 病例报告

Nivolumab Induced Reactivation of Hepatitis B in a Patient with Metastatic Gastric Adenocarcinoma—A Case Report
作者： Jan Naseer Kaur, Parikshit Padhi, Abhinav Dodeja
摘要中文翻译背景和临床意义:使用免疫检查点抑制剂(ICIs)引起肝毒性的最常见原因是自身免疫性肝炎。由于大多数先前患有乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎等病毒感染的患者被排除在使用 ICI 的试验中,因此 ICI 在这些患有活动性或既往治疗过的肝炎患者中的安全性尚不清楚。随着这些药物在许多恶性肿瘤中的广泛使用,了解这些药物导致病毒再激活的风险非常重要。There are only few case series and reports documenting hepatitis B reactivations with the use of ICIs. Case Presentation: We present a middle-aged woman with a history of treated hepatitis B who presented with metastatic gastric cancer. She was treated with two cycles of 5-FU,oxaliplatin and nivolumab followed by maintenance nivolumab.使用纳武利尤单抗 14 个月后,她出现明显的转氨酶升高,并被发现乙型肝炎重新激活。由于最初怀疑是自身免疫性肝炎,患者开始服用泼尼松 1 mg/kg,但转氨酶没有改善。Due to the significant elevation of HBV DNA,she was diagnosed with hepatitis B reactivation. She was initiated on entecavir with normalization of transaminases and improvement in HBV DNA levels. She was successfully rechallenged with nivolumab with no evidence of recurrent transaminitis or worsening HBV DNA levels. Conclusions: There are case series of HBV reactivation with the use of ICIs.我们认为,任何已知有 HBV 病史的患者都应在开始 ICI 之前通过 DNA 水平连续监测获得基线病毒滴度。我们可能需要进行评估病毒再激活风险的前瞻性研究,以更好地了解病毒再激活的风险及其对 ICI 安全性和有效性的潜在影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解有乙型肝炎治疗史并接受免疫检查点抑制剂(ICIs)治疗的患者乙型肝炎再激活的风险,因为由于将此类患者排除在初始试验之外,这种风险尚未得到充分确定。
设计与对象
这是一位中年女性的 病例报告,她有乙型肝炎治疗史,后来患上转移性胃癌,并接受了纳武单抗治疗。
核心方法
该患者接受了两个周期的 5-FU、奥沙利铂和纳武单抗治疗,随后接受纳武单抗维持治疗,并监测肝毒性和乙型肝炎再激活。
主要结果
使用纳武单抗 治疗 14 个月后,患者出现明显的转氨炎,并发现乙型肝炎重新激活,HBV DNA 显着升高证实了这一点。
解释与创新
作者将此病例解释为纳武利尤单抗诱导乙型肝炎再激活的证据,强调这是 ICI 的已知但尚未广泛记录的风险。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,已知有乙型肝炎病史的患者在开始 ICI 之前应检测基线病毒滴度,并连续监测 DNA 水平,以防止病毒再激活。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于单个 病例报告,尽管详细的临床过程增强了内部有效性,但对研究结果的普遍性的信心较低。

Nivolumab Induced Reactivation of Hepatitis B in a Patient with Metastatic Gastric Adenocarcinoma—A Case Report

Authors: Jan Naseer Kaur, Parikshit Padhi, Abhinav Dodeja
Original abstract (English)
Background and Clinical Significance: The most common cause of liver toxicity with the use of immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) is autoimmune hepatitis. As most patients with prior viral infections such as hepatitis B and hepatitis C were excluded in trials for the use of ICIs, the safety of ICIs in these patients with active or prior treated hepatitis is unknown. With expanded use of these medications in many malignancies, it is important to understand the risk of viral reactivation with these medications. There are only few case series and reports documenting hepatitis B reactivations with the use of ICIs. Case Presentation: We present a middle-aged woman with a history of treated hepatitis B who presented with metastatic gastric cancer. She was treated with two cycles of 5-FU, oxaliplatin and nivolumab followed by maintenance nivolumab. After 14 months of nivolumab, she developed marked transaminitis and was found to have reactivation of hepatitis B. As autoimmune hepatitis was the initial suspicion, the patient was initiated on prednisone 1 mg/kg with no improvement in transaminases. Due to the significant elevation of HBV DNA, she was diagnosed with hepatitis B reactivation. She was initiated on entecavir with normalization of transaminases and improvement in HBV DNA levels. She was successfully rechallenged with nivolumab with no evidence of recurrent transaminitis or worsening HBV DNA levels. Conclusions: There are case series of HBV reactivation with the use of ICIs. We believe that any patients with known history of HBV should get baseline viral titers prior to initiation of ICIs with serial monitoring of DNA levels. Prospective studies to evaluate risk of reactivation may need to be performed for us to get a better understanding of risks of viral reactivation and potential effects it may have on safety and efficacy of ICIs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the risk of hepatitis B reactivation in patients with a history of treated hepatitis B who are treated with immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs), as this risk was not well established due to exclusion of such patients from initial trials.
Design and population
This was a case report of a middle-aged woman with a history of treated hepatitis B who developed metastatic gastric cancer and was treated with nivolumab.
Core methods
The patient was treated with two cycles of 5-FU, oxaliplatin and nivolumab followed by maintenance nivolumab, and was monitored for liver toxicity and hepatitis B reactivation.
Main results
After 14 months of nivolumab treatment, the patient developed marked transaminitis and was found to have reactivation of hepatitis B, confirmed by significant elevation of HBV DNA.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted this case as evidence of hepatitis B reactivation induced by nivolumab, highlighting that this is a known but not widely documented risk with ICIs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that patients with a known history of hepatitis B should have baseline viral titers prior to initiation of ICIs with serial monitoring of DNA levels to prevent reactivation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a single case report, which provides low confidence in the generalizability of the findings, though the detailed clinical course strengthens the internal validity.

埃塞俄比亚巴赫达尔大学健康科学专业毕业生的乙型肝炎知识、态度、预防实践和相关因素

Hepatitis B knowledge, attitudes, prevention practices, and associated factors among graduating health sciences students at Bahir Dar University, Ethiopia
作者： Fikirte Temesgen, Fasikaw Berihun, Malede Berihun Yismaw, Hiwot M Dessalegn, Desalegn Getnet Demsie, Chernet Tafere, Zenaw Debasu Addisu, Adane Yehualaw, Ebrahim Abdela Siraj, Gizachew Motbaynor
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 感染是一个重大的公共卫生问题,通过血液和体液传染性很高。这是一种 疫苗 可预防的疾病。健康科学研究生是未来的医疗保健专业人员,他们在 乙型肝炎病毒 的预防和管理中发挥着至关重要的作用。然而,他们接触病毒的风险也增加了。该研究旨在评估巴赫达尔大学医学与健康科学学院研究生对乙型肝炎病毒感染及其相关因素的知识、态度和预防实践。2024 年 1 月 15 日至 3 月 14 日,巴赫达尔大学医学与健康科学学院使用预先测试的结构化自填问卷进行了一项横断面研究。共有 149 名符合纳入标准的参与者参加了该研究。使用社会科学统计包第 25 版输入和分析数据。采用频率、百分比和二元逻辑回归来总结和解释数据。进行多变量逻辑回归分析来评估关联性,统计显着性定义为 p 值 ≤ 0.05 和 95% 置信区间。仅将单变量分析中 p 值 ≤ 0.2 的变量纳入多变量分析中,以考虑潜在的混杂因素。共有 86 名(57.7%)参与者表现出对乙型肝炎的良好了解,近一半(48.3%)表现出良好态度,76 名参与者(51%)报告了良好做法。学习医学(AOR:8.56;95% CI:2.42-30.29)、护理学(AOR:3.88;95% CI:1.07-14.00)、药学(AOR:6.26;95% CI:1.33-29.55)和医学检验科学(AOR:4.12;95% CI: 1.01–16.80)更有可能拥有良好的知识。此外,之前接受过 乙型肝炎病毒 感染培训的学生也更有可能拥有良好的知识(AOR:5.37;95%CI:1.79-16.23)。然而,药学专业学生的良好实践水平明显较低(AOR:0.07;95% CI:0.01-0.68)。与他们的态度和实践相比,健康科学专业的学生对 乙型肝炎病毒 感染及其预防策略表现出更多的了解。知识水平与学生的学习领域和之前的 乙型肝炎病毒 培训显着相关,而实践水平也与学习领域显着相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
需要进行这项研究是因为 乙型肝炎病毒 感染是一个主要的公共卫生问题,通过血液和体液具有很高的传染率,并且它是一种 疫苗 可预防的疾病。该研究旨在评估巴赫达尔大学医学与健康科学学院研究生对乙型肝炎病毒感染的知识、态度和预防实践以及相关因素。
设计与对象
2024 年 1 月 15 日至 3 月 14 日,巴赫达尔大学医学与健康科学学院使用预先测试的结构化自填问卷进行了一项横断面研究。共有 149 名符合纳入标准的参与者参加了该研究。
核心方法
使用社会科学统计包第 25 版输入和分析数据。采用频率、百分比和二元逻辑回归来总结和解释数据,并使用多变量逻辑回归分析来评估关联性,其中统计显着性定义为 p 值 ≤ 0.05 和 95% 置信区间。
主要结果
共有 86 名(57.7%)参与者表现出对乙型肝炎的良好了解,近一半(48.3%)表现出良好态度,76 名参与者(51%)报告了良好做法。与他们的态度和实践相比,健康科学专业的学生对 乙型肝炎病毒 感染及其预防策略表现出更多的了解。
解释与创新
作者解释说,知识水平与学生的学习领域和之前的 乙型肝炎病毒 培训显着相关,而实践水平也与学习领域显着相关。该研究强调,尽管健康科学专业的学生拥有良好的知识,但他们对乙型肝炎预防的态度和做法并不理想。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的教育干预措施提供信息,以改善健康科学学生的乙型肝炎预防实践,特别是在药学等实践明显较低的领域。该研究强调了将乙型肝炎培训纳入健康科学课程以加强未来医疗保健专业人员的预防工作的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,无法建立因果关系,而且样本量相对较小(只有 149 名参与者)。证据强度中等,因为该研究提供了有关知识、态度和实践的宝贵见解,但可能无法完全推广到其他环境或人群。

Hepatitis B knowledge, attitudes, prevention practices, and associated factors among graduating health sciences students at Bahir Dar University, Ethiopia

Authors: Fikirte Temesgen, Fasikaw Berihun, Malede Berihun Yismaw, Hiwot M Dessalegn, Desalegn Getnet Demsie, Chernet Tafere, Zenaw Debasu Addisu, Adane Yehualaw, Ebrahim Abdela Siraj, Gizachew Motbaynor
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus infection is a major public health concern with a high contagious rate via blood and body fluids. It is a vaccine-preventable disease. Graduate-level students of health sciences are future healthcare professionals who play a crucial role in the prevention and management of hepatitis B virus. However, they are also at increased risk of exposure to the virus. The study was aimed to assess knowledge, attitude and prevention practices towards HBV infection and associated factors among graduate students at the College of Medicine and Health Sciences, Bahir Dar University. A cross-sectional study was conducted at the College of Medicine and Health Sciences, Bahir Dar University, from 15 January to 14 March 2024, using a pre-tested, structured, self-administered questionnaire. A total of 149 eligible participants who met the inclusion criteria were enrolled in the study. Data were entered and analyzed using Statistical Package for the Social Sciences version 25. Frequencies, percentages and binary logistic regression were employed to summarize and interpret the data. A multivariable logistic regression analysis was conducted to assess associations, with statistical significance defined as a p-value ≤ 0.05 and 95% confidence intervals. Only variables with a p-value ≤ 0.2 in the univariate analysis were included in the multivariate analysis to account for potential confounding. A total of 86 (57.7%) participants demonstrated good knowledge about hepatitis B, while nearly half (48.3%) exhibited a favorable attitude, and 76 participants (51%) reported good practices. Those studying medicine (AOR: 8.56; 95% CI: 2.42–30.29), nursing (AOR: 3.88; 95% CI: 1.07–14.00), pharmacy (AOR: 6.26; 95% CI: 1.33–29.55), and medical laboratory science (AOR: 4.12; 95% CI: 1.01–16.80) were more likely to have good knowledge. Furthermore, students who had received prior training on hepatitis B virus infection were also more likely to have good knowledge (AOR: 5.37; 95% CI: 1.79–16.23). However, pharmacy students were found to have significantly lower levels of good practice (AOR: 0.07; 95% CI: 0.01–0.68). Health science students showed greater knowledge about hepatitis B virus infection and its prevention strategies compared to their attitudes and practices. Knowledge levels were significantly associated with the students’ field of study and prior training on hepatitis B virus, while practice levels also showed a significant association with the field of study.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed because hepatitis B virus infection is a major public health concern with a high contagious rate via blood and body fluids, and it is a vaccine-preventable disease. The study aimed to assess knowledge, attitude and prevention practices towards HBV infection and associated factors among graduate students at the College of Medicine and Health Sciences, Bahir Dar University.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted at the College of Medicine and Health Sciences, Bahir Dar University, from 15 January to 14 March 2024, using a pre-tested, structured, self-administered questionnaire. A total of 149 eligible participants who met the inclusion criteria were enrolled in the study.
Core methods
Data were entered and analyzed using Statistical Package for the Social Sciences version 25. Frequencies, percentages and binary logistic regression were employed to summarize and interpret the data, with a multivariable logistic regression analysis conducted to assess associations, where statistical significance was defined as a p-value ≤ 0.05 and 95% confidence intervals.
Main results
A total of 86 (57.7%) participants demonstrated good knowledge about hepatitis B, while nearly half (48.3%) exhibited a favorable attitude, and 76 participants (51%) reported good practices. Health science students showed greater knowledge about hepatitis B virus infection and its prevention strategies compared to their attitudes and practices.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that knowledge levels were significantly associated with the students' field of study and prior training on hepatitis B virus, while practice levels also showed a significant association with the field of study. The study highlights that despite good knowledge, attitudes and practices towards hepatitis B prevention were suboptimal among health science students.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted educational interventions to improve hepatitis B prevention practices among health science students, particularly in fields like pharmacy where practices were significantly lower. The study underscores the importance of incorporating hepatitis B training in health science curricula to enhance prevention efforts among future healthcare professionals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its cross-sectional design, which prevents establishing causality, and the relatively small sample size of 149 participants. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study provides valuable insights into knowledge, attitudes, and practices but may not be fully generalizable to other settings or populations.

Multiplex gene editing to target the roots of hepatitis B virus infection

Authors: Anuj Kumar, Fabien Zoulim
Original abstract (English)
Publisher of over 50 scientific journals across the life, physical, earth, and health sciences, both independently and in partnership with scientific societies including Cell, Neuron, Immunity, Current Biology, AJHG, and the Trends Journals.

Design and population

术前肠道微生物群结合机器学习预测 HBV 相关肝细胞癌肝切除术后肝功能衰竭:一项试点研究

Preoperative gut microbiota combined with machine learning for predicting post-hepatectomy liver failure in HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma: a pilot study
作者： Yu-Chong Peng, Jing-Xuan Xu, Hai-Yan Lu, Yu-Miao Bi, Lu-Nan Qi, Ying-Guo Chen, Jian-Hong Zhong, Andrea Schlegel, Le-Qun Li
摘要中文翻译目的 本研究调查了新诊断的 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 (HBV-HCC) 患者术前肠道微生物群 (GM) 特征与肝切除术后肝衰竭 (PHLF) 之间的关联,旨在探索用于围手术期风险分层的非侵入性、可修改的 GM 生物标志物。方法 对总共 233 份粪便样本进行 16S rRNA 测序,并分为三个不同的组:训练集 (n = 179)、内部验证集 (n = 32) 和独立的外部验证集 (n = 22)。对患有和不患有 PHLF 的患者的术前 GM 特征进行比较,以确定诊断生物标志物并构建机器学习驱动的风险模型(XGBoost、随机森林 [RF] 和梯度提升机 [GBM])。结果 各组之间观察到显着的 β 多样性差异(加权 UniFrac),鉴定出 11 个差异属;PHLF 患者表现出粪杆菌和布劳氏菌的特征性富集,同时拟杆菌明显减少。功能推论揭示了 PHLF 组中明显的代谢重塑,其特征是氨基酸生物合成(特别是赖氨酸和精氨酸)和核苷分解代谢的上调,同时肝脏相互作用的尿素循环、中心碳代谢(糖酵解和三羧酸 [TCA] 循环)和短链脂肪酸 [SCFA](丁酸)代谢显着下调。在独立的外部验证集中,XGBoost、RF 和 GBM 模型的受试者工作特征曲线下面积 (AUC) 分别为 78.12%、71.88% 和 63.54%。重要的是,这些模型显示出强大的排除性能,XGBoost 和 RF 的特异性为 81.25%,GBM 的特异性为 72.22%,而所有三个模型都保持了 81.25% 的稳定阴性预测值 (NPV) 和异常稳定的阴性 F1 分数(F1- 阴性)。决策曲线分析 (DCA) 证实了所有模型的显着临床净效益。结论 我们是最早描述 PHLF 患者不同 GM 特征的人之一,具有跨地理可重复性。GM 展示了作为针对 PHLF 主要终点的术前风险分层的非侵入性工具的巨大潜力。该特征具有高特异性和强大的排除能力,为准确的低风险患者识别和分诊建立了临床上稳健的框架,从而优化围手术期管理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探索非侵入性、可修改的肠道微生物生物标志物,用于新诊断的 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者的围手术期风险分层,满足改进肝切除术后肝功能衰竭预测的需求。
设计与对象
开展了一项试点研究,涉及 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者的 233 份粪便样本,分为训练集、内部验证集和独立外部验证集,以评估肠道微生物群概况的预测性能。
核心方法
使用 16S rRNA 测序比较有和没有肝切除术后肝衰竭患者的术前肠道微生物群特征,并构建机器学习驱动的风险模型(XGBoost、随机森林和梯度提升机)来识别诊断生物标志物。
主要结果
在 PHLF 和非 PHLF 组之间观察到显着的 β 多样性差异,确定了 11 个差异属。PHLF 患者表现出粪杆菌和布劳氏菌富集,同时拟杆菌减少。功能推论揭示了 PHLF 组中明显的代谢重塑。
解释与创新
该研究确定了 PHLF 患者独特的肠道微生物群特征,具有跨地理重复性,为针对 PHLF 主要终点的术前风险分层提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
肠道微生物群特征显示出作为术前风险分层的非侵入性工具的强大潜力,能够准确识别低风险患者并优化围手术期管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项试点,样本量有限,虽然模型显示出较高的特异性和临床净效益,但由于缺乏初始组之外的长期随访和外部验证,证据强度中等。

Preoperative gut microbiota combined with machine learning for predicting post-hepatectomy liver failure in HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma: a pilot study

Authors: Yu-Chong Peng, Jing-Xuan Xu, Hai-Yan Lu, Yu-Miao Bi, Lu-Nan Qi, Ying-Guo Chen, Jian-Hong Zhong, Andrea Schlegel, Le-Qun Li
Original abstract (English)
Objective This study investigated the association between preoperative gut microbiota (GM) profiles and post-hepatectomy liver failure (PHLF) in patients with newly diagnosed HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HBV-HCC), aiming to explore non-invasive, modifiable GM biomarkers for perioperative risk stratification. Methods A total of 233 fecal samples underwent 16S rRNA sequencing and were stratified into three distinct sets: a training set ( n = 179), an internal validation set ( n = 32), and an independent external validation set ( n = 22). Preoperative GM features were compared between patients with and without PHLF to identify diagnostic biomarkers and construct machine learning-driven risk models (XGBoost, Random Forest [RF], and Gradient Boosting Machine [GBM]). Results Significant β-diversity differences (Weighted UniFrac) were observed between the groups, identifying 11 differential genera; PHLF patients exhibited a characteristic enrichment of Faecalibacterium, and Blautia, alongside a profound depletion of Bacteroides. Functional inferences revealed distinct metabolic remodeling in the PHLF group, characterized by the upregulation of amino acid biosynthesis (specifically lysine and arginine) and nucleoside catabolism, coupled with a prominent downregulation of the hepatic-interactive urea cycle, central carbon metabolism (glycolysis and tricarboxylic acid [TCA] cycle), and short-chain fatty acid [SCFA] (butyrate) metabolism. In the independent external validation set, the XGBoost, RF, and GBM models achieved areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUCs) of 78.12%, 71.88%, and 63.54%, respectively. Importantly, the models showed potent exclusionary performance, with specificities of 81.25% for XGBoost and RF versus 72.22% for GBM, while all three maintained a stable negative predictive value (NPV) of 81.25% and exceptionally stable negative F1-scores (F1- negative).Decision curve analysis (DCA) confirmed notable clinical net benefit of all models. Conclusion We are among the first to characterize distinct GM signatures in PHLF patients with cross-geographic reproducibility. GM demonstrates strong potential as a non-invasive tool for preoperative risk stratification targeting the primary endpoint of PHLF. Characterized by high specificity and potent exclusionary capacity, this signature establishes a clinically robust framework for accurate low-risk patient identification and triage, thereby optimizing perioperative management.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore non-invasive, modifiable gut microbiota biomarkers for perioperative risk stratification in patients with newly diagnosed HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma, addressing the need for improved prediction of post-hepatectomy liver failure.
Design and population
A pilot study involving 233 fecal samples from patients with HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma was conducted, stratified into training, internal validation, and independent external validation sets to assess the predictive performance of gut microbiota profiles.
Core methods
Preoperative gut microbiota features were compared between patients with and without post-hepatectomy liver failure using 16S rRNA sequencing, and machine learning-driven risk models (XGBoost, Random Forest, and Gradient Boosting Machine) were constructed to identify diagnostic biomarkers.
Main results
Significant β-diversity differences were observed between PHLF and non-PHLF groups, identifying 11 differential genera. PHLF patients showed enrichment of Faecalibacterium and Blautia, alongside depletion of Bacteroides. Functional inferences revealed distinct metabolic remodeling in the PHLF group.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified a distinct gut microbiota signature in PHLF patients with cross-geographic reproducibility, offering a novel approach for preoperative risk stratification targeting the primary endpoint of PHLF.
Scientific and public-health significance
The gut microbiota signature demonstrates strong potential as a non-invasive tool for preoperative risk stratification, enabling accurate identification of low-risk patients and optimizing perioperative management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a pilot with limited sample size, and while the models showed high specificity and clinical net benefit, the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of long-term follow-up and external validation beyond the initial sets.

乙型肝炎与酒精共存对肝硬化患者肝细胞癌发生的影响

The Effect of Hepatitis B and Alcohol Coexistence on the Development of Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Cirrhotic Patients
作者： Tahir Alper Cinli, Sezgin Vatansever
摘要中文翻译简介:本研究旨在回顾性比较不同病因的肝硬化患者中肝细胞癌(HCC)的发病率、总生存期(OS)和无HCC生存期:酒精相关、发病率(HBV)相关和合并HBV-酒精相关。此外,在HBV阳性的患者中评估了HBV病毒载量与无HCC生存期之间的关系方法:这项回顾性单中心 队列研究 研究纳入了 2007 年 7 月至 2014 年 4 月期间随访超过 6 个月的 648 名肝硬化患者。患者被分为三个病因组:酒精相关性肝硬化 (n=310)、HBV 相关性肝硬化 (n=294) 和 HBV 酒精相关性肝硬化(n=44)。比较各组的临床特征、HCC 发病率、总生存率和无 HCC 生存率。结果:在随访期间,648 名患者中有 138 名 (21.3%) 发生 HCC。各组之间 HCC 的 发病率 差异显着:酒精组为 5.5%,HBV 组为 34.7%,HBV-酒精联合组为 43.2% (p<0.001)。酒精组的 5 年累积 HCC 风险为 7%,HBV 组为 40%,HBV-酒精组为 49%。尽管 HCC 发病率 存在显着差异,但不同病因组之间的总生存率没有显着差异 (p=0.367)。HBV 阳性患者,基线 e HBV DNA 病毒载量(<10 6 与≥10 6 拷贝/mL)在无 HCC 生存方面没有显示出统计学上的显着差异(p=0.408)。结论:HBV 感染,特别是与大量饮酒联合使用时,会显着且协同地增加肝硬化患者发生 HCC 的风险。在 HBV-酒精联合组中,累积 HCC 发病率 异常高,40% 的患者在第一年内发展为 HCC,强调了在这个高风险亚组中迫切需要密切 监测。尽管 HCC 发生率不同,但各组之间的总生存率仍然相似,反映了肝硬化相关 监测 的复杂和多因素决定因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在回顾性比较不同病因的肝硬化患者中肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的 发病率、总生存期 (OS) 和无 HCC 生存期:酒精相关、发病率 (HBV) 相关以及 HBV 与酒精联合相关。此外,在 HBV 阳性患者中评估了 HBV 病毒载量与无 HCC 生存率之间的关系。
设计与对象
这项回顾性单中心 队列研究 研究纳入了 2007 年 7 月至 2014 年 4 月期间随访超过 6 个月的 648 名肝硬化患者。患者被分为三个病因组:酒精相关性肝硬化 (n=310)、HBV 相关性肝硬化 (n=294) 和 HBV 酒精相关性肝硬化 (n=44)。
核心方法
比较三个病因组的临床特征、HCC 发病率、总生存期和无 HCC 生存期。在 HBV 阳性患者中评估了 HBV DNA 病毒载量(<10^6 与 ≥10^6 拷贝/mL)与无 HCC 生存率之间的关系。
主要结果
在随访期间,648 名患者中有 138 名(21.3%)发展为 HCC。HCC 的 患病率 在各组之间存在显着差异:酒精组为 5.5%,HBV 组为 34.7%,HBV 与酒精联合组为 43.2%(p<0.001)。酒精组的 5 年累积 HCC 风险为 7%,HBV 组为 40%,HBV 酒精组为 49%。不同病因组之间的总生存率没有显着差异(p=0.367)。
解释与创新
乙型肝炎病毒感染,特别是与大量饮酒相结合,会显着且协同地增加肝硬化患者患肝癌的风险。HBV-酒精联合治疗组中累计 HCC 发病率 异常高,其中 40% 的患者在第一年内发展为 HCC,这突显了这一高风险亚组迫切需要密切使用 发病率。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了将 HBV-酒精共存视为肝硬化患者发生 HCC 的主要危险因素的重要性,并强调在这一高危人群中需要加强 监测 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性单中心设计和 HBV-酒精联合组的样本量相对较小(n=44)而受到限制。尽管存在这些限制,但由于总体样本量大且具有统计显着性,证据强度中等。

The Effect of Hepatitis B and Alcohol Coexistence on the Development of Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Cirrhotic Patients

Authors: Tahir Alper Cinli, Sezgin Vatansever
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: This study aimed to retrospectively compare the incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), overall survival (OS), and HCC-fr ee survival among patients with cirr hosis of differen t etiologies: alcohol-related, hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related, and combined HBV-alcohol-related.Additionally, the relationship between HBV viral load and HCC-free survival was evaluated in HBV-positive patients.Methods: This retrospective single-center cohort study included 648 patients with cirrhosis followed for more than 6 months between July 2007 and April 2014.The patients were categorized into three etiological groups: alcohol-related cirrhosis (n=310), HBV-related cirrhosis (n=294), and HBV-alcohol-related cirrhosis (n=44).Clinical characteristics, HCC incidence, overall survival, and HCC-free survival were compared across these groups.Results: During the follow-up period, HCC developed in 138 (21.3%) of the 648 patients.The prevalence of HCC differed significantly among the groups: 5.5% in the alcohol group, 34.7% in the HBV group, and 43.2% in the combined HBV-alcohol group (p<0.001).The 5-year cumulative HCC risk was 7% for the alcohol group, 40% for the HBV group, and 49% for the HBV-alcohol group.Despite these striking differences in HCC incidence, overall survival did not differ significantly among the etiological groups (p=0.367).Furthermore, in HBV-positive patients, baselin e HBV DNA viral load (<10 6 vs. ≥10 6 copies/mL) did not show a statistically significant difference in HCC-free survival (p=0.408).Conclusion: HBV infection, particularly when combined with heavy alcohol use, significantly and synergistically increases the risk of HCC in patients with liver cirrhosis.The exceptionally high cumulative HCC incidence in the combined HBV-alcohol group, with 40% of patients developing HCC within the first year, underscores the critical need for close surveillance in this high-risk subgroup.Despite the differing HCC rates, overall survival remain s similar across the groups, reflecting the complex and multifactorial determin ants of cirrhosis-related mortality .
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to retrospectively compare the incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), overall survival (OS), and HCC-free survival among patients with cirrhosis of different etiologies: alcohol-related, hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related, and combined HBV-alcohol-related. Additionally, the relationship between HBV viral load and HCC-free survival was evaluated in HBV-positive patients.
Design and population
This retrospective single-center cohort study included 648 patients with cirrhosis followed for more than 6 months between July 2007 and April 2014. The patients were categorized into three etiological groups: alcohol-related cirrhosis (n=310), HBV-related cirrhosis (n=294), and HBV-alcohol-related cirrhosis (n=44).
Core methods
Clinical characteristics, HCC incidence, overall survival, and HCC-free survival were compared across the three etiological groups. The relationship between HBV DNA viral load (<10^6 vs. ≥10^6 copies/mL) and HCC-free survival was evaluated in HBV-positive patients.
Main results
During the follow-up period, HCC developed in 138 (21.3%) of the 648 patients. The prevalence of HCC differed significantly among the groups: 5.5% in the alcohol group, 34.7% in the HBV group, and 43.2% in the combined HBV-alcohol group (p<0.001). The 5-year cumulative HCC risk was 7% for the alcohol group, 40% for the HBV group, and 49% for the HBV-alcohol group. Overall survival did not differ significantly among the etiological groups (p=0.367).
Interpretation and novelty
HBV infection, particularly when combined with heavy alcohol use, significantly and synergistically increases the risk of HCC in patients with liver cirrhosis. The exceptionally high cumulative HCC incidence in the combined HBV-alcohol group, with 40% of patients developing HCC within the first year, underscores the critical need for close surveillance in this high-risk subgroup.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of considering HBV-alcohol coexistence as a major risk factor for HCC development in cirrhotic patients, emphasizing the need for enhanced surveillance strategies in this high-risk population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective single-center design and relatively small sample size for the combined HBV-alcohol group (n=44). Despite these limitations, the evidence strength is moderate due to the large overall sample size and statistically significant findings.

疫苗接种 混合算子分段经典和分数阶导数洞察乙型肝炎模型的数学和动力学分析

Mathematical and Dynamical Analysis of Hepatitis B Model with Hybrid Operator Piecewise Classical and Fractional Derivative Insight of Different Stages of Vaccinations
作者： Muhammad Farman, Kottakkaran Sooppy Nisar
摘要中文翻译为了观察 疫苗接种 在不同阶段的动态和影响,我们在本文中提出了一种流行病模型,其中包括经典算子和 Atangana-Baleanu 分段算子,用于混合算子的记忆效应。创建的模型的属性考虑了线性增长和 Lipschitz 要求,根据平衡点与许多子区间内的效应率相关,包括可行域中的正性、唯一解、存在性和有界性。通过对影响分段分数算子模型的不同参数进行敏感性分析,得出模型的生物学可行性的再生数 \(R_0\)。全局稳定性采用李亚普诺夫函数理论来深入了解一阶导数结果。系统的混沌行为也通过仿真确定,这表明解绑定到稳态点并且位于可行区域内。对于数值解,使用牛顿多项式插值方法求解不同分数阶值的经典和分段分数混合算子。这项研究增进了我们对乙型肝炎动力学的了解,并为评估疾病控制规划和决策的干预措施的有效性提供了坚实的框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了使用数学模型了解 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 动态以及 疫苗接种 在不同阶段的影响的需要。它旨在探索结合经典和分数导数的混合算子如何捕获与 HBV 传播 和控制相关的记忆效应。
设计与对象
作者提出了一种 HBV 数学流行病模型,该模型集成了经典和 Atangana-Baleanu 分段算子,以解释 传播 动力学中的记忆效应。分析模型的属性,例如可行域内的正性、存在性、唯一性和有界性。
核心方法
推导出再生数\(R_0\)来评估生物学可行性,并进行敏感性分析来评估分段分数算子下不同参数对模型的影响。使用李亚普诺夫函数理论检查全局稳定性,并通过牛顿多项式插值法获得不同分数阶的经典和分段分数混合算子的数值解。
主要结果
系统的混沌行为是通过仿真确定的,证明解决方案在可行区域内保持稳定状态。该模型增进了对 HBV 动态的理解,并为评估疾病控制计划中的干预效果提供了框架。
解释与创新
该研究将这些发现解释为证据,表明具有记忆效应的混合算子可以有效地模拟 HBV 传播 动态,为 HBV 传播的稳定性和控制提供新的见解。新颖之处在于将分段分数算子应用于 HBV 建模并通过 Lyapunov 理论证明系统稳定性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将记忆效应纳入 HBV 模型可能会改善 疫苗接种 策略的评估,并为疾病控制和干预计划的公共卫生决策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告经验验证、现实世界数据应用或临床结果,限制了结论的强度。仅抽象的证据和对理论模型的依赖降低了普遍性和实际适用性。

Mathematical and Dynamical Analysis of Hepatitis B Model with Hybrid Operator Piecewise Classical and Fractional Derivative Insight of Different Stages of Vaccinations

Authors: Muhammad Farman, Kottakkaran Sooppy Nisar
Original abstract (English)
In order to observe the dynamics and impacts of vaccination at different phases, we suggested an epidemic model in this paper that includes classical and Atangana-Baleanu piecewise operators for the hybrid operator’s memory effects. The created model’s properties, which take into account linear growth and Lipschitz requirements relating the rate of effects within its many sub-compartments according to the equilibrium points, include positivity, unique solution, existence, and boundedness in the feasible domain. The reproductive number \(R_0\) is derived for the biological feasibility of the model with sensitivity analysis with different parameters impacting the model for the piecewise fractional operator. Global stability employs Lyapunov function theory to gain insight into first derivative results. The chaotic behavior of the system was also determined through simulation, which shows the solution is bound to a steady state point and lies in a feasible region.For numerical solutions, classical and piecewise fractional hybrid operators at various fractional order values are solved using the Newton polynomial interpolation method.This research advances our knowledge of the dynamics of hepatitis B and offers a solid framework for assessing the effectiveness of interventions for disease control planning and decision-making.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to understand hepatitis B virus (HBV) dynamics and the impact of vaccination across different phases using a mathematical model. It aims to explore how hybrid operators incorporating classical and fractional derivatives can capture memory effects relevant to HBV transmission and control.
Design and population
The authors propose a mathematical epidemic model for HBV that integrates classical and Atangana-Baleanu piecewise operators to account for memory effects in transmission dynamics. The model is analyzed for properties such as positivity, existence, uniqueness, and boundedness within a feasible domain.
Core methods
The reproductive number \(R_0\) is derived to assess biological feasibility, and sensitivity analysis is performed to evaluate the impact of different parameters on the model under the piecewise fractional operator. Global stability is examined using Lyapunov function theory, and numerical solutions are obtained via the Newton polynomial interpolation method for classical and piecewise fractional hybrid operators at varying fractional orders.
Main results
The chaotic behavior of the system is determined through simulation, demonstrating that solutions remain bound to a steady state within a feasible region. The model advances understanding of HBV dynamics and provides a framework for evaluating intervention effectiveness in disease control planning.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets the findings as evidence that hybrid operators with memory effects can effectively model HBV transmission dynamics, offering new insights into the stability and control of HBV spread. The novelty lies in applying piecewise fractional operators to HBV modeling and demonstrating system stability through Lyapunov theory.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that incorporating memory effects into HBV models may improve the assessment of vaccination strategies and inform public health decision-making for disease control and intervention planning.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report empirical validation, real-world data application, or clinical outcomes, limiting the strength of the conclusions. The abstract-only evidence and reliance on theoretical modeling reduce generalizability and practical applicability.
