HBcAg 诱导的 IL-17F 在隐匿性 HBV 感染中导致肝纤维化

HBcAg-Induced IL-17F drives liver fibrosis in occult HBV infection
作者： Bochao Liu, Jieting Huang, Hualong Yang, Qiao Liao, Min Wang, Ru Xu, Zhengang Shan, Huishan Zhong, Fengfang Liao, Tingting Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 虽然隐匿性 乙型肝炎病毒 感染是进行性肝病的已知危险因素,但人们对这种关联的机制基础知之甚少。为了调查这一点,我们进行了一项比较研究,涉及 40 名 OBI 献血者和 100 名健康献血者,以评估肝纤维化的临床参数,包括血清生物标志物、FIB-4 指数和肝脏硬度测量。对这些供体的外周血单核细胞进行转录组测序,发现 OBI 组中 IL-17F 显着上调。从机制上讲,我们发现 IL-17F 主要由 CD4 + T 细胞在 HBcAg 刺激后分泌,并在功能上促进关键纤维化相关细胞因子和标志物的表达。总的来说,我们的研究揭示了一种新途径,其中 HBcAg 诱导 CD4 + T 细胞分泌 IL-17F,进而加速肝纤维形成,为 OBI 相关肝病提供机制基础。重要性 这项研究首次揭示了 OBI 驱动肝纤维化的新机制,证明 HBcAg 刺激 CD4+T 细胞分泌 IL-17F,进而激活巨噬细胞和肝星状细胞,从而促进肝脏炎症和纤维化。这一发现不仅阐明了OBI相关肝病进展的关键分子途径,而且为开发IL-17F靶向诊断方法和抗纤维化治疗提供了重要的理论基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
隐匿性 乙型肝炎病毒 感染 (OBI) 被认为是进行性肝病的危险因素,但 OBI 与肝纤维化联系的机制基础仍不清楚,并且存在严重的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究对 40 名 OBI 献血者和 100 名健康献血者进行了比较分析,以评估与肝纤维化相关的临床参数。
核心方法
对外周血单核细胞进行转录组测序以鉴定差异表达基因,并通过机制实验评估 HBcAg 刺激后 CD4+ T 细胞分泌 IL-17F。
主要结果
OBI 组中 IL-17F 显着上调,机制分析表明 IL-17F 主要由 CD4+ T 细胞响应 HBcAg 分泌,促进纤维化相关细胞因子和标志物表达。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为揭示了 HBcAg 诱导 CD4+ T 细胞分泌 IL-17F 的新途径,从而加速肝纤维化,为 OBI 相关肝病提供了机制解释。
科研与公卫意义
这一发现将 IL-17F 确定为潜在的治疗靶点,并支持开发针对 OBI 相关肝病的 IL-17F 靶向诊断和抗纤维化策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖抽象级证据和小样本而受到限制,这限制了普遍性和因果推理的强度。

HBcAg-Induced IL-17F drives liver fibrosis in occult HBV infection

Authors: Bochao Liu, Jieting Huang, Hualong Yang, Qiao Liao, Min Wang, Ru Xu, Zhengang Shan, Huishan Zhong, Fengfang Liao, Tingting Li
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT While occult hepatitis B virus infection is a known risk factor for progressive liver diseases, the mechanistic basis for this association is poorly understood. To investigate this, we conducted a comparative study involving 40 OBI blood donors and 100 healthy blood donors to assess clinical parameters of liver fibrosis, including serum biomarkers, the FIB-4 index, and liver stiffness measurement. Transcriptome sequencing of peripheral blood mononuclear cells from these donors identified IL-17F as significantly upregulated in the OBI group. Mechanistically, we found that IL-17F was chiefly secreted by CD4 + T cells upon HBcAg stimulation and functionally promoted the expression of key fibrosis-related cytokines and markers. Collectively, our study unveils a novel pathway wherein HBcAg induces CD4 + T cells to secrete IL-17F, which in turn accelerates liver fibrogenesis, providing a mechanistic basis for OBI-associated liver disease. IMPORTANCE This study reveals, for the first time, a novel mechanism by which OBI drives liver fibrosis, demonstrating that HBcAg stimulates CD4 + T cells to secrete IL-17F, which in turn activates macrophages and hepatic stellate cells, thereby promoting liver inflammation and fibrosis. This discovery not only elucidates a key molecular pathway underlying OBI-related liver disease progression, but also provides an important theoretical foundation for developing IL-17F-targeted diagnostic approaches and anti-fibrotic therapies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Occult hepatitis B virus infection (OBI) is recognized as a risk factor for progressive liver diseases, yet the mechanistic basis linking OBI to liver fibrosis remains unclear and represents a critical knowledge gap.
Design and population
The study performed a comparative analysis involving 40 OBI blood donors and 100 healthy blood donors to evaluate clinical parameters associated with liver fibrosis.
Core methods
Transcriptome sequencing of peripheral blood mononuclear cells was conducted to identify differentially expressed genes, and mechanistic experiments assessed IL-17F secretion by CD4+ T cells upon HBcAg stimulation.
Main results
IL-17F was significantly upregulated in the OBI group, and mechanistic assays showed that IL-17F was primarily secreted by CD4+ T cells in response to HBcAg, promoting fibrosis-related cytokine and marker expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as revealing a novel pathway where HBcAg induces CD4+ T cells to secrete IL-17F, which accelerates liver fibrogenesis, providing a mechanistic explanation for OBI-associated liver disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
This discovery identifies IL-17F as a potential therapeutic target and supports the development of IL-17F-targeted diagnostic and anti-fibrotic strategies for OBI-related liver disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on abstract-level evidence and a small sample size, which constrains generalizability and the strength of causal inference.

全球肝硬化住院患者 队列研究 中与 30 天和 90 天再入院相关的因素比较。

Comparison of Factors Associated With 30-Day and 90-Day Readmission Among a Global Cohort of Patients Hospitalised With Cirrhosis.
作者： Ramazan Idilman, Busra Haktaniyan, Ashok Choudhury, Florence Wong, Patrick S Kamath, Qing Xie, Mark Topazian, Peter C Hayes, Mario Reis Alvares-da-Silva, Aldo Torre
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:尚不清楚不同因素是否影响 30 天和 90 天的再入院率。我们在肝硬化住院患者的全球 队列研究 中确定了 90 天 死亡率 的预测因子。方法:在前瞻性入组、非选择性住院的成年肝硬化患者中,比较与出院后 30 天和 90 天再入院、肝移植 (LT) 和 死亡率 相关的变量。结果:我们招募了来自 37 个国家 125 个中心的 4208 名患者。酒精相关性肝病是肝硬化最常见的病因(40%),其次是 乙型肝炎病毒 感染(21%);47% 的患者再次入院,8% 的患者接受肝移植 (LT),23% 的患者在出院后 90 天内死亡。在多变量分析中,国家收入与 30 天和 90 天的再入院率、LT 的接受情况以及出院后 30 和 90 天内的 死亡率 显着相关(所有 p 值 < 0.001)。30 天和 90 天再入院率较高的独立预测因素分别是疾病严重程度 (p < 0.001,p = 0.003)、既往腹水 (p = 0.025,p = 0.016)、既往住院情况 (p < 0.001)、低钠血症 (p < 0.001,p = 0.008) 和住院情况血管加压药的使用(p = 0.002,p = 0.011)。既往静脉曲张出血独立预测较高的 90 天再入院率,而年龄、疾病严重程度、机械通气和院内血管加压药的使用(全部,p < 0.001)增加出院后 30 天和 90 天的死亡几率。此外,入院感染 (p = 0.011) 和院内感染 (p = 0.022) 会增加出院后 90 天的死亡几率。结论:在全球肝硬化住院患者 队列研究 中,高收入国家患者的 30 天和 90 天再入院率最高,同时 死亡率 率较低,LT 发病率 较高。预测 30 天再入院的变量也预测 90 天再入院。了解这些差异对于实现健康公平至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了不同的临床或人口统计因素是否对肝硬化住院患者 30 天和 90 天的再入院产生不同的影响,并指出相同的预测因素是否适用于这些时间范围的不确定性。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性、多中心、全球 队列研究 研究纳入了 37 个国家 125 个中心的非选择性住院成年肝硬化患者。
核心方法
使用多变量分析比较与出院后 30 天和 90 天再入院、肝移植和 死亡率 相关的变量,以确定独立的预测因素。
主要结果
在4208名患者中,47%再次入院,8%接受肝移植,23%在出院后90天内死亡。国家收入水平与 30 天和 90 天的再入院、移植和 死亡率 显着相关(所有 p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,30 天再入院的预测因素也可以预测 90 天再入院的情况,凸显了不同国家收入水平之间的差异以及解决肝硬化护理中健康公平问题的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对疾病严重程度、既往腹水、既往住院情况、低钠血症和院内升压药使用等问题采取针对性干预措施,可以减少住院肝硬化患者的早期和晚期再入院率。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察设计和对抽象级证据的依赖而受到限制,这限制了因果推断和对特定医疗保健环境的普遍性。

Comparison of Factors Associated With 30-Day and 90-Day Readmission Among a Global Cohort of Patients Hospitalised With Cirrhosis.

Authors: Ramazan Idilman, Busra Haktaniyan, Ashok Choudhury, Florence Wong, Patrick S Kamath, Qing Xie, Mark Topazian, Peter C Hayes, Mario Reis Alvares-da-Silva, Aldo Torre
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: It remains unclear whether different factors influence 30- and 90-day readmission rates. We identified predictors of 90-day mortality in a global cohort of hospitalised patients with cirrhosis. METHODS: Variables associated with 30- and 90-day post-discharge readmission, liver transplantation (LT), and mortality were compared in prospectively enrolled, non-electively hospitalised adult patients with cirrhosis. RESULTS: We enrolled 4208 patients from 125 centres across 37 countries. Alcohol-related liver disease was the most common aetiology of cirrhosis (40%), followed by hepatitis B virus infection (21%); 47% were readmitted, 8% received liver transplantation (LT), and 23% died within 90 days post-discharge. In multivariable analysis, country income was significantly associated with both 30- and 90-day readmission rates, the receipt of LT, and mortality within 30 and 90 days post-discharge (all p-values < 0.001). Independent predictors of higher rates of 30- and 90-day readmissions, respectively, were disease severity (p < 0.001, p = 0.003), prior ascites (p = 0.025, p = 0.016), prior hospitalisation (p < 0.001), hyponatremia (p < 0.001, p = 0.008), and in-hospital vasopressor use (p = 0.002, p = 0.011). Prior variceal bleeding independently predicted higher 90-day readmission rates, whereas age, disease severity, mechanical ventilation, and in-hospital vasopressor use (all, p < 0.001) increased odds of 30- and 90-day post-discharge death. Additionally, admission (p = 0.011) and nosocomial (p = 0.022) infections increased the odds of 90-day post-discharge death. CONCLUSION: In a global cohort of hospitalised patients with cirrhosis, patients in high-income countries exhibited the highest rates of 30- and 90-day readmission, alongside lower mortality rates and a higher incidence of LT. Variables that predict 30-day readmission also predicted 90-day readmission. Understanding these disparities is essential for achieving health equity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether distinct clinical or demographic factors differentially influence 30- and 90-day hospital readmission among patients hospitalised with cirrhosis, noting uncertainty about whether the same predictors apply across these timeframes.
Design and population
A prospective, multicentre, global cohort study enrolled non-electively hospitalised adult patients with cirrhosis across 125 centres in 37 countries.
Core methods
Variables associated with 30- and 90-day post-discharge readmission, liver transplantation, and mortality were compared using multivariable analyses to identify independent predictors.
Main results
Among 4208 patients, 47% were readmitted, 8% received liver transplantation, and 23% died within 90 days post-discharge. Country income level was significantly associated with 30- and 90-day readmission, transplantation, and mortality (all p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that predictors of 30-day readmission also predicted 90-day readmission, highlighting disparities by country income level and the need to address health equity in cirrhosis care.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted interventions addressing disease severity, prior ascites, prior hospitalisation, hyponatremia, and in-hospital vasopressor use could reduce early and late readmissions in hospitalised cirrhosis patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and reliance on abstract-level evidence, which constrains causal inference and generalisability to specific healthcare settings.

患者来源的肝癌类器官反映肿瘤生物学及其生长表型与临床结果相关

Patient-Derived Liver Cancer Organoids Reflect Tumor Biology and Their Growth Phenotype Correlates with Clinical Outcomes
作者： Yeon Su Kim, Yoon-Ha Go, Hyung Sun Kim, Jaehwi Seo, Dong Oh Kim, Dong-Youn Hwang, Wookyeom Yang, Jin Hong Lim, Jongman Yoo, 서재휘
摘要中文翻译肝癌仍然是全球主要的健康负担,死亡率 较高且 治疗 反应预测工具有限。源自患者的癌症类器官已成为有前景的临床前模型,可概括肿瘤异质性;然而,在肝细胞癌(HCC)中,已建立的类器官形态多样性的生物学意义仍然知之甚少。在这项探索性研究中,我们研究了不同的类器官生长表型是否反映了潜在的肿瘤生物学并与临床结果相关。我们从 27 名患者的切除肿瘤组织中建立了肝癌类器官,并分析了它们的临床、组织学和基因组特征。类器官在形态上分为囊状和实性类型。对六对匹配的肿瘤类器官进行全外显子组测序 (WES),以评估基因组保真度。对类器官建立、生长特征和临床参数之间的关联进行了统计评估。使用 Kaplan-Meier 方法和单变量 Cox 比例风险回归分析无进展生存期 (PFS)。27 例中有 13 例(48.1%)成功建立了类器官。与囊性类型相比,固体型类器官与较短的 PFS 显着相关(HR = 13.91;p = 0.0039,对数秩检验)。类器官的建立在老年患者(>70 岁)、HBV 感染患者以及 β-catenin 表达阳性的肿瘤中更为频繁。WES 分析表明,组织和相应类器官之间的体细胞 突变 谱和变异等位基因频率分布具有高度一致性。在单变量 Cox 回归中,类器官生长模式(实性与囊性)显示出与该探索性 队列研究 中的 PFS 显着相关(p = 0.0207)。患者来源的肝癌类器官保留了原始肿瘤的基因组和组织病理学特征。值得注意的是,实体形态与较短的 PFS 相关,表明类器官生长表型可以作为 HCC 肿瘤生物学行为的补充指标。鉴于 队列研究 规模不大且缺乏多变量分析,这些初步结果应谨慎解释,并需要进一步大规模验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肝癌仍然是全球主要的健康负担,死亡率 较高且 治疗 反应预测工具有限。源自患者的癌症类器官已成为有前景的临床前模型,可概括肿瘤异质性;然而,在肝细胞癌(HCC)中,已建立的类器官形态多样性的生物学意义仍然知之甚少。

对六对匹配的肿瘤类器官进行全外显子组测序 (WES),以评估基因组保真度。对类器官建立、生长特征和临床参数之间的关联进行了统计评估。使用 Kaplan-Meier 方法和单变量 Cox 比例风险回归分析无进展生存期 (PFS)。

27 例中有 13 例(48.1%)成功建立了类器官。与囊性类型相比,固体型类器官与较短的 PFS 显着相关(HR = 13.91;p = 0.0039,对数秩检验)。WES 分析表明,组织和相应类器官之间的体细胞 突变 谱和变异等位基因频率分布具有高度一致性。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,类器官生长表型有可能作为 HCC 肿瘤生物学行为的补充指标,有助于 治疗 反应预测和临床结果评估。
局限与证据强度
鉴于 队列研究 规模不大且缺乏多变量分析,这些初步结果应谨慎解释,并需要进一步大规模验证。由于探索性和样本量有限,证据强度中等。

Patient-Derived Liver Cancer Organoids Reflect Tumor Biology and Their Growth Phenotype Correlates with Clinical Outcomes

Authors: Yeon Su Kim, Yoon-Ha Go, Hyung Sun Kim, Jaehwi Seo, Dong Oh Kim, Dong-Youn Hwang, Wookyeom Yang, Jin Hong Lim, Jongman Yoo, 서재휘
Original abstract (English)
Liver cancer remains a major global health burden with high mortality and limited treatment response prediction tools. Patient-derived cancer organoids have emerged as promising preclinical models that recapitulate tumor heterogeneity; however, the biological significance of morphological diversity within established organoids remains poorly characterized in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). In this exploratory study, we investigated whether distinct organoid growth phenotypes reflect underlying tumor biology and correlate with clinical outcomes. We established liver cancer organoids from resected tumor tissues of 27 patients and analyzed their clinical, histological, and genomic characteristics. Organoids were classified morphologically into cystic and solid types. Whole exome sequencing (WES) was conducted on six matched tumor-organoid pairs to assess genomic fidelity. Associations between organoid establishment, growth characteristics, and clinical parameters were statistically evaluated. Progression-free survival (PFS) was analyzed using the Kaplan-Meier method and univariate Cox proportional hazards regression. Organoids were successfully established in 13 of 27 cases (48.1%). Solid-type organoids were significantly associated with shorter PFS compared to cystic types (HR = 13.91; p = 0.0039, log-rank test). Organoid establishment was more frequent in older patients (>70 years), those with HBV infection, and tumors with positive beta-catenin expression. WES analysis demonstrated high concordance in somatic mutation profiles and variant allele frequency distributions between tissues and corresponding organoids. In univariate Cox regression, organoid growth pattern (solid vs. cystic) showed a significant association with PFS within this exploratory cohort (p = 0.0207). Patient-derived liver cancer organoids preserved the genomic and histopathological features of the original tumors. Notably, solid morphology was associated with shorter PFS, suggesting that organoid growth phenotype may serve as a supplementary indicator of tumor biological behavior in HCC. Given the modest cohort size and the absence of multivariate analysis, these preliminary findings should be interpreted with caution and warrant further large-scale validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Liver cancer remains a major global health burden with high mortality and limited treatment response prediction tools. Patient-derived cancer organoids have emerged as promising preclinical models that recapitulate tumor heterogeneity; however, the biological significance of morphological diversity within established organoids remains poorly characterized in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC).

Organoids were classified morphologically into cystic and solid types. Organoid establishment was more frequent in older patients (>70 years), those with HBV infection, and tumors with positive beta-catenin expression.

喀麦隆 患病率 感染的系统回顾和荟萃分析

Prevalence of Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Cameroon: A Systematic Review and Meta‐Analysis
作者： Iya Maliki, Edward J. D. Webb, Rose Armelle Ada, Bryony Dawkins, Aaron G. Lim
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本系统回顾和荟萃分析重点关注喀麦隆的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染,旨在提供一般人群和亚人群中 HBV 患病率 的最新估计。
证据基础与方法
该研究检索了 Medline、Global Health、Embase、Web of Science、Cochrane Library、Africa Index Medicus 以及雅温得大学的《健康科学与疾病杂志》。其中包括 92 篇论文和 100 项报告 HBsAg 患病率 的研究。使用随机效应荟萃分析来生成汇总估计值,使用 I2 评估异质性,并使用 Joanna Briggs Institute 关键评估工具评估质量。
共识与结论
喀麦隆 HBV 总体 患病率 为 9.98%(95% CI 9.00-11.02),异质性较高(I2 = 98.10%)。患病率 在相对较低风险人群中较低(8.94%,95% CI 7.84–10.11),在相对较高风险人群中较高(12.70%,95% CI 10.28–15.32)。性别、地理区域、人口风险群体和研究设计被确定为异质性的主要来源。
争议与缺口
该研究指出 HBV 患病率 估计值存在高度异质性 (I2 = 98.10%),其中性别、地理区域、人群风险群体和研究设计是关键来源。没有报道具体的争议,但高度异质性表明 患病率 估计值在不同亚组和地区之间存在差异。
科研与实践启示
研究结果表明,喀麦隆迫切需要采取健康干预措施来阻止乙型肝炎病毒的传播,特别需要关注男性和 患病率 最高的地区。该研究强调了基于亚群体和地理差异的有针对性的公共卫生策略的重要性。

Prevalence of Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Cameroon: A Systematic Review and Meta‐Analysis

Authors: Iya Maliki, Edward J. D. Webb, Rose Armelle Ada, Bryony Dawkins, Aaron G. Lim
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a global public health problem, and many regions of Sub‐Saharan Africa are disproportionately affected, including Cameroon, where prevalence has previously been shown to be high. This study aimed to provide up‐to‐date estimates of HBV prevalence in Cameroon. We searched Medline, Global Health, Embase, Web of Science, Cochrane Library, Africa Index Medicus and the Journal of Health Science and Disease at the University of Yaounde. We included studies reporting HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) prevalence in the general population and subpopulations. A random‐effects meta‐analysis was used to generate pooled estimates of HBsAg prevalence, overall and for population subgroups including those considered at relatively higher‐risk and relatively lower‐risk of HBV infection. Heterogeneity was assessed using the index of heterogeneity ( I 2 ). Quality was assessed using the Joanna Briggs Institute critical appraisal tool. Out of 1236 records, 92 papers with 100 studies were included in the meta‐analysis. Overall prevalence of HBV in Cameroon was 9.98% (95% confidence interval [CI] 9.00–11.02) with high heterogeneity ( I 2 = 98.10% ) . The prevalences for relatively lower‐risk and higher‐risk populations were8.94% (95% CI 7.84–10.11) and 12.70% (95% CI 10.28–15.32), respectively. In subgroup analyses, we found that sex, geographical area (regions), population risk groups and study design were the main sources of heterogeneity. Overall, HBV prevalence remains high in Cameroon. Our findings suggest an urgent need for health interventions to halt the spread of the disease, with particular attention needed for males and regions with the highest prevalence.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review and meta-analysis focused on hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in Cameroon, aiming to provide up-to-date estimates of HBV prevalence in the general population and subpopulations.
Evidence base and method
The study searched Medline, Global Health, Embase, Web of Science, Cochrane Library, Africa Index Medicus, and the Journal of Health Science and Disease at the University of Yaounde. It included 92 papers with 100 studies reporting HBsAg prevalence. A random-effects meta-analysis was used to generate pooled estimates, with heterogeneity assessed using I2 and quality evaluated using the Joanna Briggs Institute critical appraisal tool.
Consensus and conclusions
The overall prevalence of HBV in Cameroon was 9.98% (95% CI 9.00–11.02), with high heterogeneity (I2 = 98.10%). Prevalence was lower in relatively lower-risk populations (8.94%, 95% CI 7.84–10.11) and higher in relatively higher-risk populations (12.70%, 95% CI 10.28–15.32). Sex, geographical area, population risk groups, and study design were identified as main sources of heterogeneity.
Controversies and gaps
The study noted high heterogeneity (I2 = 98.10%) in HBV prevalence estimates, with sex, geographical area, population risk groups, and study design as key sources. No specific controversies were reported, but the high heterogeneity suggests variability in prevalence estimates across subgroups and regions.
Research and practice implications
The findings suggest an urgent need for health interventions to halt the spread of HBV in Cameroon, with particular attention needed for males and regions with the highest prevalence. The study highlights the importance of targeted public health strategies based on subgroup and geographical differences.

泰国消除乙型肝炎国家指南:扩大 诊断 和 治疗 的分散卫生系统模式

National guideline for hepatitis B elimination in Thailand: a decentralized health system model for expanding diagnosis and treatment
作者： Phunchai Charatcharoenwitthaya, Tawesak Tanwandee, Pisit Tangkijvanich, Teerha Piratvisuth, Chatree Jullapetch, Cheewanan Lertpiriyasuwat, Pongtorn Chartpitak
摘要中文翻译尽管婴儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 持续普遍存在,但 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 仍然是泰国肝硬化和肝细胞癌的重要原因,特别是在 疫苗 实施之前出生的成年人中。本文总结了 2026 年泰国消除乙型病毒性肝炎国家指南,并提出了解决剩余疾病负担的卫生系统框架。该指南由多学科专家小组根据国际建议、同行评审文献、国家流行病学数据、实施研究和卫生经济分析中的证据制定。这些建议是使用 GRADE 框架制定的,并进行了调整,以便在泰国全民健康覆盖体系内实施。国家战略优先考虑对 1992 年之前出生的成年人进行出生 队列研究 HBsAg 筛查,简化基于证据的 治疗 资格,纳入非侵入性纤维化评估和病毒学标准,以及高遗传屏障核苷类似物的一线使用。预防母婴 传播 优先考虑 HBV DNA 引导的 抗病毒药物 预防,并为具有重大诊断限制的环境保留简化的实施途径。长期护理强调针对肝细胞癌的结构化监测、适当的专家转诊、保守的 治疗 停药标准、明确的再治疗阈值以及基于风险的半年超声 监测。与产前服务、艾滋病毒项目和初级保健设施的整合支持分散提供和连续性护理。泰国 2026 年乙型肝炎指南在公共资助的分散卫生系统内实施了基于证据的慢性乙型肝炎管理,并为在流行环境中扩大乙型肝炎 诊断 和 治疗 提供了可扩展的模型。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该范围研究了人类 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染、其慢性后遗症(肝硬化、肝细胞癌),以及如何通过分散的卫生系统模式在泰国消除 HBV 的核心问题,重点关注普遍婴儿 疫苗接种 实施之前出生的成年人。
证据基础与方法
该审查提出了 2026 年泰国消除病毒性乙型肝炎国家指南,该指南由多学科专家小组利用国际建议、同行评审文献、国家流行病学数据、实施研究和卫生经济分析中的证据制定,并使用 GRADE 框架制定并适应泰国全民健康覆盖体系的建议。
共识与结论
该指南优先考虑对 1992 年之前出生的成年人进行出生 队列研究 HBsAg 筛查,简化基于证据的 治疗 资格,纳入非侵入性纤维化评估和病毒学标准,一线使用高遗传屏障核苷(酸)类似物,HBV DNA 指导的 抗病毒药物 预防母婴 传播,结构化采用保守的 治疗 停药标准和基于风险的半年一次超声 监测 进行肝细胞癌长期护理。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有报告泰国分散的卫生系统区域的指南遵守情况存在异质性,也没有报告关于在诊断限制的情况下简化实施路径的有效性的相互矛盾的发现。
科研与实践启示
研究重点包括评估出生 队列研究 筛查和简化的 治疗 途径对 HBV 相关肝硬化和肝细胞癌 发病率 的现实影响,而实践意义则强调将 HBV 护理与产前服务、HIV 项目和初级保健设施相结合,以支持分散的交付和护理的连续性。

National guideline for hepatitis B elimination in Thailand: a decentralized health system model for expanding diagnosis and treatment

Authors: Phunchai Charatcharoenwitthaya, Tawesak Tanwandee, Pisit Tangkijvanich, Teerha Piratvisuth, Chatree Jullapetch, Cheewanan Lertpiriyasuwat, Pongtorn Chartpitak
Original abstract (English)
Despite sustained universal infant hepatitis B vaccination, chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains an important cause of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma in Thailand, particularly among adults born before vaccine implementation. This article summarizes the 2026 National Guideline on Elimination of Viral Hepatitis B in Thailand and presents a health-system framework for addressing the remaining disease burden. The guideline was developed by a multidisciplinary expert panel using evidence from international recommendations, peer-reviewed literature, national epidemiological data, implementation studies, and health economic analyses. The recommendations were formulated using the GRADE framework and adapted for implementation within Thailand's Universal Health Coverage system. The national strategy prioritizes birth-cohort HBsAg screening for adults born before 1992, simplified evidence-based treatment eligibility incorporating non-invasive fibrosis assessment and virological criteria, and first-line use of high genetic-barrier nucleos(t)ide analogs. Prevention of mother-to-child transmission prioritizes HBV DNA-guided antiviral prophylaxis, with simplified implementation pathways reserved for settings with substantial diagnostic constraints. Long-term care emphasizes structured monitoring, appropriate specialist referral, conservative treatment discontinuation criteria, defined retreatment thresholds, and risk-based semiannual ultrasound surveillance for hepatocellular carcinoma. Integration with antenatal services, HIV programs, and primary care facilities supports decentralized delivery and continuity of care. Thailand's 2026 hepatitis B guideline operationalizes evidence-based CHB management within a publicly financed decentralized health system and provides a scalable model for expanding hepatitis B diagnosis and treatment in endemic settings.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The scope examines human hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, its chronic sequelae (cirrhosis, hepatocellular carcinoma), and the central question of how to eliminate HBV in Thailand through a decentralized health system model, focusing on adults born before universal infant vaccination implementation.
Evidence base and method
The review presents the 2026 National Guideline on Elimination of Viral Hepatitis B in Thailand, developed by a multidisciplinary expert panel using evidence from international recommendations, peer-reviewed literature, national epidemiological data, implementation studies, and health economic analyses, with recommendations formulated using the GRADE framework and adapted for Thailand's Universal Health Coverage system.
Consensus and conclusions
The guideline prioritizes birth-cohort HBsAg screening for adults born before 1992, simplified evidence-based treatment eligibility incorporating non-invasive fibrosis assessment and virological criteria, first-line use of high genetic-barrier nucleos(t)ide analogs, HBV DNA-guided antiviral prophylaxis for prevention of mother-to-child transmission, structured long-term care with conservative treatment discontinuation criteria and risk-based semiannual ultrasound surveillance for hepatocellular carcinoma.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report heterogeneity in guideline adherence across Thailand's decentralized health system regions or conflicting findings regarding the effectiveness of simplified implementation pathways in settings with diagnostic constraints.
Research and practice implications
Research priorities include evaluating the real-world impact of birth-cohort screening and simplified treatment pathways on HBV-related cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma incidence, while practice implications emphasize integrating HBV care with antenatal services, HIV programs, and primary care facilities to support decentralized delivery and continuity of care.

乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性供体肾移植至 HBsAg 阴性受者的长期临床结果:现实世界研究的系统回顾和荟萃分析

Long-Term Clinical Outcomes of Kidney Transplantation from Hepatitis B Surface Antigen (HBsAg)-Positive Donors to HBsAg-Negative Recipients: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Real-World Studies
作者： Chengze Liang, Yun Luo, Saifu Yin, Turun Song, Tao Lin
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述探讨了乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性 (HBsAg+) 供体肾移植给 HBsAg 阴性受者的长期临床结果,重点关注感染风险、器官功能和生存结果。
证据基础与方法
对现实世界研究的系统回顾和荟萃分析,检索了截至 2023 年 8 月 20 日的 5 个数据库,其中包含 16 项研究。发病率 的置信区间为 95%,并使用比值比和风险比评估风险因素和生存率。
共识与结论
通过适当的 抗病毒药物 预防和谨慎的受体选择,HBsAg + 肾脏可以获得可接受的长期结果。3.60% 的受者发生供体源性 HBV 感染,其中 HBsAb 阴性和 HBcAb 阴性受者的风险较高。1-3 年和 5-10 年移植物的肝肾功能和患者存活率与对照组相当。
争议与缺口
供者源性 HBV 感染的风险需要仔细评估,特别是对于缺乏 HBsAb 或 HBcAb 的受者。虽然结果总体上是有利的,但该研究强调需要标准化的乙型肝炎预防方案以及对特定亚群的长期感染风险进行进一步研究。
科研与实践启示
研究结果支持在适当选择的受者中谨慎和选择性地使用 HBsAg 阳性肾脏并进行标准化 HBV 预防。未来的研究应侧重于优化预防策略并评估高风险接受群体的长期结果。

Long-Term Clinical Outcomes of Kidney Transplantation from Hepatitis B Surface Antigen (HBsAg)-Positive Donors to HBsAg-Negative Recipients: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Real-World Studies

Authors: Chengze Liang, Yun Luo, Saifu Yin, Turun Song, Tao Lin
Original abstract (English)
Background: The use of hepatitis B surface antigen-positive (HBsAg+) kidneys may expand the donor pool for transplantation. This study evaluated the incidence, risk factors, complications, organ function, and survival outcomes of transplants from HBsAg+ donors to HBsAg− recipients. Methods: A systematic search of five databases was conducted through 20 August 2023. Incidence was expressed with 95% confidence intervals (CIs); odds ratios (ORs) and hazard ratios (HRs) assessed risk factors and survival. Results: Sixteen studies were included. Donor-derived HBV infection occurred in 3.60% (21/583). Risk was higher in HBsAb-negative (OR: 6.67, 95% CI: 1.08–41.10) and HBcAb-negative recipients (OR: 12.96, 95% CI: 2.06–81.69). Abnormal liver function occurred in 35.18%, hepatitis in 3.07%, and hepatic failure in 0.84%. Liver and kidney function at 1–3 years were comparable between HBsAg+ and HBsAg− kidney recipients. Five- and ten-year graft survival (85.2%, 82.7%) and patient survival (93.0%, 91.4%) were similar to controls (p > 0.05). Conclusions: HBsAg+ kidneys can achieve acceptable long-term outcomes with appropriate antiviral prophylaxis and cautious recipient selection, though infection risk requires careful assessment. These findings supports the cautious and selective use of HBsAg-positive kidneys in appropriately selected recipients use and standardized HBV prophylaxis.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review examines the long-term clinical outcomes of kidney transplantation from hepatitis B surface antigen-positive (HBsAg+) donors to HBsAg-negative recipients, focusing on infection risk, organ function, and survival outcomes.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and meta-analysis of real-world studies, searching five databases through August 20, 2023, with 16 studies included. Incidence was reported with 95% confidence intervals, and risk factors and survival were assessed using odds ratios and hazard ratios.
Consensus and conclusions
HBsAg+ kidneys can achieve acceptable long-term outcomes with appropriate antiviral prophylaxis and cautious recipient selection. Donor-derived HBV infection occurred in 3.60% of recipients, with higher risk in HBsAb-negative and HBcAb-negative recipients. Liver and kidney function at 1–3 years and 5–10-year graft and patient survival were comparable to controls.
Controversies and gaps
The risk of donor-derived HBV infection requires careful assessment, particularly in recipients lacking HBsAb or HBcAb. While outcomes are generally favorable, the study highlights the need for standardized HBV prophylaxis protocols and further research on long-term infection risk in specific subpopulations.
Research and practice implications
The findings support the cautious and selective use of HBsAg-positive kidneys in appropriately selected recipients with standardized HBV prophylaxis. Future research should focus on optimizing prophylaxis strategies and assessing long-term outcomes in high-risk recipient groups.

慢性乙型肝炎 中的性功能障碍:病理生理学、临床负担和综合管理的全面回顾

Sexual dysfunction in chronic hepatitis B: a comprehensive review of pathophysiology, clinical burden, and integrated management
作者： Sahar Muzammal, Cheng-Run Song, Jiang-Nan Peng, Yong-Hong Wang, En-Qiang Chen, Peng Jiang-nan
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 中的性功能障碍 (SD),检查其病理生理学、临床负担以及跨疾病阶段和 治疗 背景的综合管理。
证据基础与方法
这是一篇叙述性综述,综合了 流行病学、临床负担、病理生理学机制以及 SD 在 CHB 中的 抗病毒药物 治疗 影响的证据。
共识与结论
CHB 中的 SD 尚未得到充分表征,并且未常规纳入临床管理。从机制上讲,它涉及生物脆弱性、心理压力源和生活方式决定因素之间的相互作用。核苷类似物并不总是对性功能产生不利影响,而基于干扰素的治疗方案可能通过疲劳和神经精神症状间接导致 SD。
争议与缺口
Reported prevalence estimates vary widely (8.6 to 63.1%) across heterogeneous HBV-related studies. Evidence on treatment effects remains limited, and the mechanisms and management of SD in CHB are not well-defined.
科研与实践启示
提出主动筛查、多维度评估、个体化干预、纵向监测等综合管理框架。未来的研究应优先考虑前瞻性纵向研究、性别特异性分析、标准化性功能工具以及将性健康作为 抗病毒药物 治疗试验中患者报告的终点。

Sexual dysfunction in chronic hepatitis B: a comprehensive review of pathophysiology, clinical burden, and integrated management

Authors: Sahar Muzammal, Cheng-Run Song, Jiang-Nan Peng, Yong-Hong Wang, En-Qiang Chen, Peng Jiang-nan
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) affects approximately 240 million people worldwide. Advances in antiviral therapy have shifted CHB management toward long-term, multidimensional care, in which sexual health is closely linked to psychological wellbeing, intimate relationships, and patient-clinician communication. However, sexual dysfunction (SD) in CHB remains insufficiently characterized and is not yet routinely integrated into clinical management. This narrative review synthesizes evidence focused on CHB regarding the epidemiology, clinical burden, pathophysiological mechanisms, and antiviral treatment implications of SD across disease stages and treatment contexts. Reported prevalence estimates vary widely, ranging from 8.6 to 63.1% across heterogeneous HBV-related studies. Mechanistically, SD in CHB appears to reflect interactions among biological vulnerability, psychological stressors, and lifestyle determinants. Evidence on treatment effects remains limited, but available data do not indicate a consistent adverse effect of nucleos(t)ide analogues on sexual function, whereas interferon-based regimens may indirectly contribute to SD through fatigue and neuropsychiatric symptoms. Accordingly, we propose an integrated management framework comprising proactive screening, multidimensional assessment, individualized intervention, and longitudinal monitoring. In practice, this framework emphasizes validated sexual function instruments, concurrent assessment of psychological distress, transmission concerns, closer monitoring of patients at higher risk, targeted symptom management, and coordinated multidisciplinary support when needed. Future research should prioritize prospective longitudinal studies, sex-specific analyses, standardized sexual function instruments, and inclusion of sexual health as a patient-reported endpoint in antiviral therapy trials to better define the burden, mechanisms, and management of SD in CHB.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on sexual dysfunction (SD) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB), examining its pathophysiology, clinical burden, and integrated management across disease stages and treatment contexts.
Evidence base and method
This is a narrative review that synthesizes evidence on the epidemiology, clinical burden, pathophysiological mechanisms, and antiviral treatment implications of SD in CHB.
Consensus and conclusions
SD in CHB is insufficiently characterized and not routinely integrated into clinical management. Mechanistically, it involves interactions among biological vulnerability, psychological stressors, and lifestyle determinants. Nucleos(t)ide analogues do not consistently adversely affect sexual function, while interferon-based regimens may indirectly contribute to SD through fatigue and neuropsychiatric symptoms.
Controversies and gaps
Reported prevalence estimates vary widely (8.6 to 63.1%) across heterogeneous HBV-related studies. Evidence on treatment effects remains limited, and the mechanisms and management of SD in CHB are not well-defined.
Research and practice implications
An integrated management framework is proposed, including proactive screening, multidimensional assessment, individualized intervention, and longitudinal monitoring. Future research should prioritize prospective longitudinal studies, sex-specific analyses, standardized sexual function instruments, and inclusion of sexual health as a patient-reported endpoint in antiviral therapy trials.

HIV 感染者停止含替诺福韦抗逆转录病毒治疗后的 乙型肝炎病毒 结果:系统评价和荟萃分析

Hepatitis B virus outcomes in people with HIV after cessation of tenofovir-containing antiretroviral therapy: a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Amir M. Mohareb, Bonolo Phinius, Motswedi Anderson, Shahin Lockman, Julie Bottero, George A. Yendewa, Arthur Y. Kim, Anders Boyd
摘要中文翻译背景 不含替诺福韦的抗逆转录病毒治疗(ART)方案存在与乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关的风险。随着这些方案使用频率的增加,临床医生和政策制定者需要准确评估停用替诺福韦后HBV结局的风险。方法 我们对停用替诺福韦的HIV患者中HBV再激活和感染情况进行了系统综述。我们检索了Medline、Embase、Google Scholar和Cochrane数据库(2006年1月1日至2026年2月15日),查找与HIV患者停用替诺福韦相关的研究,包括(1)活动性HBV:HBV表面抗原(HBsAg)阳性;(2)HBV已 resolved:HBV核心抗体(HBcAb)阳性但HBsAg阴性;(3)HBV易感:HBsAg、HBcAb和HBV表面抗体均为阴性。我们使用随机效应模型计算停用替诺福韦后HBV再激活(在HBV已 resolved者中)和新HBV感染(在HBV易感者中)的汇总发病率率。我们使用多变量meta-regression比较不同亚组的汇总结局。我们使用I2评估研究间异质性,并使用Egger's test检验发表偏倚。本综述已在Prospero注册(#2025-CRD420251080460)。结果 在144篇摘要和文章中,53篇进行了全文审查,纳入22项独特的观察性研究。在8项活动性HBV感染研究中,仅能获取描述性数据而无法进行meta分析:大多数研究显示,在停用或中断替诺福韦后,HBV DNA增加或肝炎发作常见。在6项HBV已 resolved且在停用替诺福韦后积极监测HBV血清学标志物的观察性研究中,HBV再激活的汇总发病率率为3.79/100人年(95% CI 1.98-7.23;I2 = 57.6%;中等确定性)。这高于未积极监测HBV再激活研究的汇总发病率率(0.35/100人年,95% CI 0.12-1.03;p < 0.001)。6项观察性研究报告了易感人群在转为不含替诺福韦的ART后发生新HBV感染的情况(汇总发病率 = 1.63/100人年,95%CI = 0.27-9.92;I2 = 78.6%;极低确定性)。Egger's test显示无发表偏倚的统计学显著证据(p = 0.216)。解释 在HIV患者中,不含替诺福韦的ART与HBV再激活和感染相关。需要更高质量的前瞻性研究来评估停用替诺福韦的HIV患者。资助 美国国立卫生研究院和哈佛大学艾滋病研究中心。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这项系统评价和荟萃分析检查了 HIV 感染者停止含替诺福韦的抗逆转录病毒治疗 (ART) 后的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 结局。核心问题是评估不同 HBV 血清学状态组中替诺福韦停药后 HBV 再激活和新 HBV 感染的风险:活动性 HBV(HBsAg 阳性)、已治愈 HBV(HBcAb 阳性,无 HBsAg)和 HBV 易感性(HBsAg、HBcAb 和 HBV 表面抗体阴性)。
证据基础与方法
这是在 Prospero 注册的系统评价和荟萃分析 (#2025-CRD420251080460)。作者从 2006 年 1 月 1 日到 2026 年 2 月 15 日检索了 Medline、Embase、Google Scholar 和 Cochrane 数据库。他们从经过全文审查的 144 篇摘要和文章中纳入了 22 项独特的观察性研究。使用随机效应模型来计算汇总的 发病率 率,并采用多变量元回归进行亚组比较。使用 I2 评估异质性,并使用 Egger 检验测试发表偏倚。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,保留替诺福韦的 ART 与 HIV 感染者的 HBV 再激活和感染风险相关。对于在主动 监测 下 HBV 感染得到缓解的患者,HBV 再激活的汇总 发病率 为 3.79/100 人年(95% CI 1.98-7.23;中等确定性),显着高于未主动监测的研究(0.35/100 人年,p < 0.001)。在易感个体中,新 HBV 感染的汇总 发病率 为 1.63/100 人年(95% CI 0.27-9.92;确定性非常低)。未检测到显着的发表偏倚(p = 0.216)。
争议与缺口
该评价发现汇总估计值存在中度至高度异质性(已解决的 HBV 再激活 I2 = 57.6%,新 HBV 感染 I2 = 78.6%),表明研究之间存在差异。新的乙肝病毒感染证据的质量被评为非常低。作者指出,在没有荟萃分析的情况下,只能检索活动性 HBV 感染的描述性数据,并且需要对停止替诺福韦的 HIV 感染者进行更高质量的前瞻性研究。
科研与实践启示
研究结果强调,在停用替诺福韦后需要仔细监测 HBV 血清学标志物,特别是对于 HBV 感染已解决的个体。临床医生和政策制定者在使用保留替诺福韦的 ART 方案时应考虑 HBV 再激活和感染的风险。未来的研究应侧重于前瞻性研究,以更好地了解和减轻该人群的这些风险。

Hepatitis B virus outcomes in people with HIV after cessation of tenofovir-containing antiretroviral therapy: a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Amir M. Mohareb, Bonolo Phinius, Motswedi Anderson, Shahin Lockman, Julie Bottero, George A. Yendewa, Arthur Y. Kim, Anders Boyd
Original abstract (English)
Background Antiretroviral therapy (ART) regimens without tenofovir have risks related to hepatitis B virus (HBV). As these regimens are being used more frequently, clinicians and policy makers would benefit from an accurate estimate of the risk of HBV outcomes following tenofovir cessation.Methods We conducted a systematic review of HBV reactivation and infection in people with HIV who discontinue tenofovir. We searched Medline, Embase, Google Scholar, and the Cochrane database (1 January 2006-15 February 2026) for studies related to tenofovir cessation in people with HIV and (1) active HBV: positive HBV surface antigen (HBsAg); (2) resolved HBV: positive HBV core antibody (HBcAb) without HBsAg; and (3) susceptibility to HBV: negative HBsAg, HBcAb, and HBV surface antibody. We used a random effects model to calculate the pooled incidence rate of HBV reactivation (in resolved HBV) and new HBV infection (in those susceptible to HBV) after tenofovir cessation. We used multivariable meta-regression to compare pooled outcomes across subgroups. We used I2 to assess inter-study heterogeneity, and we tested for publication bias using Egger's test. This review is registered with Prospero (#2025-CRD420251080460).Findings Of 144 abstracts and articles, 53 underwent full-text review, and 22 unique observational studies were included. In eight studies of active HBV infection, only descriptive data could be retrieved without a meta-analysis: HBV DNA increase or hepatitis flares commonly occurred in most studies after tenofovir cessation or interruption. In six observational studies of resolved HBV infection with active surveillance of HBV serological markers after tenofovir cessation, pooled incidence of HBV reactivation was 3.79/100 person-years (95% CI 1.98-7.23; I2 = 57.6%; moderate certainty). This was higher than the pooled incidence in studies without active monitoring for HBV reactivation (0.35/100 person-years, 95% CI 0.12-1.03; p < 0.001). Six observational studies reported new HBV infections in susceptible people following transition to tenofovir-sparing ART (pooled incidence = 1.63/100 person-years, 95%CI = 0.27-9.92; I2 = 78.6%; very low certainty). Egger's test showed no statistically significant evidence of publication bias (p = 0.216).Interpretation In people with HIV, tenofovir-sparing ART is associated with risks related to HBV reactivation and infection. Higher quality prospective studies are needed in people with HIV who stop tenofovir.Funding National Institutes of Health and Harvard University Center for AIDS Research.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review and meta-analysis examines hepatitis B virus (HBV) outcomes in people with HIV after cessation of tenofovir-containing antiretroviral therapy (ART). The central question is to estimate the risk of HBV reactivation and new HBV infection following tenofovir discontinuation in different HBV serological status groups: active HBV (HBsAg positive), resolved HBV (HBcAb positive without HBsAg), and HBV susceptibility (negative for HBsAg, HBcAb, and HBV surface antibody).
Evidence base and method
This is a systematic review and meta-analysis registered with Prospero (#2025-CRD420251080460). The authors searched Medline, Embase, Google Scholar, and the Cochrane database from January 1, 2006, to February 15, 2026. They included 22 unique observational studies out of 144 abstracts and articles that underwent full-text review. A random effects model was used to calculate pooled incidence rates, and multivariable meta-regression was employed for subgroup comparisons. Heterogeneity was assessed using I2, and publication bias was tested using Egger's test.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that tenofovir-sparing ART is associated with risks related to HBV reactivation and infection in people with HIV. For those with resolved HBV infection under active surveillance, the pooled incidence of HBV reactivation was 3.79/100 person-years (95% CI 1.98-7.23; moderate certainty), which was significantly higher than in studies without active monitoring (0.35/100 person-years, p < 0.001). In susceptible individuals, the pooled incidence of new HBV infection was 1.63/100 person-years (95% CI 0.27-9.92; very low certainty). No significant publication bias was detected (p = 0.216).
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies moderate to high heterogeneity in the pooled estimates (I2 = 57.6% for resolved HBV reactivation and I2 = 78.6% for new HBV infection), indicating variability across studies. The certainty of evidence for new HBV infections was rated as very low. The authors note that only descriptive data could be retrieved for active HBV infection without meta-analysis, and higher quality prospective studies are needed in people with HIV who stop tenofovir.
Research and practice implications
The findings highlight the need for careful monitoring of HBV serological markers after tenofovir cessation, particularly in individuals with resolved HBV infection. Clinicians and policy makers should consider the risks of HBV reactivation and infection when using tenofovir-sparing ART regimens. Future research should focus on prospective studies to better understand and mitigate these risks in this population.

🚨 突发动态与新闻 / Health News

Published: 2026-08-01  |  Publisher: Jamaica Information Service
A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

医务人员敦促预防乙型肝炎 - 牙买加信息服务

Medical Officer Urges Precaution Against Hepatitis B - Jamaica Information Service
新闻简报牙买加威斯特摩兰卫生官员马西娅·格雷厄姆博士敦促居民对乙型肝炎采取预防措施,恰逢 7 月 28 日世界肝炎日。格雷厄姆强调该病毒的高传染性——它可以在体外存活长达一周,并且比艾滋病毒更具传染性——格雷厄姆强调 疫苗接种 和安全性行为是关键的保护措施。由于血液供应仍然安全,许多病例是通过献血检测发现的。乙型肝炎会攻击肝脏,如果不治疗可能导致慢性疾病或癌症,牙买加正在通过新生儿 疫苗接种 和针对高危人群的成人免疫接种来应对乙型肝炎。没有提供 暴发 数据,但格雷厄姆还指出下呼吸道感染有所增加,建议在医疗机构中佩戴口罩。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 8 月 1 日。威斯特摩兰市政公司最近举行的月度会议期间提到了这一事件,并与 7 月 28 日的世界肝炎日相关的认识。
地点与对象
牙买加威斯特摩兰。该教区的居民是受影响的人口。
事件
威斯特摩兰卫生官员 Marcia Graham 博士在一次市政会议上敦促居民采取预防乙型肝炎的措施,包括 疫苗接种 和安全性行为。
规模、影响与风险
没有报告具体病例数或死亡数字。乙型肝炎被描述为具有高度传染性,在体外可存活长达一周,比艾滋病毒更具传染性。性接触是最常见的传播途径,许多病例是通过献血检测发现的。
应对、状态与不确定性
牙买加正在通过新生儿 疫苗接种 和高危成人免疫接种加强保护。除 疫苗接种 和宣传活动外,没有提及 暴发 调查或具体应对措施。来源是牙买加信息服务处的完整原始报告。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Medical Officer Urges Precaution Against Hepatitis B - Jamaica Information Service

News Brief
Dr. Marcia Graham, Medical Officer of Health for Westmoreland, Jamaica, has urged residents to take preventive measures against hepatitis B, coinciding with World Hepatitis Day on July 28. Highlighting the virus's high infectivity—it can survive outside the body for up to a week and is more transmissible than HIV—Graham emphasized vaccination and safe sex as key protections. Many cases are detected through blood donation testing, as the blood supply remains safe. Hepatitis B, which attacks the liver and can lead to chronic disease or cancer if untreated, is being countered in Jamaica through newborn vaccinations and adult immunization for high-risk groups. No outbreak figures were provided, but Graham also noted an increase in lower respiratory tract infections, advising mask-wearing in healthcare settings.
Original source text (English)

Hurricane Beryl Relief & Recovery Emancipendence 2026 Search for:Search Button Medical Officer Urges Precaution Against Hepatitis B By: Serena Grant, August 1, 2026 Listen Education Share Photo: Serena Grant Medical Officer of Health for Westmoreland, Dr. Marcia Graham. The Full Story Residents of Westmoreland are being encouraged to take steps to protect themselves against hepatitis B by getting vaccinated and practising safe sex. Medical Officer of Health for Westmoreland, Dr. Marcia Graham, made the call against the backdrop of World Hepatitis Day, which was observed on July 28. Speaking during the recent monthly meeting of the Westmoreland Municipal Corporation, Dr. Graham said the observance provided an opportunity to raise awareness about a disease that often receives less attention than HIV despite being more infectious. She noted that hepatitis B can survive outside the body for up to a week, making it highly transmissible. She noted that while sexual contact remains the most common means of transmission, many people only discover they are infected after donating blood. “Our blood supply chain is safe... a lot of times that’s how we alert persons that they have hepatitis B because when they donate blood and we test it, it comes back positive,” she pointed out. Hepatitis B is a viral infection that attacks the liver and can lead to chronic liver disease, cirrhosis and liver cancer if left untreated. The virus is spread through contact with infected blood and other body fluids and can be prevented through vaccination, safe health practices and responsible sexual behaviour. Dr. Graham said Jamaica continues to strengthen protection against the disease through newborn vaccination and immunisation for adults at high risk. Meanwhile, she reported an increase in the number of persons admitted with lower respiratory tract infection and encouraged mask-wearing in hospitals and health centres, especially when visiting vulnerable patients. She also reminded residents that proper cough etiquette remains an important infection-prevention measure. Last Updated: August 1, 2026 Post navigation Previous Article Next Article ×
View five news elements
Time
Published on August 1, 2026. Event referenced during the recent monthly meeting of the Westmoreland Municipal Corporation, with awareness tied to World Hepatitis Day observed on July 28.
Location and population
Westmoreland, Jamaica. Residents of the parish are the affected population.
Event
Medical Officer of Health for Westmoreland, Dr. Marcia Graham, urged residents to take precautionary measures against hepatitis B, including vaccination and safe sex practices, during a municipal meeting.
Scale, impact and risk
No specific case counts or death figures reported. Hepatitis B is described as highly infectious, surviving outside the body for up to a week, and more infectious than HIV. Sexual contact is the most common transmission route, with many cases discovered through blood donation testing.
Response, status and uncertainty
Jamaica is strengthening protection through newborn vaccination and immunization for high-risk adults. No outbreak investigation or specific response measures beyond vaccination and awareness campaigns are mentioned. The source is a full original report from the Jamaica Information Service.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

评估 SOF/VEL 治疗 和 TAF 前 治疗 在 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒/丙型肝炎病毒合并感染患者中的安全性和有效性 - EurekAlert!

Evaluation of the safety and efficacy of SOF/VEL treatment and pre-treatment of TAF in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus/hepatitis C virus coinfection - EurekAlert!
新闻简报最近发表在《临床与转化肝病学杂志》上的一项研究评估了索磷布韦/维帕他韦 (SOF/VEL) 与 治疗 前的富马酸替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 对中国 13 个中心的 96 名慢性 HBV/HCV 双重感染患者的安全性和有效性。为期 12 周的试验取得了 97.9% 的总体持续病毒学应答,其中代偿性肝硬化患者的持续病毒学应答率为 100%。尽管两名非肝硬化患者经历了 HBV 再激活或 HCV 复发,但观察到肝功能和纤维化指数显着改善。没有报告与药物相关的不良事件。该研究表明 TAF pre-治疗 可能会降低 HBV 再激活风险,这凸显了针对该合并感染人群的一种有前景的治疗方法。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 8 月 6 日。该研究于2021年5月至2024年12月进行,为期12周的治疗阶段,随后是12/48周的观察期。
地点与对象
中国,参与者来自 13 个中心。该研究包括 96 名患有慢性 HBV/HCV 合并感染的成年人:77 名非肝硬化患者(第 1 组)和 19 名代偿性肝硬化患者(第 2 组)。
事件
一项多中心、前瞻性、单臂、开放标签试验,评估索磷布韦/维帕他韦 (SOF/VEL) 与富马酸替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 预 治疗 在慢性 HBV/HCV 合并感染患者中的安全性和有效性。该研究得到证实并发表在《临床和转化肝病学杂志》上。
规模、影响与风险
总体持续病毒学应答为 97.9%(第 2 组为 100%)。所有患者的 HCV RNA、HBV DNA 和 HBV RNA 水平均显着下降。第 2 组的肝功能标志物有所改善,非侵袭性纤维化指数略有改善。第 1 组的两名患者分别经历了 HBV 再激活和 HCV 复发。没有报告与药物相关的不良事件。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究得出的结论是,SOF/VEL 是有效的,且安全性可接受,并且预给予 TAF 可以降低 HBV 再激活风险。来源是 EurekAlert! 的完整原始报告,由信誉良好的科学出版商(Xia & He Publishing Inc.)出版,内容经过同行评审。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Evaluation of the safety and efficacy of SOF/VEL treatment and pre-treatment of TAF in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus/hepatitis C virus coinfection - EurekAlert!

News Brief
A recent study published in the Journal of Clinical and Translational Hepatology evaluated the safety and efficacy of sofosbuvir/velpatasvir (SOF/VEL) with pre-treatment of tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in 96 chronic HBV/HCV coinfected patients across 13 centers in China. The 12-week trial achieved a 97.9% overall sustained virologic response, with 100% in compensated cirrhotic patients. Significant improvements in liver function and fibrosis indices were observed, though two non-cirrhotic patients experienced HBV reactivation or HCV relapse. No drug-related adverse events were reported. The study suggests TAF pre-treatment may reduce HBV reactivation risk, highlighting a promising therapeutic approach for this coinfected population.
Original source text (English)
Home Article Highlight | 6-Aug-2026 Evaluation of the safety and efficacy of SOF/VEL treatment and pre-treatment of TAF in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus/hepatitis C virus coinfection Xia & He Publishing Inc. Facebook X LinkedIn WeChat Bluesky Message WhatsApp Email image: SOF/VEL effectively achieves a sustained virologic response and improves liver function, with an acceptable safety profile in chronic HBV/HCV coinfected patients, including those with compensated cirrhosis, who achieved modest improvement in non-invasive fibrosis indices. Pre-administration of TAF may mitigates the risk of HBV reactivation in this population. view more Credit: Xiaoyuan Xu, Yanyan Yu Background and Aims Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection and hepatitis C virus (HCV) infection are among the leading causes of chronic liver diseases worldwide. Through the same transmission routes, HBV/HCV coinfection is widespread and aggravates liver damage. In this study, we aimed to assess the safety and efficacy of sofosbuvir/velpatasvir (SOF/VEL) and the pre-treatment of tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) on HBV reactivation in HBV/HCV coinfected patients. Methods A multicenter, prospective, single-arm, open-label 12-week trial, followed by a 12/48-week observational clinical trial, was conducted. Ninety-six adults with chronic HBV/HCV coinfection were enrolled from May 2021 to December 2024 in thirteen centers in China. Seventy-seven non-cirrhotic patients were included in Group 1 and nineteen compensated cirrhotic patients in Group 2. All subjects were enrolled to receive SOF/VEL once daily for 12 weeks. Non-cirrhotic subjects received TAF once daily for 28 weeks, and compensated cirrhotic subjects received TAF once daily for 64 weeks simultaneously. Statistical significance was set at P < 0.05. Results At the end of SOF/VEL treatment, the overall sustained virologic response was 97.9%, of which 100% was achieved in Group 2. HCV RNA, HBV DNA, and HBV RNA levels were substantially decreased in all patients. Alanine aminotransferase (ALT) (61.5 vs. 21.9, P < 0.001) and aspartate aminotransferase (AST) (50.8 vs. 25.7, P < 0.001) levels decreased, and albumin (ALB) (42.4 vs. 45.1, P < 0.001) level increased compared to pre-treatment in Group 1 at 12 weeks post-treatment. ALT (64.1 vs. 25.2, P < 0.001), AST (65.7 vs. 29.7, P < 0.001), alkaline phosphatase (ALP) (111.6 vs. 88.2, P < 0.05), and alpha-fetoprotein (AFP) (17.9 vs. 4.7, P < 0.05) levels decreased, and ALB (41.3 vs. 42.5, P = 0.051) and platelet count (PLT) (114.0 vs. 127.2, P = 0.052) levels showed a trend toward increase compared to pre-treatment in Group 2 at 48 weeks post-treatment. Liver stiffness measurement (LSM) (22.6 vs. 12.7, P < 0.01), aspartate aminotransferase to platelet ratio index (APRI) (1.6 vs. 0.6, P < 0.001), and fibrosis-4 index (FIB-4) (4.7 vs. 2.6, P < 0.05) significantly decreased after treatment in Group 2. Two patients in Group 1 with genotype 3 showed HBV reactivation and HCV relapse, respectively. No drug-related adverse events were observed in the study. Conclusions SOF/VEL effectively achieves a sustained virologic response and improves liver function, with an acceptable safety profile in chronic HBV/HCV coinfected patients, including those with compensated cirrhosis, who achieved modest improvement in non-invasive fibrosis indices. Pre-administration of TAF may mitigates the risk of HBV reactivation in this population. Full text https://www.xiahepublishing.com/2310-8819/JCTH-2026-00168 The study was recently published in the Journal of Clinical and Translational Hepatology. Time
Published on 6-Aug-2026. The study was conducted from May 2021 to December 2024, with a 12-week treatment phase followed by a 12/48-week observational period.
Location and population
China, with participants enrolled from thirteen centers. The study included 96 adults with chronic HBV/HCV coinfection: 77 non-cirrhotic patients (Group 1) and 19 compensated cirrhotic patients (Group 2).
Event
A multicenter, prospective, single-arm, open-label trial evaluating the safety and efficacy of sofosbuvir/velpatasvir (SOF/VEL) with pre-treatment of tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic HBV/HCV coinfected patients. The study is confirmed and published in the Journal of Clinical and Translational Hepatology.
Scale, impact and risk
Overall sustained virologic response was 97.9% (100% in Group 2). HCV RNA, HBV DNA, and HBV RNA levels decreased significantly in all patients. Liver function markers improved, and non-invasive fibrosis indices showed modest improvement in Group 2. Two patients in Group 1 experienced HBV reactivation and HCV relapse, respectively. No drug-related adverse events were reported.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study concluded that SOF/VEL is effective with an acceptable safety profile, and pre-administration of TAF may mitigate HBV reactivation risk. The source is a full original report from EurekAlert!, published by a reputable scientific publisher (Xia & He Publishing Inc.) with peer-reviewed content.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

我没有感觉不舒服,但我患有乙型肝炎:与隐藏病毒的斗争 - Daily Nation

I wasn't feeling sick, but I had Hepatitis B: The fight against a hiding virus - Daily Nation
新闻简报A 29-year-old Kenyan woman, Night Sheritan, was unexpectedly diagnosed with hepatitis B during routine antenatal screening despite showing no symptoms, illustrating the virus's 'silent' nature. Globally, 254 million people live with chronic hepatitis B, causing 1.1 million deaths annually, with only 13% aware of their status and fewer than 5% receiving treatment. In Kenya, county-level prevalence varies significantly, with Turkana at 16.8%, Baringo at 11.9%, and Migori at 10%. The diagnosis highlights critical gaps in screening beyond pregnant women and the urgent need for expanded testing and treatment to prevent severe liver damage and cancer.
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-08-03。事件日期:未明确说明,但 诊断 发生在怀孕期间(产前门诊就诊)。
地点与对象
地点:肯尼亚,特别是内罗毕(科马罗克)以及图尔卡纳、巴林戈和米戈里等县。受影响人群:孕妇(常规筛查组)、未确诊慢性乙型肝炎的一般人群。
事件
一名 29 岁孕妇 (Night Sheritan) 在常规产前筛查中确诊为 诊断 乙型肝炎。诊断 出人意料,因为她没有任何症状,这凸显了乙型肝炎的“无声感染”性质。
规模、影响与风险
Global: 254 million people living with chronic hepatitis B in 2024; 1.1 million annual deaths; 83% of viral hepatitis deaths. Kenya: National average prevalence not stated, but Turkana (16.8%), Baringo (11.9%), and Migori (10%) have higher rates. Only 13% of global cases are diagnosed; fewer than 5% of diagnosed receive treatment. High risk of liver cirrhosis and cancer, especially in sub-Saharan Africa (60-80% of liver cancer cases linked to HBV).
应对、状态与不确定性
回应:在肯尼亚,对孕妇进行例行筛查是强制性的。验证测试是在私人实验室进行的。患者的丈夫接受了检测,结果呈阴性。不确定性:来源限制不适用(完整内容)。没有关于更广泛的公共卫生应对措施、治疗 获取或孕妇筛查以外的预防计划的信息。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

I wasn't feeling sick, but I had Hepatitis B: The fight against a hiding virus - Daily Nation

News Brief
A 29-year-old Kenyan woman, Night Sheritan, was unexpectedly diagnosed with hepatitis B during routine antenatal screening despite showing no symptoms, illustrating the virus's 'silent' nature. Globally, 254 million people live with chronic hepatitis B, causing 1.1 million deaths annually, with only 13% aware of their status and fewer than 5% receiving treatment. In Kenya, county-level prevalence varies significantly, with Turkana at 16.8%, Baringo at 11.9%, and Migori at 10%. The diagnosis highlights critical gaps in screening beyond pregnant women and the urgent need for expanded testing and treatment to prevent severe liver damage and cancer.

Renew subscription Maybe later Subscribe for a month to get full access I wasn't feeling sick, but I had Hepatitis B: The fight against a hiding virus Monday, August 03, 2026 29-year-old Night Sheritan holding her 10-month old baby at her home in Komarock, Nairobi. Photo credit: Pool By Hellen Aura What you need to know: Only 13 per cent of people living with chronic hepatitis B globally know their status. The remaining 87 per cent are walking around undiagnosed, transmitting the virus without knowing it, accumulating liver damage without feeling it. Fewer than five per cent of those diagnosed are receiving treatment. Night Sheritan walked into the antenatal clinic when she was four months pregnant, expecting routine reassurance. She and her husband had been preparing for their third child with the particular quietness of people who have done this before, discussing names, slowly rearranging the house, imagining what a third child would add to a family that already felt complete. The clinic visit was a formality; a tick on the calendar. Then the blood test came back. "Night, you have tested positive for hepatitis B." She asked the doctor to repeat the test in her presence. The second result also came back positive. "I felt numb," she says. "I didn't even know what hepatitis B really was." Also read: Prevention and treatment of Hepatitis B She had no symptoms; no jaundice, no persistent fatigue, none of the signs associated with liver disease. She considered herself healthy. The diagnosis arrived not through illness but through a routine screening, the kind that most Kenyans never have; the test is typically offered only to expectant mothers. "I couldn't understand how I could have hepatitis B when I wasn't sick," she says. Her confusion is one of the most common responses doctors hear. Hepatitis B is called a "silent infection" because the majority of people who carry the virus have no idea. It causes no immediate pain, no obvious symptoms, no signal that something is wrong, while the virus quietly damages the liver over years and decades. By the time symptoms appear, the damage is often severe; by the time many people test, it is already late. Hepatitis B is a viral infection that attacks the liver. The World Health Organization classifies it as one of the most serious global public health threats, responsible for approximately 1.1 million deaths every year, primarily from liver cirrhosis and liver cancer. It accounts for 83 per cent of all viral hepatitis deaths worldwide. In 2024, an estimated 254 million people globally were living with chronic hepatitis B. Africa carries 68 per cent of all new chronic infections despite having a fraction of the world's healthcare resources to respond to them. In sub-Saharan Africa, between 60 and 80 per cent of all liver cancer cases are linked to hepatitis B. The virus is transmitted through contact with infected blood or body fluids, most commonly from mother to child during birth, through early childhood exposure within households, unprotected sexual contact with an infected partner, and unsafe injections or sharp instruments. In Kenya, mother-to-child transmission is among the most significant routes, particularly because it is the most preventable, if caught in time. Only 13 per cent of people living with chronic hepatitis B globally know their status. The remaining 87 per cent are walking around undiagnosed, transmitting the virus without knowing it, accumulating liver damage without feeling it. Fewer than five per cent of those diagnosed are receiving treatment. Sheritan drove home from the clinic that afternoon carrying something heavy and nameless. "I kept asking myself where I got it from. I was worried that people would judge me." She was afraid to tell her husband, not because she did not trust him, but because she did not know how to explain something she did not yet understand herself. She feared he would think she had been unfaithful. She sat with the diagnosis alone for a day before she could bring herself to tell him. "He was shocked, but instead of blaming me, he comforted me. He told me we would face it together." Their healthcare provider recommended that her husband be tested. When his results arrived, he was negative, which meant Sheritan had likely been living with the virus undetected for years, simply because she had never been screened. "I asked myself, what if he thought I was unfaithful? Yet I myself did not know how I contracted it." This is one of hepatitis B's cruelest features. Because transmission can occur at birth or in early childhood through household contact, shared items, or maternal transmission, people can carry the virus for decades without any sexual or behavioural exposure. Yet the stigma associated with the diagnosis always lands on the person carrying it. According to the Ministry of Health, approximately 1.5 million Kenyans are living with hepatitis B. The national prevalence is around three per cent, but that figure masks significant geographic variation that existing data is only beginning to capture. The highest regional prevalence is in North Eastern Kenya at 7.6 per cent, followed by Rift Valley North at five per cent and Nyanza at 4.6 per cent. At the county level, the picture is more alarming: Turkana has a reported prevalence of 16.8 per cent, more than five times the national average, while Baringo sits at 11.9 per cent and Migori at ten per cent. Also read: Growing liver cancer burden linked to Hepatitis B amid vaccine shortage Dr Khadija Hassan, manager of the National AIDS and STI Control Programme at the Ministry of Health, is direct about the gap Kenya faces. "It is easy to test pregnant women because we included mandatory screening among pregnant women," she says. "But many people in Kenya do not know they are living with the virus, and some test when it is already late." The data that exists at county level is incomplete. Many counties have no systematic hepatitis B data outside antenatal programmes. The true burden,particularly among men and non-pregnant women, is almost certainly larger than current figures suggest because the people who would have been counted are not being tested. Sheritan was referred to Kenyatta National Hospital after her initial diagnosis. The next step was a series of confirmatory tests at a private laboratory. "I paid Sh21,000 for the tests, and that was a lot of money for me." Her viral load turned out to be low enough that she did not need to begin antiviral medication. But she would need monitoring every three months—liver function tests, viral load checks, a regular relationship with a specialist that would continue indefinitely. Then came the question of her unborn child. Without intervention, 70 to 90 per cent of babies born to highly infectious hepatitis B-positive mothers contract the virus during delivery. With timely vaccination, specifically a birth dose of the hepatitis B vaccine administered within 24 hours of delivery, combined with Hepatitis B Immune Globulin where indicated, that transmission rate drops to below ten per cent. The vaccine was administered immediately after delivery. Her baby is now ten months old, healthy, and not infected. "The vaccine is the best assurance to protect against mother-to-child transmission of hepatitis B," Dr Hassan says. "Without treatment, 70 to 90 per cent of babies born to highly infectious mothers become infected. With timely birth-dose vaccination and appropriate treatment, transmission drops to below 10 per cent." The cost of that protection is Sh35,000 at a private facility, unavailable through the public system. It is a structural gap that sits directly in the path of every hepatitis B-positive mother who delivers at a public hospital in Kenya without knowing whether the vaccine her child needs will be there. Sheritan's virus is what doctors call inactive, or sleeping. She is not on medication, but she is not free of the virus either. She attends clinic every three months for monitoring that will continue indefinitely. "I was told my virus is asleep. I still need to go for regular screening." Dr Hassan explains: "The reason we tell patients to go for regular check-ups even when their virus is not active is to prevent or catch the early stages when it gets active, to prevent severe illness." She encourages every couple planning a pregnancy to know their hepatitis B status before conception. Login to join the discussion Please enable JavaScript to view the comments powered by Disqus. Confirmed diagnosis of hepatitis B in a 29-year-old pregnant woman (Night Sheritan) during routine antenatal screening. The diagnosis was unexpected as she had no symptoms, highlighting the 'silent infection' nature of hepatitis B.

Global: 254 million people living with chronic hepatitis B in 2024; 1.1 million annual deaths; 83% of viral hepatitis deaths. Only 13% of global cases are diagnosed; fewer than 5% of diagnosed receive treatment. High risk of liver cirrhosis and cancer, especially in sub-Saharan Africa (60-80% of liver cancer cases linked to HBV).

Response: Routine screening for pregnant women is mandatory in Kenya. Confirmatory testing was conducted at a private laboratory. Patient's husband was tested and found negative. 全国性研究追踪英夫利昔单抗 治疗 期间乙型肝炎再激活和肝功能障碍 - Bioengineer.org

Nationwide Study Tracks Hepatitis B Reactivation and Liver Dysfunction During Infliximab Treatment - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A nationwide study conducted in China tracked hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation and liver dysfunction among patients with inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) receiving infliximab treatment. Published on August 1, 2026, by Bioengineer.org, the study found that HBV reactivation can occur in patients with chronic HBV infection, previous exposure, or unknown status. It noted that China has an estimated 70 million chronic HBV carriers, making the issue particularly relevant. The research highlighted the risk of liver injury, including jaundice and acute liver failure, due to HBV reactivation. It emphasized the importance of HBV screening before biologic therapy and recommended regular laboratory monitoring and antiviral intervention when needed. The study aimed to reflect routine clinical practice rather than controlled trials, underscoring the need for a multidisciplinary approach to HBV safety in IBD patients.
查看新闻五要素
时间
一项在中国进行的全国性研究,发表日期为 2026 年 8 月 1 日。事件日期没有明确说明,但该研究追踪了英夫利昔单抗 治疗 期间的再激活和肝功能障碍。
地点与对象
中国,重点关注接受英夫利昔单抗 治疗 治疗的炎症性肠病 (IBD) 患者。该人群包括患有慢性 HBV 感染、既往接触过或感染状况未知的个体。
事件
一项全国性的真实世界研究检查了接受英夫利昔单抗 治疗 治疗的中国 IBD 患者的 HBV 感染、再激活和相关肝功能障碍。该研究于 2026 年 8 月 1 日由 Bioengineer.org 报道,并根据研究结果得到证实。
规模、影响与风险
The study quantified HBV reactivation in patients receiving infliximab and highlighted the risk of liver dysfunction, including jaundice, impaired hepatic function, and acute liver failure. It noted that China has an estimated 70 million chronic HBV carriers, making the issue particularly significant in this population.
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究强调了生物治疗前进行 HBV 筛查的重要性,并建议对 IBD 患者进行普遍筛查。它还强调了必要时定期实验室 监测 和 抗病毒药物 干预的必要性。研究结果基于真实世界的数据并反映了常规临床实践。

Nationwide Study Tracks Hepatitis B Reactivation and Liver Dysfunction During Infliximab Treatment - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A nationwide study conducted in China tracked hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation and liver dysfunction among patients with inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) receiving infliximab treatment. Published on August 1, 2026, by Bioengineer.org, the study found that HBV reactivation can occur in patients with chronic HBV infection, previous exposure, or unknown status. It noted that China has an estimated 70 million chronic HBV carriers, making the issue particularly relevant. The research highlighted the risk of liver injury, including jaundice and acute liver failure, due to HBV reactivation. It emphasized the importance of HBV screening before biologic therapy and recommended regular laboratory monitoring and antiviral intervention when needed. The study aimed to reflect routine clinical practice rather than controlled trials, underscoring the need for a multidisciplinary approach to HBV safety in IBD patients.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Friday, August 7, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Biology Nationwide Study Tracks Hepatitis B Reactivation and Liver Dysfunction During Infliximab Treatment by Bioengineer August 1, 2026 in Biology Reading Time: 4 mins read 0 Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Inflammatory bowel disease (IBD), an umbrella term for Crohn’s disease and ulcerative colitis, is becoming an increasingly significant public-health challenge worldwide. The burden is rising particularly rapidly in developing countries, including China, where changes in diet, urbanization, environmental exposures and improved diagnosis are contributing to a growing number of cases. For patients with moderate to severe IBD, biologic medicines have transformed treatment by suppressing the immune pathways that drive chronic intestinal inflammation. Among the most widely used of these medicines is infliximab, an antibody that blocks tumor necrosis factor-alpha (TNF-α), a central inflammatory signaling molecule. By inhibiting TNF-α, infliximab can reduce intestinal inflammation, promote mucosal healing and limit patients’ dependence on corticosteroids. In many cases, treatment also lowers the likelihood of hospitalization and surgery. However, TNF-α has important functions in host defense, particularly in the control of intracellular pathogens and the maintenance of immune surveillance. Suppressing this pathway can therefore create an opportunity for otherwise controlled infections to re-emerge. One of the most serious concerns is hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation, a complication that can cause severe hepatitis, liver failure and, in extreme cases, death. HBV reactivation occurs when viral replication resumes or increases in a person whose infection was previously controlled. It may affect people with persistent infection, identified by the presence of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), as well as individuals with resolved or occult infection. Patients who are HBsAg-negative but anti-hepatitis B core antibody-positive may retain HBV genetic material in liver cells even after apparently clearing the infection. Under normal immune conditions, viral replication remains suppressed. Immunosuppressive treatment can weaken this control, allowing HBV DNA levels to rise before liver injury becomes clinically apparent. The risk of reactivation is well established in some immunosuppressed populations, particularly patients receiving intensive chemotherapy or B-cell-depleting therapies such as rituximab. Less certain has been the scale of the problem among people with IBD treated with TNF-α inhibitors. This question is especially important in China, which has intermediate HBV endemicity and an estimated 70 million chronic carriers. In this setting, a substantial number of patients beginning biologic treatment may have chronic HBV infection, previous exposure or no knowledge of their infection status. A nationwide real-world study has examined HBV infection, reactivation and HBV-associated liver dysfunction among Chinese patients with IBD receiving infliximab. The investigation drew on patients from multiple centers across the country, aiming to reflect routine clinical practice rather than the highly selected conditions of a controlled clinical trial. Before infliximab treatment began, patients underwent HBV screening, and those receiving therapy were monitored through measurements of HBV DNA and liver-function indicators. This approach enabled researchers to evaluate viral behavior alongside the clinical course of intestinal disease and the immunosuppressive treatments used. The study quantified the occurrence of HBV reactivation in this treatment population and examined factors that may influence individual risk. Baseline HBV serology was a central consideration, distinguishing patients with active or chronic infection from those with evidence of previous exposure. The analysis also considered concurrent immunosuppression and the activity of IBD, both of which may alter the balance between viral replication and immune control. These factors are clinically relevant because infliximab is often used alongside corticosteroids, immunomodulators or other therapies that can intensify overall immune suppression. The investigators also characterized liver dysfunction linked to HBV in patients receiving infliximab. HBV-related injury is not caused simply by the presence of viral DNA; it commonly reflects an immune response against infected liver cells after viral replication has accelerated. As a result, alanine aminotransferase and other liver enzymes may rise during or after reactivation, sometimes progressing to jaundice, impaired hepatic function or acute liver failure. Regular laboratory surveillance can identify these changes earlier, although monitoring is most effective when it is paired with timely HBV DNA testing and a clear plan for antiviral intervention. The findings support universal HBV screening before biologic therapy is initiated in patients with IBD. Screening generally involves HBsAg, antibodies to hepatitis B surface antigen and anti-HBc, with HBV DNA testing used to clarify infection and replication status when indicated. Patients who are HBsAg-positive should be assessed for antiviral prophylaxis or treatment before immunosuppression begins. For patients with resolved infection, the appropriate strategy may depend on viral DNA status, the intensity and duration of immunosuppression, and the feasibility of close laboratory follow-up. Preventive antiviral therapy or pre-emptive treatment can reduce the likelihood that silent viral persistence will develop into clinically significant hepatitis. The study further emphasizes that HBV safety cannot be managed by gastroenterologists alone. Coordinated care involving hepatologists, infectious-disease specialists and primary clinicians can help ensure that serological results are interpreted correctly, antiviral medicines are started when necessary and monitoring continues throughout and after infliximab treatment. Patients should also be informed that reactivation may occur even when they have no symptoms initially, and should be instructed to report jaundice, dark urine, severe fatigue, nausea or abdominal discomfort. As biologic treatment expands across China and other regions where HBV remains common, integrating viral screening and surveillance into IBD care will be essential for preserving the benefits of immune-targeted therapy while limiting preventable liver complications. 寻求乙型肝炎的持久治疗方法 - 好人计划

Seeking a Lasting Cure for Hepatitis B - The Good Men Project
新闻简报This article discusses the development of a new therapeutic vaccine for chronic hepatitis B, part of the EU-funded TherVacB initiative. The vaccine aims to train the immune system to fight the virus, unlike traditional vaccines that prevent infection. Hepatitis B is a major global health issue, affecting around 254 million people and causing 1.1 million deaths annually, mostly from cirrhosis and liver cancer. In the EU and associated countries, approximately 3.6 million individuals are chronically infected, often unaware of their condition until serious liver damage occurs. Current antiviral treatments suppress the virus but do not eliminate it, and the virus can return after treatment stops. Early clinical trials in Germany, Italy, Spain, the UK, and Tanzania have shown the vaccine is well tolerated and stimulates the desired immune response, but its effectiveness in curing chronic hepatitis B is still under investigation. Researchers believe a functional cure may come from combining therapies that work together, rather than relying on a single treatment. The World Health Organization aims to eliminate viral hepatitis as a public health threat by 2030, which would require earlier diagnosis, broader vaccination, and more effective treatments for those already infected.

时间

地点与对象
欧盟国家、冰岛、列支敦士登、挪威、德国、意大利、西班牙、英国、坦桑尼亚。
事件
研究人员正在为慢性乙型肝炎开发一种新的治疗药物疫苗,旨在训练免疫系统重新控制病毒。这是欧盟资助的 TherVacB 计划的一部分,该计划已进入第六个年头,将于 2026 年结束。疫苗 面向已经慢性感染的人,与预防感染的传统 疫苗 不同。
规模、影响与风险
Hepatitis B affects approximately 254 million people globally, causing about 1.1 million deaths annually, primarily from cirrhosis and liver cancer. Around 3.6 million people in the 27 EU countries, Iceland, Liechtenstein, and Norway are chronically infected. The current antiviral medicines can suppress the virus but cannot eliminate it, and the virus often returns after treatment stops.
应对、状态与不确定性
治疗药物疫苗正在多个国家进行早期临床试验,初步结果显示其耐受性良好,并能刺激预期的免疫应答。然而,该研究仍处于早期阶段,疫苗 治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 的有效性尚未得到证实。欧盟资助的 TherVacB 计划正在进行中,目标是开发 功能性治愈 的联合疗法。
